*Johnna Sundberg,Rayid Ghani,Eli Ben-Michael,Edward Kennedy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对策略学习中治疗数据缺失的问题，将ATE的高效估计方法扩展到策略价值和CATE估计，证明了MAR估计量在MCCAR假设下比MCCAR估计量更有效，并通过实验验证了正确设定缺失机制的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有策略学习方法大多假设治疗数据完整，但实际应用中常存在数据缺失，导致估计偏差和次优策略。本文旨在填补这一空白，为缺失治疗数据下的稳健策略学习提供理论支撑和实用工具。

Method: 将ATE的高效估计方法扩展到策略价值和CATE估计，分别针对MAR和MCCAR两种缺失机制设计估计量，并进行渐近效率分析，证明MAR估计量在MCCAR假设下依然有效且更高效。

Result: 理论证明MAR估计量在MCCAR假设下既有效又比MCCAR估计量更高效；实验表明正确指定缺失机制至关重要，错误指定的估计量无论样本大小均存在偏差，而正确指定时可达到接近oracle的性能。

Conclusion: 为缺失治疗数据下的策略学习提供了理论上有根据、实证上经过验证的稳健工具，推荐在两种缺失设定下优先使用基于MAR的估计。

Abstract: Policy learning methods are increasingly used to inform treatment allocation under budget constraints. Most proposed methods assume complete treatment data, yet applications frequently suffer from missingness that can bias estimates and lead to suboptimal policies. We address this gap by extending efficient estimators for average treatment effect (ATE) estimation to policy value and conditional average treatment effect (CATE) estimation under missing at random (MAR) and missing completely conditionally at random (MCCAR) treatment data. Through asymptotic efficiency analysis, we prove that the MAR estimator, which leverages partially-observed units, is both valid and more efficient than the MCCAR estimator when MCCAR assumptions hold. This result provides formal justification for preferring MAR-based estimation in policy learning under both missing data settings. Our comprehensive experiments using synthetic and semi-synthetic datasets confirm that correctly specifying the missingness mechanism is crucial: misspecified estimators remain biased regardless of sample size, while our estimators achieve near-oracle performance when assumptions are satisfied. Our work provides practitioners with theoretically grounded, empirically validated tools for robust policy learning in the presence of missing treatment data.

</details>


### [33] [Dysco: Dynamic Subspace Boosting to Mitigate LoRA Interference in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14367)
*Haobo Zhang,Jiankun Wang,Suraj Rajendran,Weishen Pan,Lam Tsoi,Yong Chen,Fei Wang,Jiayu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dynamic Subspace Boosting (Dysco)，一种针对联邦LoRA微调中数据-参数干扰问题的插件方法。通过在每轮通信中分配客户端特定的子空间并进行多轮子空间提升，Dysco有效降低了聚合误差，在合成任务上将训练损失降低9倍，在MIMIC-IV临床文本分类任务上将所有五种FL算法提升最高4.3%，且仅增加0.9%的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦微调大模型时，异构客户端的LoRA适配器聚合存在不稳定问题，其根源是数据-参数干扰（data-parameter interference），即LoRA更新子空间与客户端激活之间的几何对齐不匹配。现有方法仅将联邦LoRA视为参数平均，忽略了子空间分配这一维度。

Method: Dysco包含三个关键组件：(1) 客户端计算激活不敏感子空间并传输基向量；(2) 服务器通过闭式解构造客户端特定合并子空间，最大化与其他客户端不敏感方向的兼容性；(3) 多轮子空间提升（subspace boosting）以应对表征漂移，在保留历史更新方向的同时适应未来表征。理论层面，将数据-参数干扰嵌入标准联邦优化界作为聚合误差项，并证明Dysco的服务器固定合并子空间可收紧该上界。

Result: 在可控合成联邦任务上，Dysco在正交子空间划分下将最终轮训练损失降低最多9倍；在MIMIC-IV临床文本分类任务上，使用Llama-3.2-1B模型，Dysco将五种FL算法均提升最高4.3%，优于现有联邦LoRA方法，且仅增加0.9%的墙钟开销。

Conclusion: 联邦LoRA聚合不仅是参数平均，更是子空间分配问题。Dysco通过动态子空间分配与提升有效缓解了数据-参数干扰，提供了理论保证和实验验证，是一种轻量高效的联邦LoRA增强方案。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning of large pre-trained models increasingly relies on Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to reduce communication and computation, but heterogeneous clients can make adapter aggregation unstable. We identify the data-parameter interference as a geometric source of this instability. This interference is controlled by the alignment between LoRA update subspaces and client activations, suggesting that federated LoRA aggregation should be viewed not only as parameter averaging but also as subspace allocation. We propose Dynamic Subspace Boosting (Dysco), a plug-in method that allocates client-specific LoRA subspaces in a federated and dynamic manner. In each round, clients compute activation-insensitive subspaces from local representations and transmit only the resulting bases; the server then constructs client-specific merged subspaces through a closed-form solution that maximizes compatibility with other clients' insensitive directions. To handle representation drift, Dysco performs multi-round subspace boosting to preserve past update directions while adapting to future representations. We provide a convergence analysis that embeds the data-parameter interference as an aggregation-error term in a standard federated optimization bound, and prove that Dysco's server-fixed merged subspaces yield a tighter upper bound on this error. Experiments on controlled synthetic federated tasks and on MIMIC-IV clinical-note classification with Llama-3.2-1B show that Dysco substantially reduces interference, reduces the final-round synthetic training loss by up to 9 times relative to baselines under the orthogonal-subspace partition the theory identifies, improves all five tested FL algorithms by up to 4.3% on MIMIC, outperforms recent federated LoRA methods, and adds only 0.9% wall-clock overhead. Our code is available at https://github.com/illidanlab/Dysco.

</details>


### [34] [A Noise-Robust Elicit-to-Optimize Framework for Distortion Riskmetrics via Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14373)
*Yang Liu,Yuhao Liu,Yunran Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个结合逆强化学习和强化学习的噪声鲁棒性框架，用于推断智能体的风险偏好并在失真风险度量下优化策略，在有限次迭代内达到指数级收敛速度，并通过扩展PPO算法实现多种风险目标的统一优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以在智能体行为存在噪声（随机次优行动）的场景下准确推断其潜在风险偏好，且缺乏一个能够统一处理多种失真风险度量目标、同时具备鲁棒性和高效性的策略优化框架。

Method: 1) 提出自适应贝叶斯IRL方法，从含噪声的观测决策中推断智能体潜在风险目标，证明了存在有限个区分性问题可识别候选类内的偏好风险度量，并给出O(exp(-cm+O(√m log m)))的收敛速率；2) 开发基于条件失真风险度量的无模型RL算法，将目标表示为条件成本分位数函数关于失真函数的积分，扩展PPO算法引入策略、价值和分位数三个神经网络，其中分位数网络估计完整条件成本分位数函数以数值评估一般风险目标。

Result: 在复杂金融环境的综合实证研究中，该框架展示了准确的偏好推断能力和有效的策略优化效果。

Conclusion: 该框架为噪声环境下智能体风险偏好的推断与风险敏感策略优化提供了理论上保证收敛速度和实证上有效的统一解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a noise-robust elicit-to-optimize framework that integrates inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) and reinforcement learning (RL) for eliciting agents' risk preferences and optimizing policies under a broad class of risk objectives characterized by distortion riskmetrics. On the elicitation side, we propose an adaptive Bayesian IRL method that infers agents' latent risk objectives from their noisy observed decisions, explicitly allowing agents to take stochastic and suboptimal actions. We establish the existence of a finite set of distinguishing questions that identifies the preferred distortion riskmetric within the candidate class and prove that the convergence rate of the algorithm is of order $O(\exp(-cm+O(\sqrt{m\log m})))$ under general settings, where $c>0$ is a constant and $m$ denotes the number of algorithm iterations. On the optimization side, we develop a model-free RL algorithm for optimizing policies under conditional distortion riskmetrics. By representing the objective as an integral of the conditional cost quantile function with respect to the distortion function, the method unifies distortion-riskmetric objectives. We optimize diverse risk objectives by extending the Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) algorithm with policy, value, and quantile neural networks, where the quantile network estimates the full conditional cost quantile function and enables numerical evaluation of general risk objectives. A comprehensive empirical study demonstrates the framework's elicitation accuracy and effectiveness in complex financial environments.

</details>

*Xiaoxuan Liang,Saeid Naderiparizi,Berend Zwartsenberg,Frank Wood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种通过在线 rollout 获取约束指导并融入训练过程的微调框架，利用固定噪声计划对去噪过程进行数值积分求导，使训练与采样对齐，从而在保持采样质量的同时提升约束满足度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束生成方法存在两个问题：训练时优化基于训练分布诱导的状态，与采样时遇到的状态差异大；采样时修正方法在推理阶段修改采样过程，引入分布偏移且需昂贵调参，尤其对少步采样不友好。

Method: 提出一个微调框架，通过在线 rollout（即模拟采样轨迹）获得约束违反信号，并将其作为指导信号融入训练过程；关键创新在于通过固定噪声计划对去噪过程的数值积分进行求导，使梯度能够从约束违反反向传播到模型参数，从而对齐训练和采样分布。

Result: 在多个任务上的实验表明，该方法相比先前方法在约束满足度上有提升，同时保持了具有竞争力的采样质量。

Conclusion: 通过在线 rollout 将约束指导融入训练过程，并利用可微的固定噪声计划对齐训练与采样，是解决约束生成中训练-采样分布偏移问题的有效方案。

Abstract: Constrained generative models aim to produce samples that satisfy complex feasibility constraints while remaining faithful to the data distribution. Existing constrained generation methods typically enforce constraints either through training-time optimization or sampling-time correction. Training-time optimization approaches optimize on states induced by the training distribution, which can differ substantially from those encountered during sampling. Sampling-time correction methods instead modify the sampling process at inference, introducing distribution shift and requiring expensive tuning, particularly for few-step sampling. We propose a fine-tuning framework that incorporates constraint guidance obtained through online rollout into the training process, which aligns training with sampling by differentiating through the fixed noise schedule used to numerically integrate the denoising process. This exposes the model to violations that arise along the denoising trajectory and aligns diffusion learning with the sampling process. Experiments across multiple tasks show that our method improves constraint satisfaction while maintaining competitive sampling quality compared to prior methods.

</details>


### [36] [LATTICE: Graph Self-Supervised Learning for Multimodal Spatial Omics Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14410)
*Jagan Mohan Reddy Dwarampudi,Veena Kochat,Suresh Satpati,Kunal Rai,Tania Banerjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LATTICE是一个基于图的自监督框架，用于整合空间多组学数据（包括Visium RNA、scMultiome RNA/ATAC、空间ATAC和空间CUT&Tag），通过掩码重建、跨模态对齐和空间平滑性目标学习spot级别的统一表征。在11个黑色素瘤样本（54,912个spots）上验证，多模态整合显著提升了聚类一致性和空间连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间组学研究中转录组和表观基因组检测日益增多，但下游分析仍常使用单模态流程，缺乏统一的多模态整合方法。

Method: LATTICE构建空间邻域图，使用TransformerConv编码器，通过掩码重建、跨模态对齐和空间平滑性三个自监督目标进行训练，将五种模态（Visium RNA、scMultiome RNA、scMultiome ATAC、空间ATAC、空间CUT&Tag）整合到统一的晶格表征中。

Result: 在11个黑色素瘤样本（54,912个spots）上，添加scMultiome RNA使ARI提升0.157、NMI提升0.143、空间连续性提升0.174；加入更多模态进一步提高空间连续性和多模态效用分数（MUS），但有时会降低与RNA派生参考标签的一致性。

Conclusion: LATTICE是一个实用且经验有效的多模态空间组学整合框架，同时表明需要更强的监督信号和更广泛的外部基准测试。

Abstract: Spatially resolved omics studies increasingly combine transcriptomic and epigenomic assays, yet downstream analysis is often still performed using single-modality pipelines. We present LATTICE (Latent Alignment of Tissue-level and Transcriptomic Information for Cross-modal Embedding), a graph-based self-supervised framework that learns spot-level representations from harmonized multimodal features. LATTICE integrates five aligned modality blocks per Visium spot: Visium RNA, scMultiome RNA, scMultiome ATAC, spatial ATAC, and spatial CUT\&Tag. These modalities capture spatial transcriptomic measurements, single-cell inferred regulatory activity, and in situ chromatin and histone states within a unified lattice representation. LATTICE constructs a spatial neighborhood graph and trains a TransformerConv encoder using masked reconstruction, cross-modal alignment, and spatial smoothness objectives. On a private 11-sample melanoma cohort from an anonymized clinical collaborator comprising 54{,}912 total spots, LATTICE demonstrated stable optimization behavior, reproducible embeddings across analysis seeds, and complete multimodal integration across all samples. Adding scMultiome RNA to Visium RNA alone substantially improved concordance with Space Ranger clusters across 11 runs (adjusted Rand index [ARI] +0.157, normalized mutual information [NMI] +0.143, and spatial contiguity +0.174). Additional modalities further improved spatial contiguity and multimodal utility score (MUS), although they sometimes reduced agreement with RNA-derived reference labels, likely because the learned embeddings captured chromatin and regulatory structure beyond transcriptomic similarity alone. These results position LATTICE as a practical and empirically grounded framework for multimodal spatial omics integration, while also highlighting the need for stronger supervision and broader external benchmarking.

</details>


### [37] [Adaptive Ad Load Design for Sponsored Search Markets: Evidence, Theory, and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14418)
*Mohammad Rashid,Hema Yoganarasimhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模随机田野实验（500万+用户）研究安卓应用商店中赞助广告位数量（1-6个）对收入和用户体验的权衡，发现增加广告位最多提升43%收入，但会降低5%转化率和2.2%日活。基于异质性结果，提出并部署了e-LAAL自适应算法，在2230万用户和7760万次搜索中改善了收入-转化权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赞助搜索中广告位数量（ad-load）是核心供给侧决策：增加广告位可能提升收入，但可能挤占自然搜索结果、损害用户体验。现有研究缺乏大规模实验证据来量化这一权衡，且忽略了不同查询间的异质性效应。

Method: 1) 在Android应用商店进行随机田野实验，5M+用户暴露于1-6个赞助广告位；2) 基于实验结果发现异质性，设计e-LAAL算法：结合无模型查询级决策规则LAAL（利用近期结果更新推荐）与静态探索臂（维持支持并提供固定策略反事实基准）；3) 提供有限时间动态遗憾保证；4) 在平台上部署22.3M用户、77.6M次搜索。

Result: 增加广告位使收入提升最多43%，但搜索转化减少最多5%，日活降低最多2.2%。高广告转化查询的额外广告位产生大额收入增益，低转化查询边际收入很小或为负。品牌广告主存在会改变权衡。e-LAAL在平台级部署中改善了收入-转化权衡，优于均匀和历史查询依赖的静态基准。

Conclusion: 广告位数量与收入、用户体验之间存在显著的异质性权衡，e-LAAL自适应算法能有效优化这一权衡，在保持收入的同时减少对用户转化的负面影响。

Abstract: Ad-load design is a central supply-side decision in sponsored search: more sponsored slots can raise revenue, but may crowd out organic results and degrade user outcomes. We study this trade-off using a large-scale randomized field experiment on an Android app store, where over five million users are exposed to one through six sponsored slots. Increasing ad load raises revenue by up to 43%, but reduces total search conversions by up to 5% and daily engagement by up to 2.2%. These average effects mask substantial heterogeneity: additional slots generate large revenue gains for high-ad-conversion queries, but little or negative marginal revenue for low-conversion queries. The trade-off also shifts within query as advertiser composition changes, such as brand-advertiser presence. Motivated by these findings, we design and deploy a novel adaptive algorithm -- exploration-augmented Locally Adaptive Ad Load (e-LAAL). e-LAAL combines LAAL, a model-free query-level decision rule that updates ad-load recommendations using recent outcomes, with static exploration arms that maintain support and provide fixed-policy counterfactual benchmarks. We provide a finite-time dynamic-regret guarantee for the e-LAAL architecture. In a platform-level production deployment serving 22.3 million users and 77.6 million searches, e-LAAL improves the empirical revenue--conversion trade-off relative to deployed static benchmarks and outperforms uniform and historical query-dependent static benchmarks.

</details>


### [38] [HyperShadow: A Benchmark for Detecting 3D Projections of Higher-Dimensional Spatial Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14419)
*Akshay Sasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HyperShadow基准测试，任务是区分真实3D点云与高维空间（R^4到R^6）中刚性物体投影的"阴影"，通过点云网络和刚性对齐残差分别达到96.6%准确率和0.982 AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"4D"数据集的第四维均为时间维度，缺乏将第四、五、六维作为空间维度的基准测试。本文旨在研究哪些可观测统计量能证明数据与纯三维解释的不兼容性。

Method: 1) 构建HyperShadow基准，区分原生3D形状与高维空间刚性物体的3D投影（阴影）；2) 提出19万参数点云网络学习投影签名、密度折叠等特征；3) 引入零参数刚性见证量：Kabsch算法计算连续帧间最优刚性3D对齐残差。

Result: 标准本征维估计器（TwoNN, Levina-Bickel MLE）仅71-73%准确率；点云网络在四种损坏等级下达96.6%准确率，对未见物体泛化79-91%；刚性见证量AUROC达0.982。

Conclusion: 投影阴影检测与本征维估计本质不同，可通过投影签名、密度折叠和刚性对齐残差等统计量实现高精度分类。所有数据可复现生成并公开。

Abstract: Machine-learning datasets labelled "4D" universally denote three spatial dimensions plus time. We introduce HyperShadow, the first public benchmark in which the fourth, fifth, and sixth dimensions are spatial: the task is to decide whether a 3D point cloud is a native three-dimensional shape or the projection, the "shadow", of a rigid object living in R^N (N = 4-6). We show this task is fundamentally distinct from intrinsic-dimension estimation: a shadow is still at-most-3-dimensional data, and standard estimators (TwoNN, Levina-Bickel MLE) reach only 71-73% accuracy. Detection instead requires projection signatures, density folds, filled volumes with characteristic radial profiles, and topology changes, which a 190k-parameter point network recovers at 96.6% accuracy across four corruption tiers, generalizing at 79-91% to object families never seen in training. On a temporal track of rigidly rotating objects we introduce a zero-parameter rigidity witness: the residual of the optimal rigid 3D alignment (Kabsch) between consecutive frames, which must vanish for any rigid 3D motion but cannot vanish for the shadow of a rigid rotation in R^N. This single interpretable statistic separates the classes at AUROC 0.982. All data are generated reproducibly from seeds; the dataset, models, and code are released publicly. HyperShadow makes no claim about physical reality; it is a controlled instrument for studying which observable statistics can certify incompatibility with a purely three-dimensional explanation.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 主动评估框架通过序贯实验设计显著提升了机器人策略评估的样本效率，能够系统性地识别失效模式，为通用策略的可靠评估提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Generalist robot manipulation policies trained on large, diverse datasets have shown remarkable promise across a wide range of tasks. However, rigorously evaluating these policies remains a fundamental challenge. Real-world performance depends on a large combinatorial space of task factors including object poses and camera viewpoints, making full, exhaustive evaluation intractable. Additionally, real hardware evaluation is slow and resource-intensive, so current practice is to use narrow test suites that can miss critical failure modes and misrepresent true deployment readiness. We propose an active evaluation framework that addresses this challenge by treating policy evaluation as a sequential experimental design problem. Our approach fits a probabilistic surrogate model over a structured space of task factors and adaptively selects evaluation configurations to maximize information gain over the policy's performance distribution, allowing for sample-efficient characterization of policy behavior across unseen conditions and a systematic identification of failure-prone regions. We conduct 2331 real-world evaluations across 3 tasks with 3 factor variations and find that our approach typically saves the evaluator at least 20-40% of trials compared to typical random testing.

</details>

Abstract: We study Dynamical System Autoencoders (DSAE) for LiDAR point-cloud classification using spatial coordinates and Product Coefficient feature augmentations. The experiments compare separately trained DSAE architectures at encoder depths $K=1,\ldots,5$ and evaluate the resulting hidden representations with Random Forest, kNN, and a majority-class Dummy baseline. The main finding is a hidden-state collapse at $K=5$. For both xyz and xyz plus Product Coefficient inputs, the hidden-state standard deviation falls to the order of $10^{-5}$, while all three classifiers attain the same macro F1 score of $0.224688$. We prove that between-class hidden scatter is bounded by total hidden scatter, which in turn is controlled by the reported hidden-state variance. Thus a nearly constant hidden representation cannot retain substantial class-separating structure. Product Coefficients neither improve pre-collapse macro F1 nor prevent the $K=5$ collapse in the present DSAE setting. These results identify large-depth representation collapse as a concrete failure mode for DSAE LiDAR classification.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一种交替式（开-关-开-关...）而非单调衰减的噪声注入调度策略，证明了该策略在优化和鲁棒性上的显著效果。理论分析表明脉冲噪声近似雅可比正则化，高斯噪声起曲率惩罚作用。交替调度通过混合噪声与干净数据阶段帮助优化器逃离局部最小值，同时引入梯度范数稳定技术防止损失剧烈波动。在CIFAR-100-C、ImageNet-C和ImageNet-R上对ResNet和ViT架构均取得显著提升，并能与其他增强方法叠加使用。通过注意力图分析揭示了不同噪声类型针对不同模型归纳偏好的机制：脉冲噪声对抗CNN的局部性偏差，高斯噪声减少ViT对大规模虚假特征的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非单调（交替式）噪声注入调度策略的效果，揭示其在优化目标和模型鲁棒性方面的潜力，并提供理论解释。

Method: 提出交替式噪声注入调度（clean-noisy-clean-noisy交替），辅以梯度范数稳定技术（基于干净梯度幅值缩放噪声更新）；进行了理论分析（脉冲噪声→雅可比正则化，高斯噪声→曲率惩罚）；通过注意力图和显著性图分析噪声类型对不同架构归纳偏好的影响。

Result: 在CIFAR-100-C、ImageNet-C和ImageNet-R数据集上，对ResNet和ViT架构均显著提升了损坏容错性和分布外鲁棒性；可与其他数据增强方法叠加获得最佳效果；基本零额外计算成本。

Conclusion: 交替式噪声注入是一种高效且零额外计算成本的训练策略，能同时提升干净数据、噪声数据和分布外数据上的测试性能，其效果源于对不同模型架构的归纳偏差的针对性惩罚。

Abstract: Noise injection is a well-known technique in stochastic optimization. We report its surprising effectiveness with an interleaved (on-off-on-off...) rather than the usual monotonic decay schedule. We present a theoretical analysis of noise injection, which confirms that corruption by impulse noise approximates a Jacobian regularization, whereas Gaussian noise acts as a curvature penalty. This regularization behavior has been invoked to explain why noise injection increases model robustness. But the interleaved nature of our proposed schedule produces superior results even for the optimization objective: mixing phases of noisy data permits the optimizer to escape local minima and increase exploration without the risk of catastrophically forgetting the important features from the clean data. To stabilize this training scheme against the rapid changes of the loss when switching between clean and noisy data, we introduce a gradient-norm stabilization technique that scales noisy updates based on clean gradient magnitudes. We compare this method with other common augmentation methods and find substantial improvements in corruption tolerance and robustness to real-world distribution shifts on CIFAR-100-C, ImageNet-C, and ImageNet-R for ResNet and ViT architectures, with the best results being achieved by stacking our method on top of other augmentations. Through saliency and attention maps we show that the effect of interleaved noise injection stems from penalizing the failure modes encouraged by the inductive bias of the models: impulse noise works against the locality bias of convolutional (ResNet) architectures, and Gaussian noise reduces the tendency of attention-based models to pick up large-scale spurious features. Interleaved noise injection is therefore an effective tool to improve the test performance on clean, noisy, and out-of-distribution data at essentially zero computational cost.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文首次为十亿参数规模的强化学习可验证奖励（RLVR）微调建立了非平凡泛化界，通过PAC-Bayes压缩界结合Gumbel-max重参数化技巧，并提出了Progressive RLVR框架（融合在线策略蒸馏、TinyLoRA和模型量化），在数学、编程、常识推理和Text-to-SQL四个领域取得了84-97%的性能保留，同时模型可压缩性提升14,796倍，泛化界超出基模9-51%，且与微调模型精度差距仅6-11%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管RLVR被广泛用于提升LLM推理能力，但其微调后模型的泛化性仍缺乏理论理解，现有工作未能为十亿参数规模的RLVR微调建立非平凡泛化界。

Method: 1) 将PAC-Bayes压缩界适配到RLVR微调场景；2) 使用Gumbel-max重参数化技巧处理token生成的内在随机性；3) 提出Progressive RLVR框架，整合RLVR与在线策略蒸馏、TinyLoRA和模型量化。

Result: 在数学、编程、常识推理和Text-to-SQL四个领域，Progressive RLVR保留了标准LoRA微调84-97%的性能，模型可压缩性提升14,796倍；泛化界超出基模9-51%，与微调模型精度差距仅6-11%。

Conclusion: 首次为十亿参数规模的RLVR微调建立了非平凡泛化界，证明了所提框架在多种推理任务上的有效性和可压缩性。

Abstract: While reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is widely used to improve the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs), the generalizability of the resulting models remains poorly understood. In this work, we establish the first non-vacuous generalization bounds for parameter-efficient RLVR fine-tuning at the billion-parameter scale. Our approach adapts PAC-Bayes compression bounds to this setting, and addresses the inherent stochasticity of token generation by applying the Gumbel-max reparameterization trick. To operationalize these bounds, we propose the Progressive RLVR framework, which integrates RLVR with on-policy distillation, TinyLoRA, and model quantization. Progressive RLVR empirically retains 84-97% performance of standard LoRA fine-tuning while producing models that are 14,796x more compressible. We show that this framework yields non-vacuous generalization bounds in four domains: mathematical problem-solving, programming, general-knowledge reasoning, and Text-to-SQL. Our bounds exceed the accuracy of the base model by 9-51% and lie within 6-11% of the accuracy of the fine-tuned models.

</details>

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了连续时间下的强化学习框架，将状态建模为连续时间马尔可夫链，推导出PPO和GRPO的连续时间变体，并应用于基于得分的离散扩散模型微调，支持中间奖励信号，在数学推理和编码任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GRPO的方法仅依赖终端奖励，无法在去噪轨迹中引入中间奖励或优势信号；且离散扩散模型的策略优化缺乏统一的理论框架和高效计算手段。

Method: 采用随机控制方法，将强化学习建模为连续时间、离散状态空间、任意动作空间的CTMC控制问题；推导连续时间策略梯度，得到CT-PPO和CT-GRPO；针对掩码扩散语言模型提出轨迹子采样技术以高效估计轨迹似然。

Result: 在低维熵正则化优化问题和dLLM的数学推理与编码任务上验证了方法的有效性，能够利用中间奖励信号进行奖励驱动优化且无需奖励信号可微。

Conclusion: 该连续时间RL框架为离散扩散模型的策略优化提供了统一视角，支持中间奖励，具有更好的探索性和计算可行性。

Abstract: We formulate reinforcement learning (RL) in continuous time with discrete state spaces and possibly arbitrary action spaces via a stochastic control approach, where the state dynamics are modeled as a controlled continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC). We consider policy optimization problems and derive the corresponding policy gradient methods, leading to continuous-time variants of proximal policy optimization (PPO) and group relative policy optimization (GRPO). As a primary application, we develop a complete continuous-time RL framework for fine-tuning score-based discrete diffusion models. The proposed framework enables reward-driven optimization without requiring differentiability on the reward signals. In contrast to the existing GRPO-based approaches that only rely on terminal rewards, our formulation allows intermediate reward or advantage signals to be incorporated throughout the denoising trajectory. Importantly, when specialized to masked diffusion models (MDMs), our framework encompasses a rich class of policy parameterizations over the vocabulary simplex with analytically tractable probability ratios, providing a unified perspective on exploration and policy optimization in MDMs. For masked diffusion large language models (dLLMs), we further propose trajectory subsampling techniques to efficiently estimate computationally prohibitive trajectory likelihoods, reducing the computational cost of computing per-position probability ratios. We showcase the effectiveness of our methods on both low-dimensional entropy-regularized optimization problems and RL post-training of dLLMs on mathematical reasoning and coding tasks.

</details>

*Xiangdong Zhang,Xiaohan Qin,Sunan Zou,Tuo Dai,Xiaoming Shi,Huaijin Wu,Yebin Yang,Zhuo Xia,Shaofeng Zhang,Lin Yao,Yuliang Liu,Yu Cheng,Junchi Yan*

TL;DR: 本文提出xHC（扩展超连接）方法，通过时间特征增强和稀疏残差流架构（N=16中仅更新k=4个流），解决了超连接家族方法在N>4时性能增益递减和训练成本激增的问题。在18B和28B MoE模型上，xHC相比mHC平均提升4.0个下游分数，且计算效率显著优于vanilla和mHC基线。同时引入xHC-Flash降低内存流量，使大规模N的残差流扩展在大语言模型预训练中变得有效且实用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超连接家族方法（如mHC）在扩展残差流数量N>4时面临两个瓶颈：1）流数量增加导致写回信息不足；2）残差混合生成的计算成本随N立方增长。因此需要一种能在N>4时仍保持有效扩展的新方法。

Method: 1) 时间特征增强（temporal feature augmentation）来丰富写回信息；2) 稀疏残差流架构，仅更新N=16个流中的k=4个流，同时保留对完整残差状态的密集访问；3) xHC-Flash通过优化内存流量，将每子层内存流量从73.5C降至40C。

Result: 在18B MoE模型上，xHC相比mHC平均下游分数提升4.0点，且训练FLOPs仅略高于vanilla基线。缩放定律实验表明，vanilla和mHC需要分别花费xHC的1.50倍和1.19倍计算量才能达到相同损失。xHC-Flash将内存流量降至与mHC(N=4)的34C相当的水平（40C）。

Conclusion: xHC和xHC-Flash使得大规模N（>4）的残差流扩展在大语言模型预训练中既有效又实用，为大模型提供了新的可扩展维度。

Abstract: Hyper-Connections (HC) expand the residual stream of Transformers into $N$ parallel streams, providing a form of memory scaling beyond model width and depth. Manifold-Constrained HC (mHC) stabilizes this formulation at scale. The large gains from $N{=}1$ to $N{=}4$ suggest residual-stream expansion as a promising scaling axis. However, existing HC-family methods typically stop at $N{=}4$. Our experiments reveal why: scaling mHC beyond this point yields diminishing performance gains and rapidly increasing training cost. We attribute this limitation to two bottlenecks: insufficient write-back information for an expanding number of streams and residual-mixing generation whose cost scales cubically with $N$. To address both bottlenecks, we propose xHC (Expanded Hyper-Connections), the first HC-family method to achieve meaningful expansion beyond $N{=}4$. xHC combines temporal feature augmentation for richer write-back with a sparse residual-stream architecture that updates only $k=4$ of the $N=16$ streams while retaining dense access to the full residual state. Across 18B and 28B MoE models, xHC delivers strong and consistent downstream improvements. On an 18B MoE model, xHC improves the average downstream score by 4.0 points over mHC, while adding only modest training FLOPs over the vanilla baseline. Scaling-law experiments show that the vanilla and mHC require $1.50\times$ and $1.19\times$ the compute of xHC, respectively, to reach the same loss. Practical large-$N$ training also requires controlling memory traffic from the expanded residual state. We therefore introduce xHC-Flash, which reduces the per-sublayer memory traffic from $73.5C$ to $40C$, comparable to the $34C$ required by mHC at $N{=}4$, while retaining the gains of full xHC. Together, xHC and xHC-Flash make large-$N$ residual-stream expansion effective and practical for LLM pre-training.

</details>

*Da Chang,Qiankun Shi,Lvgang Zhang,Di He,Yaoshuai Ma,Ganzhao Yuan,Yongxiang Liu*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon风格优化器对矩阵动量施加极映射，但其更新依赖于正交化前每个参数块的表示方式。这种表示选择作为一种优化器几何形式尚未被系统性研究，理解其影响有助于设计更高效的优化器。

Method: 提出\method系列优化器，在原生、最近平方、瘦形和向量表示上共享相同的动量规则和Newton-Schulz后端。每种Frobenius等距表示产生不同的极陡下降几何，通过理论分析结合教师-学生模型实验研究表示对优化行为的影响。

Result: 在LLaMA2-130M和LLaMA2-600M上的预训练实验表明，平衡的非原生表示可匹配原生表示性能；缩减短维度会弱化缩放和奇异通道支持，使其行为趋近归一化动量；教师-学生模型中曲率崩溃和Marchenko-Pastur谱分布与早期耗散相关。

Conclusion: 表示几何是Muon风格优化器的一个重要设计维度，非原生表示在合适的平衡条件下能达到与原生表示相当的性能，而短维度缩减会导致优化行为退化。

Abstract: Muon-style optimizers apply a polar map to matrix momentum, but their updates also depend on the representation of each parameter block before orthogonalization. We study this representation choice as a form of optimizer geometry and introduce {\method}, a family of Muon-style optimizers that shares the same momentum rule and Newton--Schulz backend across native, nearest-square, skinny, and vector representations. Each Frobenius-isometric representation induces a distinct polar steepest-descent geometry, in which the shorter matrix dimension determines the number of supported singular channels, the pullback scaling, and the constants in stochastic nonconvex convergence bounds. In a teacher--student model, curvature collapse and an isotropic Marchenko--Pastur spectral profile connect early-stage dissipation to the represented nuclear-to-squared-Frobenius norm ratio. Pretraining experiments on LLaMA2-130M and LLaMA2-600M, together with fixed-momentum diagnostics, show that balanced non-native representations can match the performance of the native representation, whereas reducing the shorter dimension weakens the scaling and singular-channel support, leading to behavior that increasingly resembles normalized momentum.

</details>


### [46] [CASP: Learning-Augmented Offline Approximation with Verifiable Certificates and Bounded-Loss PAC Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14545)
*Haifeng Li,Mo Hai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CASP框架，利用可验证的预测来加速NP-hard优化问题，通过多项式时间验证器确保正确性不依赖预测质量，并建立了相应的学习理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的机器学习辅助NP-hard优化方法依赖预测质量，一旦预测错误会丧失最坏情况保证；本文旨在设计一种即使预测有误也能保持正确性的框架。

Method: 提出CASP（Certificate-Augmented Solution Pruning）方法：仅接受经过声音多项式时间验证器检查的预测，将预测限制在搜索空间的哪些部分可忽略；开发该设计的学习理论，分析损失类的可学习性。

Result: 可验证的承诺类在$\tilde O(\varepsilon^{-2}\log K)$样本下可学习，而不可验证的承诺类无分布自由率；过滤噪声预测优于标准min-combiner；在五个问题上的实验表明，分布偏移下未验证剪枝损失高达26%最优解，而验证部署零损失。

Conclusion: CASP通过可验证的预测机制，既保留了预测加速的优点，又杜绝了对预测质量的依赖，在理论学习和实际部署中均优于传统方法。

Abstract: Machine-learned predictions can speed up offline NP-hard optimization, but asking a predictor what to do amounts to asking it to solve the problem, and committing an unchecked prediction forfeits every worst-case guarantee. CASP (Certificate-Augmented Solution Pruning) instead asks which parts of the search space may be ignored, and accepts each answer only after a sound polynomial-time verifier has checked it, so correctness never depends on prediction quality. We develop the learning theory of this design. The verifier makes the induced loss class uniformly bounded, so certificate parameters are learnable from $\tilde O(\varepsilon^{-2}\log K)$ samples ($K$ the maximum instance size), whereas the unverified commitment class admits no distribution-free rate and, under cost spread $R$, none below $Ω(R/\varepsilon^2)$. Filtering noisy predictions by verifiable confidence dominates the standard min-combiner, with a margin we compute in closed form, and the prediction stays useful even given the LP, because it breaks ties on degenerate optimal faces, where every symmetric LP policy, meaning one whose commitments depend on the instance only through the verifiable confidence values, provably stalls. Experiments on five problems test the theory's quantitative predictions. With trained predictors, unverified pruning loses up to $26%$ of the optimum under distribution shift, while the verified deployment of the same predictions loses nothing.

</details>

*Dante Lok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出门零增长算子，通过零初始化门控添加残差块实现函数保持的持续学习，在300M→857M Transformer实验中实现近零遗忘（ΔA<0.1），而非FP对照组遗忘大一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习中新增容量时如何避免灾难性遗忘，需要一种函数保持的算子来安全激活新参数。

Method: 门零增长：通过零初始化门添加残差块，在横截性条件下实现功能Jacobian的秩分离，新门方向是唯一的一阶函数变化来源。

Conclusion: 门零增长是持续学习中安全容量激活的规范实例，其几何分析可覆盖LoRA、ReZero、零初始适配器等结构。

Abstract: We introduce \emph{gate-zero growth}, a function-preserving (FP) operator for continual learning that adds new residual blocks through a zero-initialised gate. Under a transversality condition, gate-zero growth induces \emph{rank separation} in the functional Jacobian: old directions are unchanged, new-weight directions are exactly flat at the growth point, and new gate directions are the only first-order source of new functional variation. As gates open during continual learning, function drift is $O(\|\boldsymbolα\|^2)$ and Jacobian leakage $O(\|\boldsymbolα\|_\infty)$, giving a controlled departure from the FP locus. On a $300\mathrm{M}\to857\mathrm{M}$ Transformer adapted from WikiText-103 to BookCorpus, gate-zero growth reaches near-zero old-domain forgetting ($Δ_A < 0.1$) under both exact-preservation (Isolation) and joint-frontier (Freeze-Nothing) operating points, while a non-FP control ($G_{\text{stack}}$) suffers an order-of-magnitude larger forgetting under the same recipe. The same geometric analysis covers LoRA, ReZero, and zero-init adapter constructions, establishing gate-zero growth as the canonical instance of a shared local geometry that governs safe capacity activation in CL.

</details>


### [48] [Accelerating A/B-Tests with Counterfactual Estimation: Reducing Variance through Policy Overlap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14604)
*Olivier Jeunen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现标准A/B测试中，当实验组和对照组策略选择相同动作时，结果仅贡献噪声而无信号，造成统计效率低下。作者提出利用策略重叠（policy overlap）来加速实验的新协议，通过将随机化处理分配机制视为元策略，并利用Δ-离策略评估方法获得无偏的平均处理效应估计。理论证明该方法在一般情况下可还原标准A/B测试，但其方差随策略间差异而非原始结果方差缩放，因而在策略有共同支持时显著优于标准差异均值估计器。实验结果验证了理论，有望显著改善推荐系统、信息检索和大语言模型界面的评估效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在线对照实验（A/B测试）存在两大问题：一是运行成本高昂，二是方差问题损害了评估处理效应的统计功效。现有方差缩减技术虽利用基于模型的控制变量来降低结果噪声，但忽略了竞争策略之间潜在的结构关系，导致在策略重叠区域贡献了大量无信号纯噪声的数据，造成不必要的置信区间膨胀。

Method: 提出一种利用策略重叠（policy overlap）加速实验的新协议。核心思想是将随机化处理分配机制视为一个元策略（meta-policy），并引入Δ-离策略评估（Δ-Off-Policy Estimation）方法，以获得平均处理效应的无偏估计。理论分析证明该方法在一般情况下能还原标准A/B测试，但其方差随策略间差异（divergence between policies）而非原始结果方差缩放。

Result: 理论证明：所提方法在一般情况下可恢复标准A/B测试实践，方差随策略间差异缩放而非原始结果方差。当策略有共同支持（common support）时，该方法严格优于标准差异均值估计器；当重叠区域贡献非零残差方差时，改进是严格的。实证结果与理论一致，表明该方法在推荐系统、信息检索和大语言模型界面评估中具有显著的实际影响潜力。

Conclusion: 本文识别出标准A/B测试协议中一个关键的效率低效问题——当处理组和对照组策略在动作上达成一致时，结果贡献噪声却不贡献处理效应信号。通过将策略重叠转化为信息优势，提出的新协议在理论和方法上均优于传统差异均值估计器，为在线实验的加速提供了新的范式，尤其适用于评估推荐系统、信息检索管道和大语言模型界面等场景。

Abstract: Online controlled experiments are the gold standard for hypothesis testing in online platforms. Notwithstanding their ubiquity, they are notoriously expensive to run, and issues of variance hamper statistical power in assessing treatment effects. While standard variance reduction techniques leverage model-based control variates to reduce outcome noise, they remain agnostic to potential structural relationships between competing policies.
  In this work, we identify a critical inefficiency in the standard A/B-testing protocol: when a treatment and control policy agree on an action, the resulting outcome contributes noise but no signal regarding the treatment effect -- unnecessarily inflating confidence intervals. We propose a novel experimental protocol that exploits this policy overlap to accelerate experimentation. The key insight is to frame the randomised treatment assignment mechanism as a meta-policy, and leverage $Δ$-Off-Policy Estimation methods to obtain unbiased estimates for average treatment effects. We prove analytically that our approach recovers standard A/B-testing practices in the general case, but that its variance scales with the divergence between policies rather than raw outcome variance. Hence, we dominate the standard Difference-in-Means estimator whenever policies have common support, and the improvement is strict whenever the overlap region contributes non-zero residual variance. Empirical results corroborate these theoretical insights -- holding promise for significant impact on the real-world evaluation of recommender systems, information retrieval pipelines, and large language model interfaces.

</details>


### [49] [Auditing Fairness-Privacy Trade-offs: Subpopulation-Level Effects of Fairness-Enhancing Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14607)
*Umid Suleymanov,Ilhama Novruzova,Khalid Mammadov,Natavan Hasanova,Murat Kantarcioglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了公平性增强算法如何影响子群体层面的成员推理隐私风险，揭示了公平性干预并非均匀增加隐私风险，其影响取决于模型架构、子群体规模和缓解策略，并提出了首个支持子群体层面联合审计的统一实证框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多关注隐私保护技术如何影响公平性，但反向问题——公平性增强算法如何影响隐私泄露——尚未得到充分探索。在医疗、执法和金融等敏感领域，模型必须同时满足效用、公平性和隐私保障，理解三者之间的相互作用至关重要。

Method: 采用似然比攻击（LiRA）进行子群体审计以发现隐私差异；分析差分隐私（DP）与不同类别公平性增强方法的交互作用；在子群体层面联合评估公平性、隐私和效用，并建立统一的实证审计框架。

Result: 公平性干预并不均匀增加隐私风险，其影响取决于模型架构、子群体规模和缓解策略；差分隐私的隐私收益和效用成本在子群体间分布不均；聚合评估会掩盖子群体层面的隐私差异。

Conclusion: 公平性、隐私和效用必须在子群体层面联合评估，本文提出的统一实证框架为实际审计提供了可行工具。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) models deployed in sensitive domains such as healthcare, law enforcement, and finance must satisfy not only utility requirements but also fairness and privacy guarantees. While prior work has largely examined how privacy-preserving techniques affect fairness, the inverse question-how fairness-enhancing algorithms influence privacy leakage-remains underexplored. We present the first comprehensive study of how fairness interventions affect membership inference privacy risks at the subpopulation level. By adapting the Likelihood Ratio Attack (LiRA) for subgroup auditing, we uncover privacy disparities that aggregate evaluations obscure. We further analyze how Differential Privacy (DP) interacts with fairness-enhancing methods across different categories, showing that DP's privacy benefits and utility costs are unevenly distributed across subpopulations. Our results demonstrate that fairness interventions do not uniformly increase privacy risk; their impact depends on model architecture, subgroup size, and mitigation strategy. These findings reveal that fairness, privacy, and utility must be jointly evaluated at the subpopulation level, and we introduce the first unified empirical framework to support such auditing in practice.

</details>


### [50] [Angular Gaussian Supervised Contrastive Learning for Long-Tailed Electrocardiogram Arrhythmia Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14613)
*Jin Dai,Qiuzhen Zhang,Chenyun Dai,Danmei Lan,Can Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AG-SCL（角高斯监督对比学习）框架，用于长尾多标签心电图心律失常诊断。该框架整合了角高斯对比分支（建模全协方差类不确定性）、自适应logit调整（学习标签状态特定的先验修正）和尾部感知增强（保护QRS主导频带），在PTB-XL和Noc-ECG两个数据集上取得了最优的宏平均性能，显著提升了对罕见心律失常的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾标签分布降低了深度学习在ECG心律失常诊断中的可靠性，尤其是临床重要但罕见的异常类别。现有重平衡和logit调整方法主要关注类别频率，忽视了跨ECG类别方向依赖的形态变异性。

Method: AG-SCL包含三个组件：1) 角高斯对比分支，在单位归一化嵌入上建模全协方差类别不确定性；2) 自适应Logit调整，学习有界标签状态特定先验修正；3) 尾部感知增强，生成保留形态的视图并保护7-25 Hz QRS主导频带。

Result: 在PTB-XL上：平衡准确率0.838，敏感性0.709，特异性0.968，平均精确率0.495，5% FPR下TPR 0.778。在Noc-ECG上：0.918，0.889，0.947，0.488，0.900。最大增益出现在罕见或形态不稳定的节律类别。

Conclusion: AG-SCL通过结合先验校准与各向异性表示学习，提升了长尾ECG诊断性能，增强了对罕见心律失常的敏感性，同时保持了临床相关的特异性。

Abstract: Long-tailed label distributions reduce the reliability of deep learning for electrocardiogram (ECG) arrhythmia diagnosis, particularly for clinically important but rare abnormalities. Existing rebalancing and logit adjustment methods mainly address class frequency while overlooking direction-dependent morphological variability across ECG classes. This study proposes Angular Gaussian Supervised Contrastive Learning (AG-SCL) for long-tailed multi-label ECG diagnosis. AG-SCL integrates three components into a unified framework: an Angular Gaussian contrastive branch that models full-covariance class uncertainty on unit-normalized embeddings, Adaptive Logit Adjustment that learns bounded label-state-specific prior corrections instead of fixed frequency-based margins, and tail-aware augmentation that generates morphology-preserving views while protecting the 7-25 Hz QRS-dominant band. The method was evaluated on the public PTB-XL benchmark and a nocturnal ECG dataset comprising 1317 hours of recordings from 141 subjects. AG-SCL achieved the best macro-level performance on both datasets. On PTB-XL, it obtained a balanced accuracy of 0.838, sensitivity of 0.709, specificity of 0.968, mean average precision of 0.495, and TPR at 5% FPR of 0.778. On Noc-ECG, the corresponding values were 0.918, 0.889, 0.947, 0.488, and 0.900. The largest gains occurred in rare or morphologically unstable rhythm classes, while ablation studies confirmed the contributions of full-covariance modelling, Adaptive Logit Adjustment, and tail-aware augmentation. AG-SCL improves long-tailed ECG diagnosis by combining prior calibration with anisotropic representation learning, enhancing sensitivity to rare arrhythmias while maintaining clinically relevant specificity. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Open-EXG/AG-SCL-for-Long-Tailed-ECG.

</details>


### [51] [Beyond Entropy: Correctness-Aware Advantage Shaping via Contrastive Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14614)
*Weiwen Xu,Jia Liu,Hou Pong Chan,Long Li,Deng Cai,Min Chen,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对比策略优化（CPO），利用参考引导与普通生成分布之间的对比分歧进行正确性感知的优势塑造，在多个基准上显著优于基于熵的RLVR方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于熵的RLVR方法无法区分有益的不确定性和有害的混淆，限制了其作为正确性信号的有效性。

Method: CPO使用token级别的对比分歧（参考引导分布与普通生成分布之间的差异）进行正确性感知的优势塑造，并证明On-policy Distillation是其特例。

Result: 在领域内和领域外基准上，CPO显著优于基于熵的RLVR方法，同时保持强泛化能力。正确和错误响应分别自然支持探索和利用，平衡两者可获得最佳性能。

Conclusion: 对比分歧能可靠指示token级别的正确性，CPO解决了零优势问题，是一种更有效的RLVR优势塑造方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) commonly uses entropy for advantage shaping. However, entropy cannot distinguish useful uncertainty from detrimental confusion, limiting its effectiveness as a correctness signal. We propose Contrastive Policy Optimization (CPO), which uses token-level contrastive disagreement between reference-guided and vanilla generation distributions for correctness-aware advantage shaping. Both theoretical and empirical results show that this disagreement reliably indicates token-level correctness. We further show that On-policy Distillation is a special case of CPO, where the posterior distribution is instantiated by an external teacher model. CPO also resolves the zero-advantage problem. Experiments on in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks demonstrate that CPO substantially outperforms entropy-based RLVR methods while maintaining strong generalization. Further analysis shows that correct and incorrect responses naturally support exploration and exploitation respectively, and balancing both leads to the best performance.

</details>


### [52] [PolyQ: Codesigning End-to-End Quantization Framework for Scalable Edge CPU LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14618)
*Hyunwoo Oh,Suyeon Jang,Hanning Chen,KyungIn Nam,Sanggeon Yun,Ryozo Masukawa,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PolyQ是一种面向CPU的编译器/量化协同设计方法，通过逐通道位宽分配（{2,3,4,8,16}）和编译时通道重组，实现了用户指定平均比特预算下的分数比特部署，在多个模型和CPU平台上显著降低了激活重排开销，提升了推理性能与能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPU是设备端LLM推理最通用的目标，但现有低比特量化方法要么提供粗粒度的操作点，要么提供细粒度混合精度但难以在CPU上高效执行。需要一种既能精细适配比特预算又能在CPU上高效运行的方法。

Method: PolyQ采用激活感知的逐通道位宽分配（从{2,3,4,8,16}中选取），通过编译时模型编译器对通道进行排列和聚类形成比特同质块，生成SIMD和LUT兼容的内核，并跨算子合并兼容的排列以将布局正则化移出运行时路径。

Result: 在Falcon-H1-3B、Llama2-13B和Qwen3-32B上，PolyQ在3-6比特范围内提供稳定的质量缩放，在3比特目标下相比先前方法困惑度改善2.4-32.1%。在三种CPU上，编译器布局正则化减少激活重排流量高达70.8%，预填延迟和解码吞吐量与配置的比特预算几乎成比例缩放，能量/令牌开销相比优化的LUT后端低于2%。

Conclusion: 分数比特CPU部署是实际可行的、可预测的且能效高的，适用于多样化的边缘目标。

Abstract: CPUs are the most universal target for on-device LLM inference, but existing low-bit quantization methods offer either coarse operating points or fine-grained mixed precision that is difficult to execute efficiently on CPUs. We present PolyQ, a CPU-oriented compiler/quantization co-design for activation-aware channel-wise bit allocation under a user-specified average-bit budget. PolyQ assigns per-channel bit-widths from $\{2,3,4,8,16\}$, then uses a compile-time model compiler to permute and cluster channels into bit-homogeneous blocks, generate SIMD- and LUT-compatible kernels, and merge compatible permutations across operators to keep layout regularization off the runtime path. This turns fine-grained budget fitting into a practical fractional-bit deployment method for CPU-only inference. Across Falcon-H1-3B, Llama2-13B, and Qwen3-32B on WikiText-2, PolyQ provides stable quality scaling from 3--6\,b and improves perplexity by 2.4--32.1\% over prior methods at a 3\,b target. End-to-end measurements on three representative CPUs -- workstation, laptop, and mobile -- show that compiler layout regularization reduces activation reorder traffic by up to 70.8\%, prefill latency and decode throughput scale nearly proportionally with the configured bit budget, and energy/token overhead stays below 2\% relative to an optimized LUT-based back-end. These results show that fractional-bit CPU deployment is practical, predictable, and energy-efficient across diverse edge targets.

</details>


### [53] [TIDE: Trustworthy and Interpretable Battery Degradation Estimation with Contextual Learning and Symbolic Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14640)
*Wen Yang Tan,Jiawei Li,Fang Liu,Wei Zhang,Sumei Sun,Peng Cheng Wang,Elisa Y. M. Ang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TIDE，一种可信且可解释的电池退化估计器，通过结合电池领域知识与运行测量，在保证估计精度的同时兼顾可信度与可解释性，平均估计精度优于基线方法19.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电池健康估计对电池管理系统至关重要，尤其智能互联系统中估计误差会跨设备传播并影响下游决策，现有方法在准确性、可信度和可解释性方面难以兼顾。

Method: TIDE采用三组件骨干结构：知识引导的退化先验（提升可信度）、单调残差组件（提供可解释的老化一致性修正）、上下文学习组件（捕捉电池特定运行效应提高精度）；随后通过符号蒸馏将训练好的骨干压缩为紧凑的符号代理模型，提供模型级解释。

Result: TIDE在估计精度上平均优于基线19.7%；知识引导先验和单调残差建模显著减少老化一致性违规；骨干支持组件级解释，符号蒸馏提供紧凑的模型级表示。

Conclusion: TIDE在准确性、可信度和可解释性三方面均表现优异，适用于智能互联系统中的电池健康监测与决策支持。

Abstract: Battery health estimation is fundamental for battery management in battery-powered systems, where inaccurate health states may affect control, maintenance, and service life. It becomes even more critical in intelligent connected systems, where estimation errors can propagate across interconnected devices and downstream decisions. In this paper, we propose TIDE, a trustworthy and interpretable battery degradation estimator for reliable battery health estimation. TIDE jointly considers accuracy, trustworthiness, and interpretability, which are all essential for practical deployment and downstream decision making. To realize these objectives, TIDE combines battery-domain knowledge with operational measurements in a three-component backbone. A knowledge-guided degradation prior promotes trustworthy estimation, a monotone residual component provides interpretable aging-consistent refinement, and a contextual learning component captures battery-specific operational effects for improved accuracy. The trained backbone is then distilled into a compact symbolic surrogate that provides a concise model-level interpretation of its learned estimation logic. Experiments show that TIDE achieves strong estimation accuracy, improving overall estimation fidelity by an average of 19.7% over representative baselines. Its knowledge-guided prior and monotone residual modelling substantially reduce aging-consistency violations, supporting trustworthy estimation. Meanwhile, the backbone enables component-level interpretation, while symbolic distillation provides a compact model-level representation of the learned estimation logic. These results support the practical use of TIDE for battery health monitoring and decision support in intelligent connected systems.

</details>


### [54] [Trajectory-Aware Flow Matching for Topology Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14652)
*Shusheng Xiao,Jinshuai Bai,Hyogu Jeong,Yunfei Xi,Yilin Gui,YuanTong Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于流匹配的拓扑优化（FMTO）框架，通过引入轨迹感知的FMTO公式，利用体积分数索引的BESO状态构建概率路径和目标速度场，实现了高效、多样化的拓扑生成，且采样步数远少于扩散基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拓扑优化需要重复的有限元分析和基于灵敏度的材料更新，在多变条件下生成多个候选设计时计算成本高昂。现有生成式TO方法依赖对抗训练、长反向扩散采样或外部引导，缺乏高效且物理一致的生成方案。

Method: 首先提出线性FMTO作为基于端点的基线，在高斯源场与BESO参考拓扑之间插值；进一步提出轨迹感知FMTO，利用体积分数索引的BESO状态构建概率路径和目标速度场，将物理引导的优化历史融入生成流学习。通过路径-速度失配分析解释了轨迹加权的作用机制。

Result: FMTO生成多样化的拓扑候选，在合规性能、体积分数满足度、拓扑保真度方面表现优异，采样步数远少于扩散基线。在有限训练数据下，轨迹感知FMTO以适中的轨迹权重取得了最佳综合性能。研究还验证了轨迹锚点密度和三维拓扑生成的适用性。

Conclusion: 提出的FMTO框架能够高效生成多样化、物理一致的拓扑结构，轨迹感知公式通过引入物理引导的中间状态改善了生成稳定性，且无需推理时优化，适用于二维和三维问题。

Abstract: Topology optimisation (TO) often requires repeated finite element analysis and sensitivity-based material updates, which can be costly when multiple candidate designs are needed under varying physical and design conditions. Generative TO offers a route to rapid design exploration, but existing models may rely on adversarial training, long reverse-diffusion sampling, or external guidance to maintain structural feasibility and physical consistency. This study develops a flow matching-based topology optimisation (FMTO) framework for conditional topology generation. Linear FMTO is first formulated as an endpoint-based baseline by interpolating between a Gaussian source field and the BESO reference topology. To introduce mechanically meaningful intermediate states, a trajectory-aware FMTO formulation is proposed, where volume-fraction-indexed BESO states are used to construct the probability path and target velocity field. This incorporates physics-guided optimisation history into generative flow learning without adding inference-time optimisation. A path--velocity mismatch analysis explains why moderate trajectory weighting can improve generation stability, whereas excessive guidance may over-constrain the learned transport. Numerical examples show that FMTO generates diverse topology candidates with improved compliance-related performance, volume-fraction satisfaction, topology fidelity, and substantially fewer sampling steps than a diffusion-based baseline. Under limited training data, trajectory-aware FMTO achieves the best overall performance with a moderate trajectory weight. Studies on trajectory-anchor density and three-dimensional topology generation further demonstrate the influence of path design and the applicability of the proposed framework beyond two-dimensional problems.

</details>


### [55] [Scalable Training of Continuous-Time Spiking Neural Networks with Differentiable Spike-Time Discretization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14672)
*Yusuke Sakemi,Tomoya Takeuchi,Takeo Hosomi,Kazuyuki Aihara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于可微分脉冲时间离散化（DSTD）的连续时间SNN训练框架，通过将不规则的突触前脉冲映射到固定时间点上的可微分加权事件，将候选相关激活内存从O(N_out N_in)降至O(N_out M)，并引入链式前馈时间正则化防止神经元死亡。在密集LIF层上，峰值内存消耗降低约100倍，训练时间降低约20倍，成功在单GPU上训练了9层（CIFAR-10）和20层（Fashion-MNIST）卷积SNN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间SNN提供事件驱动框架，但训练深度连续时间SNN受限于精确脉冲时间计算所需的大量内存，该内存依赖于突触前脉冲排序所确定的候选激发时间间隔。

Method: 提出可微分脉冲时间离散化（DSTD），将不规则突触前脉冲映射到固定时间点上的可微分加权事件，用M个固定时间间隔替代输入依赖的候选维度；引入链式前馈时间正则化组织层间激发窗口并防止神经元死亡。

Result: 在TTFS编码下，候选相关激活内存从O(N_out N_in)降至O(N_out M)；密集LIF层峰值内存消耗降低约100倍，训练时间降低约20倍；成功训练9层（CIFAR-10）和20层（Fashion-MNIST）卷积SNN。

Conclusion: DSTD框架显著降低了连续时间SNN的内存和训练时间开销，使得在单GPU上训练深度卷积SNN成为可能。

Abstract: Continuous-time spiking neural networks (SNNs) provide an event-driven framework for temporal computation, computational neuroscience, and neuromorphic hardware. However, training deep continuous-time SNNs is severely constrained by the memory required for exact spike-time computation, which evaluates and retains candidate firing times over intervals determined by presynaptic spike ordering. Here we introduce a memory-efficient training framework based on differentiable spike-time discretization (DSTD) for leaky integrate-and-fire neurons with general membrane and synaptic time constants. DSTD maps irregular presynaptic spikes onto differentiable weighted events at fixed time points, replacing the input-dependent candidate dimension with $M$ fixed time intervals while accurately approximating continuous-time membrane-potential dynamics. This reduces candidate-related activation memory from $O(N_{\mathrm{out}}N_{\mathrm{in}})$ to $O(N_{\mathrm{out}}M)$ in the case of time-to-first-spike (TTFS) coding, where $N_{\mathrm{in}}$ and $N_{\mathrm{out}}$ denote the numbers of presynaptic and postsynaptic neurons, respectively. We further introduce synfire-chain-inspired temporal regularization that organizes layer-wise firing windows, mitigates dead-neuron failures, and enables pipeline-like processing. In dense LIF layers, DSTD reduced peak memory consumption by up to approximately 100-fold and training time by up to approximately 20-fold compared with exact spike-time computation. Together, these methods allowed us to train 9-layer convolutional SNNs on CIFAR-10 and 20-layer convolutional SNNs on Fashion-MNIST on a single GPU.

</details>


### [56] [Grad2Fair: A Gradient-driven Approach for Graph Fairness without Demographics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14705)
*Yuchang Zhu,Zezhong Xie,Huizhe Zhang,Huazhen Zhong,Jintang Li,Liang Chen,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Grad2Fair，一种无需人口统计信息的图神经网络公平性方法，通过利用节点梯度分布隐式编码的人口统计信息来量化并缓解偏差，避免了不准确的人口统计预测问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图神经网络公平性方法大多假设人口统计信息完全可用，而少数使用预测人口统计信息的方法因预测不准确导致公平性提升失败。因此需要一种不依赖人口统计信息的图公平性方法。

Method: 提出GradDist，一种基于梯度的度量指标，通过测量误分类节点梯度分布中局部模式之间的距离来量化偏差；进而提出Grad2Fair，一种梯度引导的无需人口统计信息的群体公平性方法，直接利用梯度进行去偏。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上的实验表明，Grad2Fair在大多数情况下优于基线方法，有效提升了图神经网络的公平性。

Conclusion: 梯度分布隐式编码了人口统计信息，直接利用梯度进行去偏可以避免人口统计预测的不准确性，从而实现稳定的公平性能提升。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) frequently encounter group fairness issues, often yielding biased predictions against specific demographic groups defined by sensitive attributes such as gender or race. While this challenge has motivated extensive research, most existing solutions rely on the strong assumption that demographics are fully available. To bypass this strict requirement, a few recent studies have attempted to use predicted demographics as proxies to enforce fairness constraints. However, predicted demographics may be inaccurate, resulting in the failure to improve fairness. In this work, we investigate the problem of graph fairness without demographic information and avoid the utilization of predicted demographics. Motivated by our observation that the gradient distributions of misclassified nodes implicitly encode demographic information, we first propose GradDist, a gradient-based metric that quantifies bias by measuring the distance between local modes within these distributions. To mitigate this bias, we propose Gradient-to-Fairness (Grad2Fair), a gradient-guided approach for group fairness without demographics. Due to the potential demographics in gradients, Grad2Fair directly leverages gradients to debias and eliminates demographic prediction, thereby enabling stable fairness performance. Experiments on several real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of Grad2Fair, as evidenced by superior performance over baselines in most cases. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZzoomD/Grad2Fair.

</details>


### [57] [MESHA: Mechanism-Enforced Sequential Halving for Strategic Linear Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14706)
*Xin Li,Zixin Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MESHA算法，用于解决战略线性老虎机中的最佳臂识别问题。该算法采用均匀采样和逐轮格里姆触发条件（GTC），在固定预算下有效应对臂的战略性误报行为，并证明了其在纳什均衡下的失败概率上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在战略线性老虎机设定中，每个臂可能通过战略性误报其特征向量来最大化自己被识别为最佳臂的概率，而奖励基于臂的真实但不可观测的特征生成。现有最优设计（OD）类算法在此环境下会因战略性报告特征而导致最优臂被"饿死"（无采样预算），因此需要设计能抵御战略行为的BAI算法。

Method: MESHA算法包含两个核心组件：(1) 朴素均匀采样规则——降低臂战略性行为的影响；(2) 逐轮格里姆触发条件（GTC）——剔除报告特征严重偏离真实值的臂。算法在固定预算T内运行，并基于任意纳什均衡进行分析。

Result: 理论方面：证明了任何臂为最大化被识别概率都会试图通过GTC检查，并推导了MESHA在固定预算T内的失败概率上界；同时证明了基于G-最优设计的线性BAI算法在战略环境下会因OD采样规则导致最优臂被饿死。实验方面：MESHA在数值实验中优于依赖OD采样规则的基线算法和特征无关的基线算法。

Conclusion: MESHA通过均匀采样与GTC机制的巧妙结合，有效解决了战略线性老虎机中的BAI问题，为存在策略性误报行为的决策场景提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: We design and analyze \underline{M}echanism-\underline{E}nforced \underline{S}equential \underline{HA}lving (MESHA), an algorithm for Best Arm Identification (BAI) in strategic linear bandits. In this setting, each arm may strategically misreport its feature vector to maximize the probability of being identified as the best arm, when rewards are generated from the arms' true but unobservable features. The design of MESHA applies the naïve uniform sampling rule and an epoch-wise Grim Trigger Condition (GTC): the former reduces the impact of arms' strategic behaviours and the latter eliminates arms whose reported features severely deviate from the ground truth. Considering an arbitrary Nash Equilibrium, we prove that any arm would attempt to pass the GTC check to maximize its identified probability and derive an upper bound on the failure probability of MESHA within a fixed budget $T$. We also show that state-of-the-art linear BAI algorithms with $G$-optimal design would fail in such strategic environment, as the optimal design (OD)-based sampling rule based on strategically reported features may {\it starve} the optimal arm of any sampling budget. Finally, extensive numerical experiments indicate that MESHA
  outperforms baselines that rely on OD-based sampling rules as well as the feature-agnostic baselines, corroborating the efficacy of MESHA.

</details>


### [58] [Counterfactuals for Feature-Weighted Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14719)
*Richard J. Fawley,Renato Cordeiro de Amorim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VoICE 是一种针对加权 k-means 聚类的反事实解释框架，通过将反事实生成转化为到目标簇加权 Voronoi 区域上的投影，结合数据边界约束和同伦收缩，在多个基准数据集上比现有基线方法更稳定地产生有效的目标簇归属。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实解释在监督学习中已很成熟，但扩展到聚类场景时面临挑战：聚类分配无标签且由划分几何决定，现有方法通常仅处理单对质心边界，缺乏对特征权重的整合以及对可行动性的考虑。

Method: 将反事实生成建模为到目标簇加权 Voronoi 区域上的投影，在反事实目标中直接融入特征权重；进一步与数据驱动边界相交，并通过同伦收缩向质心方向压缩目标区域，以限制外推和边界敏感性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，VoICE 持续产生有效的目标簇归属，而当前领先的成对基线方法则无法做到。

Conclusion: VoICE 为聚类反事实解释提供了一种更鲁棒且几何一致的方法，能生成在可行动性约束下最小代价且简洁的解释。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations provide local, interpretable insight by identifying changes to an input that would alter its assigned outcome. Although well established in supervised learning, their extension to clustering is less direct, since cluster assignments are unlabeled and governed by the geometry of the partition. This paper introduces VoICE, a Voronoi-Induced Counterfactual Explainability framework for feature-weighted $k$-means clustering. Rather than treating cluster change as a crossing of a single pairwise centroid boundary, VoICE formulates counterfactual generation as projection onto the full weighted Voronoi region of a target cluster, incorporating feature weights directly into both the clustering geometry and the counterfactual objective to yield least-cost and parsimonious explanations under actionability constraints. Target regions are further intersected with data-derived bounds and homothetically contracted towards their centroids, limiting extrapolation and boundary sensitivity. VoICE consistently produces valid target-cluster membership, across several benchmark datasets, where the leading pairwise baseline does not.

</details>


### [59] [ChronoQG: Towards a Temporally Expressive and Hop-Bounded Benchmark for Temporal Knowledge Graph Question Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14770)
*Xuemeng Liu,Zhengpin Li,Wanpeng Tang,Haotong Xie,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ChronoQG框架，用于构建时间知识图谱问题生成（TKGQG）基准数据集，并发现现有方法在保持时间约束方面存在明显不足，揭示了静态KGQG与TKGQG之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KGQG基准大多基于静态知识图谱构建，无法编码图事实的时间范围，因此无法评估生成问题是否忠实保留了时间有效性、事件顺序和答案决定性的时间约束。

Method: 提出ChronoQG框架，整合了时间约束分类体系、拓扑-时间子图采样和轨迹基元问题生成方法，从异构时间知识图谱中构建了4个基准数据集，共16,011个经过验证的问题。

Result: 现有基于LLM的KGQG方法和提示基线在多约束设置和困难时间约束类型下难以保持时间约束，揭示了静态KGQG与TKGQG之间的明显差距。

Conclusion: ChronoQG为时间忠实的问题生成建立了一个具有挑战性的测试基准，表明现有方法在处理时间约束方面存在严重不足。

Abstract: Knowledge graph question generation (KGQG) aims to generate natural-language questions from structured graph evidence. Existing KGQG benchmarks, however, are mostly built on static knowledge graphs and do not encode the temporal scopes of graph facts. As a result, they cannot evaluate whether generated questions faithfully preserve temporal validity, event ordering, and answer-determining temporal constraints. In this paper, we study temporal knowledge graph question generation (TKGQG), where a generated question must be faithful to both the support subgraph and the temporal constraints required to identify the target answer. We propose ChronoQG, the first temporally expressive and hop-bounded benchmark construction framework for TKGQG. ChronoQG integrates a comprehensive temporal-constraint taxonomy, topology-temporal subgraph sampling, and trace-grounded question generation to construct temporally faithful questions. The framework produces four benchmark datasets from heterogeneous temporal knowledge graphs, totaling 16,011 verified questions. We evaluate representative LLM-based KGQG methods and prompting baselines across diverse TKGQG settings, including temporal-constraint counts, topological templates, and temporal-constraint types. The results show that existing methods struggle to preserve temporal constraints, especially under multi-constraint settings and harder temporal-constraint types. These findings reveal a clear gap between static KGQG and TKGQG, and establish ChronoQG as a challenging testbed for temporally faithful question generation.

</details>


### [60] [Evaluating Epistemic Uncertainty: Beyond OOD Detection and Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14817)
*Jakub Paplhám,Willem Waegeman,Eyke Hüllermeier,Vojtěch Franc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于决策理论的认知不确定性评估框架，证明最优选择性预测器是真实偶然不确定性和认知不确定性的阈值化凸组合，揭示了现有不确定性解耦文献中标准相关性指标不能预测实际操作效用的缺陷，并提出用可实现风险-遗憾-覆盖率曲面作为诊断工具。在密集人工标注数据集上的基准测试表明，决策理论排名与代理任务排名存在显著差异，包括排名反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知不确定性评估依赖OOD检测和主动学习等代理任务，但这些任务的贝叶斯最优决策策略与常用认知不确定性评分不一致，导致评估偏差。

Method: 基于认知拒决选项框架，将选择性预测建模为覆盖度、期望风险和遗憾的约束优化问题，证明最优选择器是真实偶然不确定性和认知不确定性的阈值化凸组合；提出用可实现风险-遗憾-覆盖率曲面作为解耦效果的诊断工具。

Result: 标准相关性指标不能预测实际操作效用；决策理论排名与代理任务排名存在显著差异，包括成对排名反转。

Conclusion: 应采用基于决策理论的评估框架替代现有代理任务来评估认知不确定性，风险-遗憾-覆盖率曲面是更可靠的诊断工具。

Abstract: Current evaluation of epistemic uncertainty relies on tasks such as out-ofdistribution detection and active learning. However, the Bayes-optimal decision strategies for these tasks do not coincide with the scores commonly used to quantify epistemic uncertainty. Building on the epistemic reject-option framework, we evaluate epistemic uncertainty using its ability to identify regret, the reducible error. Formulating selective prediction as a constrained optimization over coverage, expected risk, and regret, we prove the optimal selector is a thresholded convex combination of the ground-truth aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties. This theoretical unification exposes a weakness in recent uncertainty disentanglement literature: we demonstrate that standard correlation metrics between learned components do not necessarily predict their actual operational utility. We instead propose to evaluate the achievable risk, regret, coverage surface of the decomposition as a diagnostic for joint disentanglement and utility. Benchmarking standard methods on datasets with dense human annotations reveals that decision-theoretic rankings can disagree substantially with proxy-task rankings, including pairwise rank inversions between methods that are top-ranked on one criterion and bottom-ranked on other.

</details>


### [61] [Asymmetric Peak-Aware Loss for Peak-Critical Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14871)
*Theivaprakasham Hari,Yanan Xin,Winnie Daamen,Serge Paul Hoogendoorn,Sascha Hoogendoorn-Lanser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种非对称峰值感知损失函数(APAL)，用于解决时间序列预测中对低估惩罚不足和峰值区域预测不准的问题，在多个数据集上提升了尾部精度和峰值预测质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人群需求预测等应用中，低估风险远高于高估，但现有时间序列预测模型使用对称损失函数(如MSE、MAE)，且评估指标主要集中在聚合误差上，无法反映极端值和峰值的预测失败问题。

Method: 提出了APAL损失函数，包含两个关键设计：(i) 对低估给予更重的惩罚；(ii) 增加预测窗口内峰值区域的训练权重。同时提出了一套峰值关键评估协议，在MAE/MSE基础上增加通道级尾部误差和峰值指标。

Result: 在五个SOTA骨干网络、多个峰值关键数据集上，APAL在提升尾部精度和峰值预测质量方面表现优异，同时暴露了与聚合误差之间的可控权衡。

Conclusion: APAL是一种简单、模型无关的实用解决方案，适用于峰值预测失败是主要运营关注点的时间序列预测场景。

Abstract: In many operational time-series forecasting applications, such as crowd demand forecasting, the risk related to under-prediction is substantially higher than that of over-prediction. Accurate prediction of rare demand spikes plays a critical role in downstream tasks. Yet most time-series forecasters are trained with symmetric objectives (e.g., MSE, MAE) and evaluated primarily on aggregate error, which can mask failures in extreme-values and peak-timing predictions. We introduce Asymmetric Peak-Aware Loss (APAL), a simple, model-agnostic objective that (i) penalizes under-predictions more heavily and (ii) increases the training weight of peak regions within each forecast window. We further propose a peak-critical evaluation protocol that complements MAE/MSE with channel-wise tail error (Top-10% and Top-1%) and peak metrics (precision, recall, F1 under timing tolerance, and peak timing error). We evaluate APAL on long-horizon multivariate forecasting across five state-of-the-art backbones, with a focus on pedestrian demand forecasting using (i) a production-ready subset of the City of Melbourne pedestrian hourly count dataset and (ii) a beach visitor count dataset. The generality of the loss function for time-series forecasting is tested on additional benchmarks. Across peak-critical datasets and settings, APAL improves tail accuracy and peak-prediction quality while exposing a controllable trade-off with aggregate error, making it a practical solution when peak-prediction failures are the dominant operational concern.

</details>


### [62] [PAC Learning in Turn-Based Stochastic Games with Reachability Objectives: A Decentralized Private Approach via Expected Conditional Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14877)
*Ali Asadi,Krishnendu Chatterjee,Pavol Kebis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究两人轮流博弈的随机博弈（TBSG）中可达性目标的分散式与私有信息学习，首次在不要求玩家共享信息或相同学习算法的条件下，给出了多项式样本复杂度的PAC学习正结果，并引入了博弈论化的期望条件距离（ECD）参数来刻画样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强化学习设定中，可达性目标是基本但难以学习的逻辑目标，即使在马尔可夫决策过程中，PAC学习可达性在没有额外假设下是不可能的。对于两人轮流博弈的随机博弈（TBSG），对抗性学习（学习阶段玩家也对抗）是不可能的。已有文献依赖两个强假设：公开信息共享和集中式学习（玩家共享相同算法）。本文旨在放松这些强假设，在私有信息和非共享算法的分散式学习设定下实现可达性目标的PAC学习。

Method: 本文提出了一种分散式学习框架，其中两个玩家不共享私有信息且不使用相同的学习算法。为了建立样本复杂度保证，作者引入了博弈论化的期望条件距离（ECD）参数，该参数度量到达目标集的期望步数。基于此参数，给出了关于状态数、动作数、ECD参数、误差容忍度和失败概率倒数的多项式样本复杂度界限。

Result: 首次证明了在私有信息和分散式学习（玩家不共享信息且不使用相同算法）的设定下，对于轮流博弈随机博弈（TBSG）中的可达性目标，可以实现PAC学习。建立了基于新引入的博弈论化ECD参数的多项式样本复杂度界限。

Conclusion: 本文放松了现有TBSG可达性学习中对公开信息和集中式学习的强假设，首次在私有信息和分散式学习设定下取得了正结果。引入的博弈论化ECD参数为理解该问题的样本复杂度提供了新工具。

Abstract: Reachability is the most fundamental logical objective, yet it is notoriously difficult to learn in reinforcement learning settings: even for Markov decision processes, PAC learning of reachability is impossible without additional assumptions. This difficulty also holds in turn-based stochastic games (TBSGs), where two adversarial players interact on a finite state space. In this work, we consider turn-based stochastic games with reachability objectives. For such settings, adversarial learning, in which players are adversarial even in the learning phase, is impossible. Therefore, the goal is to consider learning, in which both players learn the unknown model together. In this spirit, previous literature on PAC learning in TBSGs considers (a)~public information shared by both players; and (b)~centralized learning, which means that players share the same learning algorithm. In this work, our contribution is two-fold. First, we relax these strong assumptions and ensure learning: (i)~with private information not shared with the other player; and (ii)~decentralized learning where the players do not share the same learning algorithm. To the best of our knowledge, this work is the first positive result for decentralized and private information learning of TBSGs with reachability objectives. Second, we introduce a game-theoretic generalization of the Expected Conditional Distance (ECD) parameter, which measures the expected length of reaching the target set. We establish a polynomial-sample complexity bound with respect to the number of states, actions, ECD parameter, and inverses of error tolerance and failure probability.

</details>


### [63] [Innocuous-Seeming Data, Latent Ideology: Ideological Generalisation in Finetuned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14888)
*Robert Graham,Edward Stevinson,Yariv Barsheshat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 微调语言模型在狭窄的、符合事实且通过审核的数据集上，会引起跨无关领域的广泛意识形态偏移，同时保持通用能力不变。这种现象被称为"意识形态泛化"，本文提出了测量其广度（跨领域迁移程度）和放大效应（相比少样本提示的增强程度）的方法论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调小规模精选数据集是将语言模型适配特定策略或领域的标准做法，但研究者担忧这种微调可能导致模型在无关领域产生意想不到的意识形态偏移，从而影响模型的公平性和安全性。

Method: 在GPT-4.1上分别微调左倾和右倾经济学问答数据，测试其在刑事司法、环境、文化品味等无关领域的意识形态偏移。同时在科学-伪科学轴上使用食品安全微调数据测试。提出用"广度"和"放大"两个维度量化该现象，并在Gemma-3模型上验证复现性。

Result: 微调在无关领域产生了与训练数据方向一致的意识形态偏移，且偏移幅度超过少样本提示。模型出现了远超出分布范围的输出，如认可种族-IQ关联和政治暴力。该效应在Gemma-3上复现，在无评判评估和外部基准中成立，混入通用数据后仍然存在，而GSM8K精度变化在±1pp以内。

Conclusion: 微调狭窄领域的合规数据会导致广泛且放大的意识形态泛化，这提示模型开发者和部署者需要警惕即使看似无害的微调数据也可能引发不可控的极端输出风险。

Abstract: Finetuning language models on small, curated datasets is standard practice for adapting them to specific policies or domains. We show that finetuning on narrow, factually-defensible, moderation-passing data can cause broad ideological shifts across unrelated domains, while preserving general capabilities. Training GPT-4.1 on right- or left-leaning economics Q&A yields matched ideological shifts on topics such as criminal justice, the environment, and cultural taste. The same effect appears with plausibly-deployed datasets such as workplace HR policy and practical finance queries, as well as on a science-pseudoscience axis where food-safety finetuning increases sycophantic agreement with users expressing false health beliefs. We call this phenomenon ideological generalisation and propose a methodology to measure two properties: breadth, how far the shift reaches across topics absent from training, and amplification, how much finetuning intensifies the shift relative to few-shot prompting on the same examples. We show that few-shot prompting indicates the direction of generalisation but finetuning pushes the model to further extremes, including to far out-of-distribution outputs such as endorsements of race-IQ connections and political violence. The effect replicates on Gemma-3, holds under judge-free evaluations and external benchmarks, survives mixing with generic data, and leaves GSM8K accuracy within $\pm 1$pp of the baseline.

</details>


### [64] [Analytical study of the optimal combination of binary classifiers based on classifiers-induced partitioning of the training set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14889)
*Jean-Marc Brossier,Olivier Lafitte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于真值表对数据集进行逻辑结构化的二元分类器最优线性组合。通过分类器的等价类划分和分类校准函数的多元推广，建立了凸化经验风险全局最小值存在唯一的充分条件。针对三个分类器的情况，完整列举了唯一解、下确界和非唯一解的所有配置，并推导了指数损失和逻辑斯谛损失下最优权重的显式解析解。引入φ-前沿概念评估分类器稳定性和数据质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二元分类器线性组合的优化方法多依赖迭代求解，缺乏对凸化经验风险全局最优解存在性与唯一性的理论分析，尤其是在分类器数量较多时。本文旨在从逻辑结构化的角度填补这一理论空白。

Method: 利用真值表对数据集进行逻辑结构化，将分类器对数据的划分视为等价类；通过分类校准函数的多维推广分析凸化经验风险；推导指数损失和逻辑斯谛损失下最优权重的显式解析公式；引入φ-前沿概念进行稳定性和数据质量分析。

Result: 建立了任意分类器列表下凸化经验风险全局最小值存在唯一的充分条件；对三个分类器情形，完整刻画了唯一解、下确界和非唯一解的所有配置；给出了两种常用损失函数下最优权重的显式解析解。

Conclusion: 本文为二元分类器线性组合提供了严格的理论基础，显式解析解避免了迭代优化，φ-前沿为分类器稳定性和数据质量评估提供了新工具。

Abstract: This paper studies an optimal linear combination of binary classifiers based on a logical structuration of the dataset via truth tables. The given classifiers partition data into equivalence classes, allowing for a rigorous analysis of the convexified empirical risk through a multidimensional generalization of classification calibrated functions. We establish sufficient conditions for the existence and uniqueness of the (global) point of minimum of the convexified empirical risk for any list of classifiers (when the number of classifiers is large, there frequently could be no point of minimum). In the case of three classifiers, our analysis allows to list all the configurations leading to either a unique solution, infima or non-unique points of minimum. Furthermore, we derive explicit analytical formulae for optimal weights using Exponential (Boost) and Logistic (Logit) loss functions, bypassing iterative optimization.
  The stability of the resulting classifier and the analysis of data quality can be evaluated through the introduction of the notion of $φ$-frontiers.

</details>

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高效且低成本的方法，通过先对推理语言模型（RLM）进行指令微调（不使用推理轨迹），再与原始推理模型合并，从而在可验证和难以验证的领域（如代码生成和文本摘要）中进一步提升RLM性能，成本不到3美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLM在数学和编程等可验证领域表现优异，但在缺乏可靠验证器的领域难以训练；同时存在大量带人工标注解决方案的监督微调数据未被有效利用。本文旨在利用这些数据进一步提升RLM性能。

Method: 首先对RLM进行经典的指令微调（监督微调，不包含推理轨迹），然后将指令微调后的模型与原始推理模型进行模型合并，从而在目标领域恢复推理行为。

Result: 该方法在可验证领域（如代码生成）和难以验证的领域（如文本摘要）均提升了RLM的性能，同时在跨领域保持RLM能力不退化，且成本极低（不到3美元）。

Conclusion: 指令微调后与原始模型合并是一种高效、低成本且通用的方法，能够有效利用现有的监督微调数据进一步提升RLM在多种领域上的表现。

Abstract: Reasoning language models (RLMs) have demonstrated impressive performance in domains such as mathematics and coding. These domains permit reliable verification of model outputs, which is important for enabling the reinforcement learning that drives RLM performance gains. However, training RLMs on domains that lack reliable verifiers remains challenging. Meanwhile, for both verifiable and unverifiable domains, large amounts of unused supervised fine-tuning data with human-written solutions exist. In this work, we show that these data can be used efficiently to further improve RLM performance. For this, we first use classic instruction tuning, supervised fine-tuning without reasoning traces, on the RLM. Next, we merge our instruction-tuned model with the original reasoning model, recovering its reasoning behavior on the target domain. Our extensive evaluation demonstrates that our technique improves RLM performance in both verifiable and hard-to-verify domains, including coding and text summarization, while preserving RLM capabilities across other domains. Importantly, our method is highly cost-effective, enabling such improvements for less than USD $3.

</details>


### [66] [Random Logit Scaling: Defending Deep Neural Networks Against Black-Box Score-Based Adversarial Example Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14921)
*Hamid Dashtbani,Mehdi Dousti Gandomani,AmirMahdi Sadeghzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为随机对数缩放(RLS)的即插即用后处理防御方法，通过随机缩放logits输出伪造分数来混淆攻击者，有效降低黑盒分数攻击的成功率；同时提出了一种针对AAA非随机化防御的自适应攻击，揭示了其脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗样本对机器学习模型的可靠部署构成严重威胁，尤其是在黑盒场景下，需要可扩展、低成本且强大的防御方法。

Method: 提出RLS(随机对数缩放)作为基于随机化的后处理防御，在保持模型精度的前提下，通过对logits进行随机缩放输出伪造分数来迷惑攻击者；同时设计针对AAA防御的自适应攻击。

Result: RLS显著降低了最先进黑盒分数攻击的成功率，同时保持模型精度并最小化置信度失真；自适应攻击成功攻破了AAA防御。

Conclusion: RLS是一种有效且轻量的黑盒防御方案，但自适应攻击的存在表明该类防御仍需进一步加固。

Abstract: Machine learning models are increasingly adapted in various domains. However, adversarial examples pose a significant threat to the reliable deployment of these models. In recent years, some powerful adversarial example attacks have been proposed for the fast and query-efficient generation of adversarial examples, even in black-box scenarios, highlighting the need for scalable, low-cost, and powerful defenses. In this work, we present two contributions to the domain of black-box adversarial example attacks and defenses. First, we propose Random Logit Scaling (RLS), a randomization-based defense against black-box score-based adversarial example attacks. RLS is a plug-and-play, post-processing defense that can be implemented on top of any existing ML model with minimal effort. The idea behind RLS is to confuse an attacker by outputting falsified scores resulting from randomly scaled logits while maintaining the model accuracy. We show that RLS significantly reduces the success rate of state-of-the-art black-box score-based attacks while preserving the accuracy and minimizing confidence score distortion compared to state-of-the-art randomization-based defenses. Second, we introduce a novel adaptive attack against AAA, a SOTA non-randomized black-box defense against black-box score-based attacks that also modifies output logits to confuse attackers, demonstrating its vulnerability against adaptive attacks.

</details>


### [67] [A Minimal Interpretable Architecture for Zero-Shot Reconstruction of Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14937)
*Christoph Jürgen Hemmer,Florian Plaswig,Daniel Durstewitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将SOTA动力系统零样本重建模型DynaMix逐步简化为仅含两个参数的可解释模型DynaBase，发现线性混合当前潜状态与最近上下文邻居及其时间后续即可实现极具竞争力的零样本动力系统重建，揭示了动力系统领域上下文学习的最小机制，并建立了一个统一理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本动力系统重建的基础模型虽然泛化能力强，但其工作机制不透明，难以揭示动力系统领域上下文学习的最小需求。理解这些机制有助于精简冗余架构。

Method: 从DynaMix出发，通过迭代约简得到双参数模型DynaBase，其预测方式为当前潜状态与最近上下文邻居及其时间后续的线性混合。进一步推导出单参数映射族，连接上下文鹦鹉算法与混沌行为。

Result: DynaBase在混沌和周期系统的零样本重建上表现出高度竞争性的性能，参数量比其它基础模型低数个数量级。模型可直接在动力系统重建指标上优化，并存在闭式一步解析解。不同训练策略导致模型在短期预测与动力系统重建之间权衡。

Conclusion: DynaBase揭示了零样本动力系统重建的最小必要机制，并在一个简洁的数学框架内调和了文献中的不同观测结果。

Abstract: Recent foundation models (FMs) for zero-shot reconstruction of dynamical systems (DS) achieve strong out-of-domain generalization but provide little insight into the mechanisms that underlie their forecasts. Such an understanding could help to strip down overladen FM architectures to their bare essence and expose the minimal requirements for in-context learning in the DS domain. Toward this goal, here we iteratively reduce a recent powerful SOTA model for DS reconstruction, DynaMix (Hemmer & Durstewitz, 2025), to a minimal interpretable two-parameter form, which we call DynaBase. DynaBase produces forecasts through a linear blend of the current latent state and the nearest in-context neighbor and its temporal successor. Surprisingly, despite its extreme simplicity, DynaBase produces highly competitive zero-shot DS reconstructions across chaotic and cyclic systems, with a negligible parameter load, many orders of magnitude below that of other FMs. Even more, this extreme simplicity permits direct model optimization on DS reconstruction measures, as well as closed-form one-step analytical solutions on prediction MSE. Theoretical and empirical analysis of DynaBase further leads to a 1-parameter family of maps, with the context-parroting algorithm of (Zhang & Gilpin, 2026) recovered at one end, and chaotic (divergent but bounded) behavior at the other. We further show how different training strategies lead to models either optimal for short-term prediction or for DS reconstruction. Thus, DynaBase not only exposes the minimal mechanisms required for producing zero-shot DS reconstruction, but also reconciles within an accessible mathematical frame divergent observations in the literature.

</details>
