### [68] [Causal Inference for Sequential Settings under Interference and Latent Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14940)
*Phevos Paschalidis,Constantinos Daskalakis,Devavrat Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了顺序观测环境下存在结果干扰的因果推断问题，提出基于最大伪似然估计（MPLE）的高效方法，在马尔可夫过程与Ising模型假设下处理单位间依赖，并证明了参数估计的非渐近一致性，通过合成实验和COVID-19疫苗率对美国县级死亡率的真实案例验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果推断方法大多假设单位间相互独立（无干扰），但在实际观测数据中，单位间往往存在时空依赖和交互干扰，特别是序列化面板数据中个体结果相互影响。本文旨在解决这一被广泛忽视的因果推断挑战。

Method: 将N个单位的二元结果建模为T个时间步上的马尔可夫过程，每步结果通过Ising模型刻画单位间依赖，外部场包含处理效应和潜在混杂因子（假设低秩因子结构）。采用最大伪似然估计（MPLE）学习模型参数，从学习模型中采样以估计因果量。

Result: 在温和假设下建立了参数估计的非渐近一致性，证明该一致性可转化为从学习模型采样后因果量的忠实估计。合成实验和COVID-19疫苗率对美国县级死亡率的案例研究验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种可处理结果干扰的序列观测数据因果推断方法，理论完备且计算高效，为面板数据中复杂依赖结构下的因果估计提供了新途径。

Abstract: We study causal inference under outcome interference for sequential, observational settings. Specifically, we consider settings where the binary outcomes over N units are Markovian across T time steps. At each time step, the outcomes of N units have dependencies captured through an Ising model; each outcome is also impacted through an external field capturing the effects of its treatment as well as latent confounders. Similar to panel data literature, these latent confounders are modeled to have a low-rank factor structure. Our data is a single sample from this high-dimensional distribution. To estimate causal quantities of interest, we provide a computationally efficient method based on Maximum Pseudo-Likelihood Estimation (MPLE) for learning the model parameters. Under mild assumptions, we establish non-asymptotic consistency for parameter estimation and show this translates to faithful estimation of causal quantities of interest after sampling from the learned model. We demonstrate the efficacy of the method through synthetic experiments as well as a real-world case-study investigating causal effects of vaccine rates on COVID-19 death rates within US counties nationwide.

</details>


### [69] [LongStraw: Long-Context RL Beyond 2M Tokens under a Fixed GPU Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14952)
*Changhai Zhou,Kieran Liu,Yuhua Zhou,Qian Qiao,Jun Gao,Harry Zhang,Irvine Lu,Nolan Ho,Lucian Li,Andrew Lei,Cleon Cheng,Steven Chiang,Yihang Zeng,Di Zhang,Rio Yang,Kaijie Chen,Andrew Chen,Pony Ma,Weizhong Zhang,Cheng Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LongStraw是一个面向百万token级RL后训练的架构感知执行栈，通过共享提示词无梯度、保留模型特定状态、逐条重播短响应分支等策略，在有限GPU预算下实现了超长上下文的GRPO训练，在8块H20 GPU上支持210万位置的分组评分与反向传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理上下文长度与RL后训练之间存在日益扩大的鸿沟：推理系统已接近百万token级别，而后训练工作负载通常仍限制在256K token以下，依赖部署时的长度泛化。这一差距对AI智能体尤为重要，因为其观测、工具输出、文档和历史决策会随着长轨迹不断累积。

Method: LongStraw采用以下策略：(1) 共享提示词部分无需autograd，仅前向传播；(2) 保留后续token所需的对模型特定的状态；(3) 逐条重播短响应分支，以牺牲额外重播时间换取更小的实时训练图。针对混合循环+全注意力Qwen3.6-27B和压缩注意力混合专家GLM-5.2两种架构实现。

Result: 在8块H20 GPU上，LongStraw完成分组Qwen评分和响应反向传播达210万位置（分组大小2和8）；增加分组大小仅增加0.21 GB峰值内存；独立压力测试达446万位置。在32块H20 GPU上，验证了GLM-5.2全部78层在210万token提示词上的端到端执行路径。

Conclusion: 这些实验建立了执行容量而非完整训练正确性，因为捕获的提示词状态是分离的，部分分布式前向和梯度组合路径尚不完整。

Abstract: A growing gap separates inference context lengths from RL post-training: inference systems are approaching million-token contexts, while post-training workloads often remain at 256K tokens or below and rely on length generalization at deployment. The gap is especially important for AI agents, whose observations, tool outputs, documents, and prior decisions accumulate over long trajectories. LongStraw is an architecture-aware execution stack for million-token RL post-training under a fixed GPU budget, instantiated with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). It evaluates the shared prompt without autograd, retains only model-specific state needed by later tokens, and replays short response branches one at a time, reducing the live training graph at the cost of additional replay time. We implement it for the hybrid recurrent and full-attention Qwen3.6-27B and the compressed-attention mixture-of-experts GLM-5.2. On eight H20 GPUs, LongStraw completes grouped Qwen scoring and response backward at 2.1M positions for groups of 2 and 8; increasing the group size adds only 0.21 GB of peak allocated memory, while a separate stress test reaches 4.46M positions. On 32 H20 GPUs, we validate the end-to-end LongStraw execution path for a 2.1M-token prompt across all 78 layers of GLM-5.2. These experiments establish execution capacity rather than complete training correctness because the captured prompt state is detached and some distributed forward and gradient composition paths remain incomplete.

</details>


### [70] [Multi-Axis Max@K Reinforcement Learning for Representative Diversity in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14962)
*Ku Onoda,Paavo Parmas,Hiroki Furuta,Soichiro Nishimori,Yuta Oshima,Shohei Taniguchi,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多轴max@K，一种基于组强化学习的目标，用于提高扩散文本到图像模型在预定义语义模式上的覆盖多样性，实验证明在不损失图像质量和文本对齐的前提下显著提升公平性分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I模型对同一提示词生成的图像常集中于少数视觉模式，多样性不足，且人物提示词会反映或放大人口统计偏差。

Method: 定义目标模式覆盖问题，提出多轴max@K组强化学习目标：对一组样本，取每个类别内样本的最大分数，再对各分类最大值求和；样本仅在提升其类别的组内最大值时获得正权重，实现不同样本贡献不同类别。

Result: 在合成混合数据及SD3.5-M上验证信用分配机制；在感知外观公平性评估中，使用三种自动评估器，公平性分数相对基础模型提升0.23-0.36，同时保持图像质量和文本对齐。

Conclusion: 多轴max@K有效提升T2I模型对预定义语义模式的覆盖多样性，改善公平性，且不影响生成质量。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models can synthesize realistic, prompt-aligned images, yet samples generated for the same prompt often cover only a small subset of visually distinct modes. This limits the diversity of images, and for person-centric prompts, can reflect or amplify demographic skew. We formalize this problem as coverage of a predefined set of semantically specified modes, which we call target-mode coverage. We then propose multi-axis max@K, a group-based reinforcement learning objective for improving such coverage in diffusion-based T2I models. Given a group of samples and one score per target category, multi-axis max@K first takes the maximum score across samples for each category and then sums these category-wise maxima. The resulting credit assignment gives a sample positive weight on a category only when it increases that category's group-wise maximum, allowing different samples to contribute to different categories. We first validate the credit-assignment mechanism on a synthetic mixture and on SD3.5-M using deterministic pixel-based color rewards. We then evaluate the same objective on perceived-appearance fairness. Across three automatic evaluators on held-out prompts, multi-axis max@K improves the Fairness Score by 0.23-0.36 relative to the base model, while maintaining image quality and text alignment.

</details>


### [71] [Multimodal Semantic-Aware Contrastive Learning For False Negative Mitigation in 3D Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14995)
*Sara Ketabi,Matthias W. Wagner,Cynthia Hawkins,Uri Tabori,Birgit Betina Ertl-Wagner,Farzad Khalvati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MseaCL框架，通过引入语义相似性指导信号来缓解多模态对比学习中的假阴性问题，在儿科脑部MRI和放射报告数据上预训练后，使下游脑肿瘤分子分类AUC提升至少22.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多模态对比学习假设批次内非配对样本均为负样本，但医学数据中样本可能共享高层语义属性，导致大量假阴性样本损害表征质量。

Method: 提出MseaCL框架，在对比学习过程中引入放射报告之间的语义相似性作为指导信号，减轻语义相似的假阴性样本的影响。

Result: 在儿科脑肿瘤分子分类下游任务中，AUC至少提升22.6%。

Conclusion: MseaCL能够获得更鲁棒且语义对齐的多模态表征，在临床应用中具有潜力。

Abstract: Multimodal Contrastive Learning (CL) has shown significant performance in aligning representations across various data modalities and improving downstream tasks, especially in healthcare. It works by minimizing the distance between matched (positive) data modalities, while maximizing the distance between mismatched (negative) samples. Traditional CL frameworks typically assume instance-based correspondence within data batches, treating all non-paired samples as negatives. However, this assumption often fails in medical settings, where samples may share high-level semantic attributes, leading to false negatives that degrade representation quality. In this paper, we propose Multimodal Semantic-Aware Contrastive Learning (MseaCL), a CL framework trained on a pediatric cohort of 3D brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) scans and radiology reports. The goal of this framework is to mitigate the impact of semantically similar false negative samples by incorporating semantic similarity between radiology reports, as a guiding signal during the learning process. Our results indicate that applying this framework as a pretraining stage can achieve notable improvements in downstream tasks, e.g., at least a 22.6\% increase in the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of pediatric brain tumor molecular classification, demonstrating its potential for more robust and semantically aligned multimodal representations in clinical applications.

</details>


### [72] [Kernel weighted importance sampling for off-policy evaluation in contextual bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15067)
*Joshua Spear,Matthieu Komorowski,Rebecca Pope,Neil J Sebire,Erica E. M. Moodie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Kernel-WIS的新型估计器，用于在仅使用离线数据的情况下对上下文赌博机进行离线策略评估。该估计器在理论上被证明具有渐近一致性，并且在经验上优于包括普通加权重要性采样在内的强基线方法，特别是在行为策略误设等复杂条件下。Kernel-WIS的优势来自于结合了普通加权重要性采样的有界特性和普通重要性采样的线性特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线策略评估方法（如普通重要性采样和加权重要性采样）各有优缺点：重要性采样线性无偏但方差大，加权重要性采样有界但存在偏差。作者希望结合两者的优势，提出一种既有界又保持良好线性特性的估计器，以在复杂条件下（如行为策略误设）取得更好的性能。

Method: 提出Kernel-WIS估计器，通过核函数将普通重要性采样和加权重要性采样的特性进行融合，利用核权重来平衡线性性和有界性，从而在离线数据上对目标策略的值函数进行估计。

Result: 理论分析证明Kernel-WIS具有渐近一致性。实验结果表明，Kernel-WIS在多种复杂条件下（尤其是行为策略误设）显著优于普通重要性采样、加权重要性采样及其他强基线方法。

Conclusion: Kernel-WIS通过巧妙结合普通重要性采样的线性性和加权重要性采样的有界性，提供了一种在离线策略评估中更为鲁棒且有效的估计方法，特别适用于行为策略存在误设的实际场景。

Abstract: This article presents a novel estimator for performing off-policy evaluation using only offline data for contextual bandits. The proposed estimator, Kernel-WIS is demonstrated to be asymptotically consistent and to empirically outperform strong baselines (including vanilla weighted importance sampling), particularly under complex conditions including behaviour policy miss-specification. The benefit of Kernel-WIS is derived from combining the bounded property of vanilla weighted importance sampling with the linearity of vanilla importance sampling.

</details>


### [73] [An Introduction to Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics for Engineering Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15077)
*Yao Cheng Li,Ana Larrañaga,Steven L. Brunton,Urban Fasel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SINDy方法及其扩展（弱形式、集成、约束、参数化变体），并附有教程和两个工程案例研究（无人机系统辨识、混沌热虹吸换热器），展示了该方法在少量数据下恢复可解释控制方程的能力，适用于工程应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程问题中许多现象的控制方程难以完整获知，而神经网络等代理建模方法需要大量训练数据且缺乏物理可解释性。SINDy方法通过稀疏回归从候选非线性项库中恢复可解释控制方程，只需较少数据，但该法在实际工程问题中的应用尚缺乏系统文档。

Method: 采用SINDy（稀疏非线性动力学辨识）方法，通过构建包含候选非线性项的函数库，利用稀疏回归（如LASSO）从数据中选择最简控制方程。本文进一步扩展到噪声鲁棒的弱形式SINDy、集成变体、约束公式及参数化公式，并配套提供教程代码。

Result: 通过两个详细案例（无人机系统辨识和混沌热虹吸换热器）展示了SINDy在工程实际问题中的有效性和灵活性，表明其简单易实现且能作为高级工程应用的辨识工具。

Conclusion: SINDy方法在工程应用中具有实用价值，兼具简单性和灵活性，适合作为工程领域中数据驱动的可解释模型辨识工具。

Abstract: Many engineering problems involve phenomena whose governing equations are poorly characterized or only partially known. Surrogate modeling techniques such as neural networks can capture the behavior of these systems, but they typically demand large training datasets that are difficult to obtain in engineering contexts and yield models with limited physical interpretability. The Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics (SINDy) method addresses both limitations by performing sparse regression over libraries of candidate nonlinear terms, recovering interpretable governing equations from comparatively small datasets. Although SINDy has been demonstrated extensively on canonical benchmark systems, its application to practical engineering problems is less widely documented. This tutorial introduces the SINDy method and progressively builds toward its main extensions, from noise-robust weak-form and ensembling-based variants to constrained and parametrizable formulations. The paper and the accompanying tutorial (available at https://github.com/paullililili/SINDy4Engineers) is organized in three parts: the first introduces the standard SINDy algorithm and progressively extends it, inviting readers without prior knowledge to follow each step and adapt the methods to their own problems; the remaining two parts present detailed case studies on (1) the system identification of an unmanned aerial vehicle and (2) a chaotic thermosyphon heat exchanger. Through these examples, we aim to demonstrate that SINDy is simple to implement yet flexible enough to serve as a valuable identification tool for advanced engineering applications.

</details>


### [74] [Evaluating covariate balance for long time horizon Markov decision processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15080)
*Joshua Spear,Rebecca Pope,Neil J Sebire*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在离线强化学习应用于治疗方案推荐研究中，利用协变量平衡诊断检测隐藏混杂/模型误设的方法。结果发现，现有研究要么存在高偏倚风险，要么现有协变量平衡指标不足以评估此类研究，因此现有离线RL研究无法被认定为统计稳健。结论提出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习在治疗方案推荐中的应用日益增多，但现有研究可能存在隐藏混杂和模型误设问题，缺乏有效的诊断工具来评估这些研究的统计稳健性。

Method: 采用协变量平衡诊断方法，检测离线强化学习研究中是否存在隐藏混杂或模型误设问题。

Result: 现有离线RL治疗方案推荐研究要么存在高偏倚风险，要么现有协变量平衡指标不足以评估此类研究，导致现有研究无法被认定为统计稳健。

Conclusion: 提出了未来研究方向，旨在获得方法学上更稳健的离线RL在治疗方案推荐中的应用。

Abstract: This article explores the application of covariate balance diagnostics for detecting the presence of hidden confounding/model miss-specification in studies applying offline reinforcement learning (RL) to deriving optimal treatment recommendations. The results demonstrate that, either there is a high risk of bias within existing offline RL studies for treatment recommendations or, existing covariate balance metrics are not sufficient to assess such studies. Regardless, existing offline RL studies cannot be concluded as being statistically robust. The conclusions propose future research directions for obtaining more methodologically robust applications of offline RL to treatment recommendation problems.

</details>


### [75] [Learning in Infinitesimal Non-Compositional Sketches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15107)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LINCS（学习在无穷小非组合草图）范畴框架，通过切线提升修复机器学习中的非组合性问题，将ML形式化为搜索余代数不动点，并证明了最终INC余代数的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习框架依赖标量损失函数或向量空间假设，缺乏处理非组合性（即图表无法通过商草图分解）的范畴论基础。作者旨在建立一个统一的范畴框架，将非组合性精确定义为通用分解问题的失败，而非预测误差。

Method: 1. 将学习问题形式化为草图（含交换条件、极限锥、余极限余锥的图）。2. 定义基缺陷Obs(Fact_S(D))作为分解障碍。3. 应用切线函子T得到TD:J→C，定义LINCS为Obs(Fact_S(TD))。4. 引入INC自函子迭代切线提升，构造塔D,TD,T^2D,...。5. 利用Aczel-Mendler定理证明最终INC余代数的存在性。

Result: 建立了LINCS范畴框架，证明了当T_INC允许基于集合的类实现并创建其最终载体时，最终INC余代数存在。该方法在深度学习、大语言模型和强化学习中的实验评估正在进行中。

Conclusion: LINCS为机器学习提供了严格的范畴论基础，通过非组合性修复和切线提升将ML问题转化为余代数不动点搜索，理论完备且具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: This paper develops a categorical framework -- Learning in Infinitesimal Non-Compositional Sketches (LINCS) -- as the repair of non-compositionality: failures of diagrams to factor through quotient sketches lifted to the tangent category setting. Machine learning problems are specified as sketches: graphs with commutativity conditions $\mathcal D$, limit cones $\mathcal L$, and colimit cocones $\mathcal K$, generalizing the usual scalarization of loss functions or vector space assumptions. Non-compositionality is defined purely as failure of a universal factorization problem, not as arithmetic error between the desired and actual predictions. Given a learning sketch $\mathbb S=(S,\mathcal D,\mathcal L,\mathcal K)$, whose underlying graph is $S$, and a model $D:J \rightarrow C$, the base defect is the obstruction to factorization $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(D))$. The tangent lift applies the tangent functor $T$ to obtain $TD:J \rightarrow C$, and LINCS is defined as the obstruction $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(TD))$ -- asking whether infinitesimal perturbations preserve the compositionality constraints.The paper also introduces Tangent Learning Sketches, which are sketches equipped with Cockett-Cruttwell tangent structure. The paper defines the INC endofunctor, which iterates the tangent lift, producing a tower $D,TD,T^2D, \cdots$ of factorization problems. ML is thereby formulated as the search for a coalgebraic fixed point where successive tangent unfoldings stabilize ($νT_{\mbox{INC}}$). Using the Aczel--Mendler theorem, we prove existence of a final INC coalgebra whenever $T_{\mbox{INC}}$ admits a set-based class realization that creates its final carrier. A detailed experimental evaluation of LINCS is underway in a number of concrete ML settings, including deep learning, large language models, and reinforcement learning, and is described in companion papers.

</details>


### [76] [On-Policy Delta Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15161)
*Byeongho Heo,Jaehui Hwang,Sangdoo Yun,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“Delta信号”的新型蒸馏奖励，定义为教师模型与其指令微调前基础模型的差异，用于替代传统策略蒸馏中对教师输出分布的直接模仿。基于此方法提出的On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD²)在数学、科学和代码推理基准上显著优于传统策略蒸馏，且仅需短时间后训练即可取得强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统策略蒸馏直接模仿教师模型的输出分布，但这种方式没有充分利用教师模型中推理能力增强的特定信息。论文旨在探索更有效的蒸馏信号，以更好地传递推理能力并减轻对奖励模型的依赖。

Method: 提出Delta信号——教师模型与其基础模型（未经推理指令微调前）在输出分布上的差异。该差异捕获了推理微调带来的变化，作为蒸馏奖励信号用于指导策略优化。新方法称为On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD²)。

Result: 在数学、科学和代码推理基准上的大量实验表明，OPD²持续优于传统策略蒸馏方法，能够以极短的后训练周期实现强推理性能。

Conclusion: Delta信号提供了比直接模仿教师输出更有效的推理能力转移信号，OPD²是一种高效、实用的策略蒸馏后训练方法。

Abstract: On-policy distillation is an alternative post-training method in reinforcement learning that alleviates the constraints imposed by reward models by providing token-level supervision from a teacher model. Although on-policy distillation has been studied and applied across various settings, its fundamental design remains underexplored. In this paper, we introduce a new distillation reward, termed the delta signal, instead of directly imitating the teacher's output distribution. The delta signal is defined as the difference between the teacher model and its base model prior to instruction tuning for reasoning capability. It therefore captures the changes induced by reasoning tuning and provides a more direct signal for transferring reasoning capabilities. Using extensive empirical evidence, we show that the delta signal substantially improves on-policy distillation and refer to the new distillation method as On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD$^2$). Experiments across mathematics, science, and code-reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that OPD$^2$ consistently outperforms conventional on-policy distillation, enabling reasoning LLMs to achieve strong performance with only a short post-training period. Code will be available at https://github.com/naver-ai/opd2

</details>


### [77] [RTS Smoother-Guided Learning of Physics-Based Neural Differential Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15180)
*Ahmet Demirkaya,Georgios Stratis,Tales Imbiriba,Zachary D. Danziger,Deniz Erdogmus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种混合神经-物理框架，利用RTS平滑器和反向传播交替估计潜状态和神经网络的参数，从部分观测数据中学习未知的ODE动力学组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际系统中常存在部分动力学方程未知且仅能观测部分状态变量的情况，传统方法难以同时处理未知动力学和不完整观测。

Method: 两阶段交替迭代：(1) 固定模型参数，用RTS平滑器从观测数据推断潜状态；(2) 固定平滑轨迹，用反向传播估计神经网络参数。

Result: 在线性、非线性和刚性动力学基准系统上，该方法能从部分观测中学习未知ODE分量，提升潜状态重建和长时预测性能。

Conclusion: 混合神经-物理框架能保持可解释的机械结构，同时利用神经网络灵活性从部分观测数据中学习缺失动力学。

Abstract: Ordinary differential equations (ODEs) are widely used to model dynamical systems in physics, biology, neuroscience, and physiology, but in many applications some equations of the dynamics are unknown and only a subset of the state variables are measured. We propose a hybrid neural--physics framework in which the known components of the ODE are kept explicit and the missing components are represented by a neural network. The proposed method consists of two stages where we alternate between state and parameter estimation and iterate until a predetermined criterion is met. Specifically, in the first step, we treat the model parameters as being known and we infer the latent states from the available measurements using a Rauch--Tung--Striebel (RTS) smoother. In the second stage, we treat the smoothed trajectories as being known and use them to estimate the neural networks' parameters through backpropagation. We evaluate the method on benchmark systems spanning linear, nonlinear, and stiff dynamics under partial state observation. Across these settings, the proposed method learns missing ODE components from incomplete measurements while exploiting and retaining interpretable mechanistic structure and improving latent-state reconstruction and long-horizon prediction.

</details>


### [78] [BadWAM: When World-Action Models Dream Right but Act Wrong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15207)
*Qi Li,Xingyi Yang,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BadWAM框架，揭示世界-动作模型（WAM）存在一种新型对抗攻击漏洞——世界-动作漂移攻击。通过微小视觉扰动破坏模型想象与执行间的对齐，包括直接驱动失败的动作攻击和保持未来预测但诱导错误动作的想象保持攻击，实验表明攻击显著降低任务成功率（如从96.5%降至43.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM通过学习联合动作生成与未来世界预测的表征，被认为是鲁棒、可解释且安全的。然而，本文质疑这一假设，并系统性地探索了WAM中想象与执行之间的对齐是否容易被攻击者利用。

Method: 提出BadWAM统一框架，定义世界-动作漂移攻击，依据攻击强度和隐蔽性两个标准，实例化两种攻击：1）动作攻击（仅破坏动作，最小化任务成功率）；2）想象保持攻击（在保持未来预测接近干净想象的同时诱导有害动作偏移）。

Result: 在多种WAM变体上的闭环执行评估中，动作攻击将任务成功率从96.5%降至43.1%；想象保持攻击表明适度的未来保持正则化能在保持攻击性能的同时减少未来想象漂移，暴露了WAM特有的脆弱性。

Conclusion: WAM中想象与执行之间的耦合并非天然鲁棒，存在可被利用的漏洞，需引起对WAM安全性的重新审视。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) are emerging as a promising foundation for embodied control: rather than predicting actions alone, they learn representations that couple action generation with future world prediction. This coupling is often viewed as a source of robustness, interpretability, and safety, as a robot's action can in principle be checked against its imagined future. In this paper, we show that this assumption is fragile. We introduce BadWAM, a unified framework for modeling and evaluating World-Action Drift Attacks: a new class of WAM-specific adversarial attacks that use small visual perturbations to break the alignment between what a WAM imagines and what it executes. BadWAM characterizes this attack surface along two natural criteria: attack strength and stealthiness. When the adversary prioritizes disruption, BadWAM instantiates an action-only adversarial attack, which directly drives the model toward task-failing actions. When the adversary additionally prioritizes stealth, BadWAM instantiates an imagination-preserving adversarial attack, which seeks to induce harmful action shifts while keeping the model's predicted future close to its clean imagination. Together, these two attacks capture a spectrum of WAM-specific failures: from overt action hijacking to stealthier cases where the model appears to imagine a plausible future but executes a desynchronized action. We evaluate BadWAM across different variants of WAMs. Results show that our attacks substantially reduce task success rates under closed-loop execution. For example, our action-only attack reduces the model performance from 96.5% to 43.1% success. The results of our imagination-preserving attack further exposes a WAM-specific vulnerability: moderate future-preserving regularization can maintain strong attack performance while reducing future imagination drift.

</details>


### [79] [Mutable Low-Rank Sketches for Retrain-Free Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15242)
*Hector J. Garcia,Nick Clayton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种可变草图（Mutable Sketches）方法，通过KP-tree（带求和聚合的稀疏线段树）存储用户偏好，并一次性拟合低秩投影，在新评分到达时即时重算嵌入向量，解决了二阶段推荐中的嵌入陈旧问题。在KuaiRec数据集上，该方法以1.8%的数据读取量达到0.810 RMSE（ALS为0.822需100%数据），批量更新速度快8倍，新用户首次评分后<1ms即可获得个性化推荐。KP-tree的范数比例采样在稀疏数据上相比均匀采样提升了40-130%的物品覆盖度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶段推荐系统中存在嵌入陈旧（embedding staleness）这一常见瓶颈：当用户对新物品进行评分时，其嵌入向量在下一个重训练周期之前保持不变，导致推荐质量下降。现有方法（如FunkSVD和eALS）无法提供预测误差包络单调收紧的理论保证。

Method: 提出可变草图（Mutable Sketches）方法，核心组件为KP-tree（带求和聚合的稀疏线段树），用于存储每个用户的偏好。该方法一次性拟合低秩投影，并在新评分到达时即时重算嵌入向量，避免了传统方法需等待重训练周期的问题。

Result: 在KuaiRec数据集上，可变草图以1.8%的数据读取量达到0.810 RMSE，而ALS需100%数据且RMSE为0.822；批量更新速度快8倍；新用户首次评分后<1ms即可获得个性化推荐，无需模型重训练。KP-tree的范数比例采样在稀疏数据（<1%密度）上相比均匀采样提升了40-130%的物品覆盖度。

Conclusion: 可变草图方法通过KP-tree和即时嵌入重算，有效解决了嵌入陈旧问题，提供了预测误差包络单调收紧的理论保证（定理1），在数据效率、更新速度和冷启动场景下显著优于现有方法。不同密度场景下的采样策略选择（稀疏数据用范数比例采样，稠密矩阵用均匀采样）为实际部署提供了指导。

Abstract: A common bottleneck in two-stage recommendation is embedding staleness: when a user rates a new item, their embedding remains fixed until the next retrain cycle. We propose mutable sketches, which store each user's preferences in a KP-tree (a sparse segment tree with sum aggregation), fit a low-rank projection once, and recompute embeddings on-the-fly as ratings arrive. We prove that each new observation monotonically tightens the prediction error envelope (Theorem 1), a guarantee that FunkSVD and eALS lack. On KuaiRec, the mutable sketch achieves 0.810 RMSE at 1.8% data read vs. ALS 0.822 at 100%, with 8x faster per-batch updates. A new user receives personalized recommendations in <1 ms after their first rating, with no model retraining required. A comparison of sampling strategies across density regimes shows that the KP-tree's norm-proportional sampling provides 40-130% better item coverage on sparse data (<1% density), while uniform sampling suffices on dense matrices.

</details>


### [80] [Decoding Market Emotion from Blockchain Activity: A Data-Driven Sentiment Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15258)
*Arthur G. Bubolz,Abreu Quevedo,Giancarlo Lucca,Rafael A. Berri,Eduardo Borges,Bruno L. Dalmazo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种结合链上数据、金融数据和社交媒体（Twitter）情感分类的新方法，用于分析比特币市场情绪。通过XGBoost模型（平均F1约0.84）和SHAP可解释性分析，验证了多源数据组合能提供有意义的情感预测信号，支持数据驱动的加密货币分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币作为去中心化数字资产和投资工具的使用日益增长，激发了对理解其市场行为的强烈兴趣。现有研究多集中于价格预测，而本研究旨在解释市场情绪，而非预测价格。

Method: 首先将区块链交易、比特币历史价格数据和每日Twitter情感分类相结合，将情感趋势与链上和金融指标归一化为数据集；然后使用多个机器学习模型进行交叉验证，其中XGBoost表现最佳；最后采用SHAP（基于博弈论的可解释性方法）量化链上特征对模型预测的贡献。

Result: XGBoost在情感分类任务中平均F1-score约为0.84，成为最可靠的模型。SHAP分析成功量化了链上特征的贡献，提升了模型透明度。

Conclusion: 链上数据、金融数据与社交媒体情感的组合能产生有意义的预测信号和洞察，支持数据驱动的加密货币分析，并为未来结合深度学习改进提供了基础。

Abstract: The growing use of Bitcoin as a decentralized digital asset and investment tool has sparked strong interest in understanding its market behavior. This study presents a new approach to analyze Bitcoin market sentiment by combining on-chain and financial data with social media posts. Unlike models that aim to predict prices, this work focuses on explaining market sentiment using blockchain transactions, historical price data of Bitcoin, and daily Twitter sentiment classifications. The method merges sentiment trends with on-chain and financial metrics, normalized into a dataset for detailed market analysis. Multiple machine learning models were tested using cross-validation, with Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) emerging as the most reliable model for classifying sentiment, achieving an average F1-score of about 0.84. SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations), a game theory-based method for model interpretability, was used to quantify the contribution of on-chain features to the model's predictions, improving transparency. The results indicate that this data combination yields meaningful predictive signals and insights, supporting data-driven cryptocurrency analysis and future improvements with deep learning.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [81] [Intelligent Three Level Learning Architecture for Autonomous UAV Swarms in Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14093)
*Oleksii Bychkov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于自主无人机群搜救任务的三级层次化学习架构，集成了Hebbian神经可塑性、多智能体强化学习与图神经网络、行为树、模型无关元学习与BDI推理及数字孪生三种不同的学习机制，通过22个架构契约提供六类形式化保证，并引入群元认知作为涌现属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在各层次使用单一学习范式，缺乏异构学习机制的有机整合；层次化强化学习方法存在五方面根本性局限，无法在无人机群搜救这一复杂场景中同时保证安全性、最优性、活性等多类形式化属性。

Method: 构建三级层次化学习架构：(1) 基于Hebbian神经可塑性的个体智能体自适应层（反射级）；(2) 基于MARL+GNN+行为树的战术协调层（技能级）；(3) 基于MAML+BDI推理+数字孪生的战略决策层（推理级）。通过22个架构契约形式化组织于六个组件中，给出六类形式化保证；定义五种SAR任务类型的构造性进展函数；提出群元认知作为组合涌现属性。

Result: 主集成定理表明所有契约满足时混合神经符号系统可保持全部六类保证；动态学习情形下新增5个契约及3项额外保证（认知韧性、优雅降级、单调元改进）；理论分析证明该架构克服了现有层次化RL的五项根本局限。

Conclusion: 通过多级异构学习机制与形式化契约的有机结合，实现了兼具形式化保证与认知柔性的自主无人机群搜救架构，为混合神经符号系统的可信设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel three level hierarchical learning architecture for autonomous UAV swarms performing search and rescue operations. Unlike conventional approaches that apply a single learning paradigm across all hierarchy levels, the proposed architecture integrates three qualitatively different learning mechanisms corresponding to the biological hierarchy of reflexes, skills, and reasoning such as Hebbian neuroplasticity for individual agent adaptation, multi agent reinforcement learning with graph neural networks and behavior trees for tactical coordination, and model agnostic meta learning with BDI reasoning and a digital twin for strategic decision making.
  The architecture is formalized through twenty two architectural contracts organized across six components such as BDI, Behavior Trees, GNN, MARL, Neuroplasticity, Meta Learning that collectively provide six classes of formal guarantees such as safety, budget correctness, optimality, liveness, starvation freedom, and inter level consistency.
  We introduce Swarm Meta Cognition as a compositional property arising from the structured interaction of all three levels, enabling the swarm to monitor its own cognitive state and switch between cognitive strategies. Five constructive progress functions for SAR task types bridge the gap between abstract optimization theory and concrete operational scenarios.
  The main integration theorem establishes that when all contracts are satisfied, the hybrid neuro-symbolic system preserves all six guarantee classes. For the dynamic case with active learning, five new contracts extend the framework with three additional guarantees such as cognitive resilience, graceful degradation, and monotonic meta improvement. Theoretical analysis demonstrates that the architecture addresses five fundamental limitations of existing hierarchical RL approaches.

</details>


### [82] [HG-RAG: Hierarchy-Guided Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Structured Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14095)
*Pranav Yadav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HG-RAG通过在层次化知识图谱上进行图遍历来检索结构化上下文，相比传统平面文档检索的RAG系统，在层次、关系和多跳推理任务上表现更优，同时减少幻觉并维持局部连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG系统从平面文档库检索上下文，难以处理需要层次或关系推理的查询。

Method: HG-RAG框架：从查询中解析命名实体锚点，然后通过父节点向上扩展、关系邻居横向扩展、子节点向下扩展上下文，实现层次化知识图谱的图遍历检索。

Result: 在三种规模（18-800节点）和四种查询类型上，HG-RAG在层次、关系和多跳推理任务上持续优于平面检索基线，同时减少幻觉并维持局部连贯性。

Conclusion: 层次化图遍历检索能有效增强RAG系统处理结构化知识推理的能力。

Abstract: Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) has proven to be a widely successful process at improving the quality of outputs from a Large Language Model (LLM) for wider context. However, RAG systems typically retrieve context from flat document stores, which struggles when queries require hierarchical or relational reasoning across structured knowledge. I present HG-RAG (Hierarchy-Guided RAG), a framework that performs graph-traversal over a hierarchical knowledge graph to deliver structured context to a language model. My retrieval pipeline resolves a named entity anchor from the query, then expands context upward through parent nodes, laterally through relational neighbors, and downward through child nodes when needed. I evaluate HG-RAG against a dense retrieval baseline across three world scales (18-800 nodes) with four query types: local fact, hierarchical, neighborhood, and multi-hop. Results show HG-RAG consistently outperforms the flat baseline on hierarchical, relational, and multi-hop reasoning tasks, while reducing hallucination and maintaining locality coherence.

</details>


### [83] [IMEX Interaction-Based Model Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14096)
*Emiliano Massi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IMEX是一种基于交互的模型解释方法，通过静态相关力(PCS)和交互相关力(PCI)两种互补指标，在不限制高阶交互分析的前提下，识别对目标预测贡献最大的变量及其交互模式。实验验证表明，在非线性、条件性和多重共线性关系下，IMEX能有效恢复相关特征结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑箱模型无法提供透明可解释的内部机制，导致即使预测准确也难以理解和验证。在关键场景中，仅凭预测精度不足以作为验证指标，需要能够解释模型决策背后原因的方法。

Method: IMEX框架基于两种互补指标：静态相关力(PCS)量化单个特征的贡献，交互相关力(PCI)捕捉特征间的非加性效应。该方法不限制高阶交互分析（允许超过两个特征的子集分析），通过构建预测的可解释性图来实现模型解释。

Result: 通过将PCS组件与INVASE方法在三个具有已知结构的合成数据集上进行实验对比，结果表明IMEX能够在输入特征与预测目标之间存在非线性、条件性和多重共线性关系时，有效恢复相关特征级结构。

Conclusion: IMEX方法提供了一种有效的可解释预测建模方向，能够识别重要特征及其交互模式，为解决黑箱模型的可解释性问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In predictive modeling, the ability to explain why a model produces a given target prediction has become increasingly important [5, 10]. Black-box models do not provide a transparent description of the internal mechanisms that generate the prediction, making even accurate predictions difficult to interpret and validate. In critical contexts, predictive accuracy alone is not a sufficient validation metric if the reasons underlying model decisions remain unexplained. The IMEX (Interaction-Based Model Explanation) approach represents a methodological direction within explainable predictive modeling. IMEX is designed to identify which variables contribute most to the target prediction and which interactions among variables are significant in determining the target. The method does not impose limitations on higher-order interaction analysis, allowing the investigation of feature subsets with cardinality greater than two. Beyond the identification of feature importance, IMEX enables the exploration of interaction patterns that may be consistent with latent mechanisms influencing the outcome. Through the application of the IMEX algorithm, it is possible to construct an interpretability map of the predictions. The IMEX framework is built on two complementary metrics: Static Correlation Power (PCS), which quantifies the contribution of individual features, and Interaction Correlation Power (PCI), which captures non-additive effects among features. In the present work, the PCS component is experimentally validated through a comparison with INVASE [18] on three synthetic datasets with known structures. The results indicate that IMEX can recover relevant feature-level structures in the presence of non-linear, conditional, and multicollinear relationships between input features and prediction targets.

</details>


### [84] [RegNetAgents: A Multi-Agent Framework for Cross-Network Regulatory Driver Identification in Cancer Genomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14097)
*Jose A. Bird*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RegNetAgents是一个面向AI的多智能体框架，用于在异构基因调控网络中进行结构化的、查询驱动的调控候选基因识别。该系统通过整合TCGA肿瘤网络和GREmLN单细胞调控网络，对目标基因进行双网络分类、癌症基因过滤和机制分配，并基于证据一致性对候选基因排序。在乳腺癌和结直肠癌实验中，该框架识别的候选调控因子显著富集OncoKB注释的癌症基因，且支持从候选识别到生物学假设生成的端到端解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基因调控网络分析方法缺乏统一的跨网络（bulk肿瘤与单细胞）整合框架，难以实现结构化、可查询的调控候选基因识别。需要一种能够融合不同来源调控网络、支持癌症基因过滤和机制分配的系统化方法。

Method: 采用多智能体LangGraph DAG工作流架构，集成TCGA-derived和GREmLN-derived ARACNe网络，通过双网络分类、OncoKB癌症基因过滤、作用机制分配，以及基于证据一致性（Both/TCGA-only/GREmLN-only）的排序策略实现调控候选基因识别。系统通过统一Python API和MCP客户端提供访问，作为预计算调控网络的下游分析层。

Result: 在11个乳腺癌和12个结直肠癌焦点基因上，TCGA-derived候选基因显著富集OncoKB癌症基因（BRCA: Z=6.69; COAD: Z=6.95），GREmLN-derived候选基因同样显著富集（BRCA: Z=5.51; COAD: Z=7.06，均p<0.0001）。对照实验（看家基因和非驱动基因集）未发现富集，验证了信号特异性。

Conclusion: RegNetAgents建立了一个可解释的AI框架，用于癌症基因组学中的跨网络调控候选基因识别，支持肿瘤发生潜力、药物可及性、临床相关性和网络脆弱性的结构化评估，实现从候选识别到生物学假设生成的完整分析流程。

Abstract: We introduce RegNetAgents, an AI-oriented multi-agent framework for structured, query-driven regulatory candidate identification across heterogeneous gene regulatory networks. The system enables unified analysis of bulk tumor and single-cell-derived ARACNe networks by integrating TCGA-derived cancer networks with large-scale single-cell regulatory networks from the GREmLN project. For a given focal gene, the framework performs dual-network classification, cancer gene filtering using OncoKB annotations, and mode-of-action (MoA) assignment for tumor-derived regulatory relationships. Candidates are ranked by evidence consistency across networks (Both, TCGA-only, GREmLN-only). The system is implemented as a multi-agent LangGraph DAG workflow, accessible through a unified Python API and Model Context Protocol (MCP) client, operating as a downstream analytical layer over precomputed regulatory networks rather than a network inference method. Across eleven breast cancer (BRCA) and twelve colorectal cancer (COAD) focal genes, RegNetAgents identifies candidate regulators significantly enriched for OncoKB-annotated cancer genes. TCGA-derived candidates show strong enrichment (Stouffer Z = 6.69 for BRCA and 6.95 for COAD), while GREmLN-derived candidates also demonstrate significant enrichment (Z = 5.51 for BRCA and 7.06 for COAD; all p < 0.0001). No enrichment is observed in housekeeping or non-driver control gene sets, supporting signal specificity. An extended module enables structured evaluation of oncogenic potential, druggability, clinical relevance, and network vulnerability, supporting end-to-end interpretation from candidate identification to biological hypothesis generation. RegNetAgents establishes an interpretable AI framework for cross-network regulatory candidate identification in cancer genomics.

</details>


### [85] [DialogueVPR: Towards Conversational Visual Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14115)
*Yukun Song,Changwei Wang,Xingtian Pei,Shibiao Xu,Wenhao Xu,Shunpeng Chen,Yu Zhang,Ke Zhang,Rongtao Xu,Xuxiang Feng,Pengyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出对话式地点识别（DlgPR），将传统静态的图文地理定位任务转变为交互式对话推理过程，并构建了首个大规模对话基准数据集DlgQuest-Cities，搭配DQ-pilot智能提问框架，在检索性能上显著超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言引导的地理定位方法采用静态单次检索范式，无法处理真实自然语言描述中的歧义性和信息不完整性，而人类沟通空间信息时是通过交互对话逐步明确的。

Method: 提出DlgPR范式，将定位建模为对话驱动的推理过程；构建DlgQuest-Cities大规模对话基准；设计DQ-pilot框架（跨模态多级检索器+智能提问器），通过课程学习（先在DQ-cities-20k SFT，再在DQ-cities-10k用GRPO强化学习）训练，引入DDI指标用于课程采样、PRG奖励直接衡量提问带来的检索增益。

Result: 基于推理的对话式方法在多个指标上显著优于传统基线方法。

Conclusion: 对话式推理检索范式能有效应对真实场景中自然语言描述的歧义与不完整性，为语言引导的地理定位提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Inspired by how humans communicate spatial information, language-guided geo-localization has gained significant traction for its intuitive and practical value. Despite this progress, most methods still rely on a static, one-shot retrieval paradigm, which fails to handle the ambiguity and incompleteness inherent in real-world natural language descriptions. We propose a paradigm shift to reasoning retrieval and introduce Dialogue Place Recognition (DlgPR), which casts localization as an interactive, dialogue-driven reasoning process. To support this new task, we present DlgQuest-Cities, the first large-scale dialogue-based benchmark for place recognition, and a unified reasoning framework that couples a cross-modal multi-level retriever with an intelligent questioner, DQ-pilot. DQ-pilot is trained in a curriculum: supervised fine-tuning on a curated DQ-cities-20k subset followed by reinforcement refinement on a harder DQ-cities-10k split via GRPO. Two task-aligned metrics guide learning: a Discriminative Difficulty Index (DDI) for curriculum sampling and a Positional Retrieval Gain (PRG) reward that directly measures retrieval improvement induced by a question. Experiments show this reasoning-based approach significantly outperforms baselines. The code and model are available at https://github.com/Graysonggg/DlgPR.

</details>


### [86] [Interpretable Language Model for Closed-Loop Type 1 Diabetes Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14126)
*Maya Sarkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-T1D方法，将强化学习（RL）与大型语言模型（LLM）结合，用于1型糖尿病胰岛素泵控制。通过将专家RL系统的知识蒸馏到微调后的LLaMA 3.1 8B和Qwen3 8B模型中，不仅超越了RL系统的性能（达到73.5%的时间在目标血糖范围内），还能用自然语言解释决策，并通过形式化安全验证防止幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1型糖尿病是危及生命的自身免疫疾病，基于强化学习的人工胰腺系统虽然有效，但其"黑箱"特性导致患者和医生难以完全信任，缺乏透明性和可解释性。

Method: 先训练一个专家RL系统，然后通过知识蒸馏将其策略迁移到微调后的LLaMA 3.1 8B和Qwen3 8B模型中，使LLM既能控制胰岛素输注，又能用通俗语言解释决策逻辑，并加入形式化安全验证防止LLM幻觉导致错误输出。

Result: 在FDA批准的UVA/Padova T1D模拟器上，LLM控制器实现了73.5%的时间在目标血糖范围内（Time in Range），性能超越原始RL系统，且通过了严格的形式化安全验证。

Conclusion: LLM-T1D成功融合了RL的精准控制能力和LLM的可解释性，为构建可信、透明、安全的AI医疗系统提供了可行路径，在糖尿病等关键医疗场景中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Type 1 Diabetes (T1D) is a chronic, life-threatening autoimmune condition characterized by the complete destruction of insulin-producing pancreatic beta cells. While Artificial Pancreas Systems (APS) powered by Reinforcement Learning (RL) have shown promise in automating insulin delivery, their ``black-box'' nature makes it hard for patients and doctors to trust them fully. This paper presents LLM-T1D, a promising approach that combines the precision of RL with the clear, human-like reasoning of Large Language Models (LLMs) to create a more transparent and reliable insulin pump controller. By training an expert RL system and distilling its knowledge into fine-tuned LLaMA 3.1 8B and Qwen3 8B models, we developed a controller that not only surpasses the RL system's performance but also explains its decisions in plain, understandable language. Tested on the FDA-approved UVA/Padova T1D simulator, the LLM controllers deliver excellent blood sugar control (73.5% Time in Range) while maintaining strict formal safety verification against hallucinations.

</details>


### [87] [Human AI Construction of Bayesian Networks for Operational Decision Support -- A Virtual Survey Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14141)
*Kumar Rahul,Shovan Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种利用大语言模型（LLM）作为AI专家面板来构建贝叶斯信念网络（BBN）的新方法，结合专家判断与数据驱动学习，并在替代医疗系统中客户咨询医生意图的案例中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BBN是不确定性下决策的强大工具，但其结构和参数估计困难；现有方法需在专家判断和大数据集学习之间二选一，缺乏有效融合两者的途径。

Method: 使用LLM构建AI智能体面板，根据特定角色和上下文估计概率；采用修剪均值规则去除噪声；开发六步BBN框架。

Result: 自我效能虽看似主要因素，实际因果影响很小；主观规范对客户意图的建模效果更强；同时提升信心和社群规范是最有效的策略。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的AI代理面板可以弥合专家判断与数据驱动学习之间的差距，为BBN构建提供新方法论。

Abstract: Bayesian Belief Networks (BBNs) are powerful tools for decision-making under uncertainty. However, building their structures and estimating parameters are difficult. Currently, researchers must choose between relying on expert judgement or using large datasets to learn the structure and parameters of the network. We propose a new methodology using Large Language Models to bridge the gap between expert opinion and data-driven learning. This approach uses a panel of AI agents to estimate probabilities based on specific personas and context. We then apply a trimmed-mean rule to remove noise from these responses. We develop a six step BBN framework and illustrate it to model customer intention to consult a doctor in an alternative healthcare system. The model reveals that while self efficacy appears to be a major factor, its actual causal impact is small. In contrast, subjective norms have a much stronger effect in modelling customers' intention. The most effective strategy is to improve both confidence and community norms simultaneously.

</details>


### [88] [Capability from Access Structure, Not Scale: Lower Bounds and Pre-Registered Tests for Hybrid Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14144)
*Wenhui Chen,Jianlin Chen,Ziyao Lin,Chi Man Vong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出能力收敛假说（CCH），挑战普拉顿表征假说（PRH）：在固定推理预算下，表征收敛不代表能力收敛。能力收敛于"存取完全混合"架构——同时具备压缩性O(1)状态通道和可扩展逐字索引通道。通过牛顿苹果问题作为见证任务，识别三道资源墙（香农墙、视界墙、电路墙），并证明混合架构能跨越所有三堵墙。预注册实验验证了剪刀差缺口、状态跟踪分叉等预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战PRH隐含的"表征收敛即能力收敛"假设，揭示在固定推理预算下表征与能力收敛之间的根本性脱耦，并阐明实现能力收敛所需的架构条件。

Method: 提出存取完全混合假设，将能力收敛归因于同时具备压缩状态通道和逐字索引通道的混合架构；以无限流中的牛顿苹果问题作为见证任务，从信息论和计算复杂性角度推导三道资源墙；通过预注册实验验证理论预测。

Result: 预注册实验证实：剪刀差缺口预测成立（64标量状态添加一层全局注意力后精确检索误差从0.994降至0.000）；状态跟踪分叉落在注册边界；联合见证显示不可约的双通道解；一项预测方向相反被如实报告。

Conclusion: 表征收敛由规模自由赋予，能力收敛必须通过存取结构来购买。能力在组成下严格超可加。存取完全原则基于信息论下界和预注册实验，而领域级收敛趋势仅是经济学动机的猜想。

Abstract: The Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) holds that as models scale, representations of heterogeneous networks converge toward a shared model of reality. We propose its sequel and boundary, the Capability Convergence Hypothesis (CCH): under a fixed per-token inference budget, representational convergence does not entail capability convergence. Capability instead converges toward a class, the access-complete hybrid: any architecture holding both a compressive O(1)-state channel and a scalable verbatim-index channel. We anchor it on a witness task, the Newton's-apple problem in an infinite stream, and name three resource walls: a Shannon wall barring any o(Nb)-state architecture, a horizon wall barring any fixed window, and a circuit wall barring fixed-depth attention-only composition (conditional on TC0 != NC1). Under an explicit separability assumption a hybrid crosses all three by paying each wall's price, so capability is strictly super-additive under composition. We separate what we prove from what we conjecture: the access-completeness principle rests on information-theoretic lower bounds and pre-registered experiments, while the field-level convergence trend is an economics-motivated conjecture. We report the first pre-registered small-scale tests under criteria frozen before the data: the predicted scissors gap is measured (exact-retrieval error 0.994 vs. 0.000 once a 64-scalar state gains one global-attention layer), the state-tracking bifurcation lands at the registered boundary, and a conjunction witness shows an irreducibly two-channel solution; one prediction failed with its direction reversed and is reported as such. Representational convergence is given freely by scale; capability convergence must be purchased by access structure.

</details>


### [89] [ToolAnchor: Anchoring Counterfactual Context to Boost Agentic Tool-use Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14145)
*Weiting Liu,Jieyi Bi,Wanqi Zhou,Jianfeng Feng,Yining Ma,Ai Han,Wenlian Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ToolAnchor框架，通过反事实锚点上下文干预打破智能体在工具集扩展时的行为惯性，使大语言模型智能体无需重新训练即可动态适应新工具，在GAIA、BrowseComp和VDR-Bench等任务上取得竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具增强型LLM智能体通常在固定工具集上训练，当任务需要新工具时难以有效整合，且重新训练不切实际。核心障碍是行为惯性——智能体倾向于使用熟悉工具和既有推理模式，忽视新工具。

Method: 提出ToolAnchor框架：(1) 教师模型在关键决策点注入反事实锚点上下文打破行为惯性；(2) 通过学生模型 rollout 验证反事实上下文的有效性；(3) 将成功的干预通过智能体后训练内化到模型中。

Result: 在通用AI助手(GAIA)、文本搜索(BrowseComp)和视觉搜索(VDR-Bench)任务上的广泛评估表明，ToolAnchor在扩展工具集下持续展现出竞争性性能。

Conclusion: 本文弥合了静态后训练与动态适应之间的鸿沟，为可扩展的智能体强化学习开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Tool-augmented large language model agents excel at long-horizon tasks, yet they are typically post-trained on fixed toolsets. When tasks demand new tools, these agents struggle to incorporate them effectively, and retraining from scratch is often impractical. We identify the core obstacle in such toolset expansion problem as behavioral inertia: the tendency of agents to fall back on familiar tools and established reasoning patterns despite having access to new ones. We demonstrate that injecting counterfactual anchor contexts at critical decision points can break this inertia, recovering failed trajectories by eliciting suppressed agent capabilities. To scale this insight, we propose ToolAnchor, a framework that uses teacher models to hypothesize these counterfactual contexts, verifies them via student rollouts, and internalizes the successful interventions through agentic post-training. Extensive evaluations across general AI assistant (GAIA), textual search (BrowseComp), and visual search (VDR-Bench) tasks demonstrate that ToolAnchor consistently exhibits competitive performance under expanded toolsets. Our work bridges the gap between static post-training and dynamic adaptation, charting a new path for scalable agentic reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [90] [Enhancing Small Language Models Reasoning through Knowledge Graph Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14149)
*Dimitrios Kelesis,Konstantinos Bougiatiotis,Georgios Paliouras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了神经符号智能体框架，通过两个工具调用（extract_facts和get_hint）增强小语言模型（Gemma 3 1B/4B、Llama 3.2 3B）在CLUTRR亲属关系推理任务上的表现。结果显示RGCN提示带来1.5-2倍性能提升，但受限于提取瓶颈和顺序推理脆弱性，且存在"分心效应"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽在零样本推理上表现优异，但部署成本高且环境负担重。小语言模型作为可持续替代方案，在复杂多跳逻辑推理任务上容易出错。作者旨在通过神经符号智能体框架增强SLM的推理能力。

Method: 将SLM转化为极简智能体，使用两个专用工具：extract_facts（符号三元组提取）和get_hint（通过RGCN提供专家推理提示）。在Oracle场景（真实三元组）和Realistic场景（自抽取知识）两种配置下评估Gemma 3 (1B, 4B)和Llama 3.2 (3B)在CLUTRR基准上的表现。

Result: RGCN提示相比仅故事基线带来1.5-2倍性能提升，但系统受限于提取瓶颈和顺序推理脆弱性（早期提取错误在多跳链中累积）。特定架构中出现"分心效应"——自生成的噪声事实即便有专家提示仍会降低性能。

Conclusion: 本研究刻画了低资源智能体系统中符号基础问题面临的挑战，为神经符号智能体流水线中的迭代验证提供了路线图。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) have set benchmarks for zero-shot reasoning, their deployment remains cost-prohibitive and environmentally taxing. Small Language Models (SLMs) offer a sustainable alternative, but prone to errors, on tasks requiring complex, multi-hop logical grounding. We investigate a neuro-symbolic agentic framework to enhance the reasoning capabilities of SLMs, specifically Gemma 3 (1B, 4B) and Llama 3.2 (3B), using the CLUTRR kinship benchmark. Our approach transforms the SLM into a minimalist agent utilizing two specialized tool calls: extract_facts for symbolic triplet extraction and get_hint for expert reasoning via a Relational Graph Convolutional Network (RGCN). We evaluate these models across two configurations, both in an Oracle scenario with ground-truth triplets and a Realistic scenario relying on self-extracted knowledge. Our results reveal that while RGCN-derived hints provide a 1.5 - 2x performance gain over story-only baselines, the system is constrained by the extraction bottleneck and sequential deductive fragility, where early extraction errors compound over multi-hop chains. Furthermore, we identify a "distraction effect" in specific architectures where noisy, self-generated facts degrade performance despite the presence of expert hints. This work characterizes the challenges of symbolic grounding in low-resource agentic systems and provides a roadmap for iterative verification in neuro-symbolic agentic pipelines.

</details>


### [91] [Orchestrating Power Grid Studies with Multi-Agent AI and MCP Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14158)
*Jérôme Picault,Clément Goubet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Agentic AI与模型上下文协议（MCP）如何支持输电系统运营商（TSO）环境下的电网研究，提出了基于MCP的工具集成方案pypowsybl-mcp，并讨论了人机协同的多智能体工作流及评估策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大语言模型与数值仿真工具、结构化工作流及人工监督相结合，以提升电网研究的交互性、可审计性和可扩展性，满足TSO对工业级智能辅助的需求。

Method: 提出pypowsybl-mcp作为MCP接口，将仿真工具pypowsybl的关键能力暴露给AI智能体；设计包含人机协同、多智能体工作流的架构原则；结合技术指标与实践反馈制定评估策略。

Result: 构建了一个测试平台，使智能体能够通过标准化工具调用完成仿真设置、分析执行、结果检索及与电力系统模拟器的交互。

Conclusion: MCP-based工具集成是实现更互动、可审计、可扩展的电网研究环境的重要一步。

Abstract: This position paper explores how Agentic AI and Model Context Protocol (MCP) can support power-grid studies in a Transmission System Operator (TSO) context. We focus on integrating Large Language Models with numerical simulation tools, structured workflows, and human supervision. We identify key industrial requirements for agent assisted grid studies and introduce pypowsybl-mcp, an MCP-based interface exposing selected capabilities of our simulation tool, pypowsybl to AI agents. This first step provides a testbed to study how agents can setup simulations, execute analyses, retrieve results, and interact with power-system simulators through standardized tool calls. We also discuss principles for human-in-the-loop, multi-agent workflows and outline an evaluation strategy combining technical metrics and practitioner feedback. The paper positions MCP-based tool integration as a step toward more interactive, auditable, and scalable grid-study environments.

</details>


### [92] [MemoHarness: Agent Harnesses That Learn from Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14159)
*Yue Huang,Wenjie Wang,Han Bao,Yuchen Ma,Xiaonan Luo,Yi Nian,Haomin Zhuang,Zheyuan Liu,Yue Zhao,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemoHarness是一种自适应智能体框架优化框架，通过分解六个可编辑控制维度、构建双层经验库并利用检索到的执行经验动态调整每个测试用例的框架，无需测试时标签或反馈，在shell智能体、代码生成和分析推理基准测试中优于固定框架，并展现出对未见套件和基础模型的选择性迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体框架（harness）设计对智能体行为影响显著，但大多数自动改进方法只优化提示词、流水线等工作流层面的狭窄工件，且部署的智能体通常对所有情况使用单一的全局框架，缺乏适应性。

Method: 将框架分解为六个可编辑的控制维度（上下文、工具、编排、记忆、解码、输出处理）；构建双层经验库（逐案例诊断+蒸馏后的全局模式）；通过检索相关经验自适应调整每个测试用例的框架，无需测试时标签、反馈或额外搜索。

Result: 在shell智能体、代码生成和分析推理基准测试上，MemoHarness优于所比较的固定框架；展现出对未见套件和基础模型的选择性迁移能力；当大量检索到的经验可缓存时，额外上下文开销具有成本竞争力。

Conclusion: 执行经验是构建比单一静态配置更具适应性的智能体框架的可行基础，但关于统计鲁棒性和组件归因的更广泛结论留待未来工作。

Abstract: An agent harness is the external control layer that turns a base LLM into an executable agent by managing context, tools, orchestration, memory, decoding, and output handling. While harness design strongly affects agent behavior, most automatic improvement methods optimize narrower artifacts such as prompts, pipelines, or workflows, and deployed agents usually reuse a single global harness for all cases. We introduce MemoHarness, an adaptive harness optimization framework that learns from its own executions. MemoHarness decomposes the harness into six editable control dimensions, stores per-case diagnoses and distilled global patterns in a dual-layer experience bank, and adapts the learned harness to each test case using retrieved experience without test-time labels, feedback, or additional search. In our evaluation across shell-agent, code-generation, and analytical-reasoning benchmarks, MemoHarness improves over the fixed harnesses we compare against and shows selective transfer to unseen suites and base models. Its additional context can also remain cost-competitive when much of the retrieved experience is cacheable. These results provide evidence that execution experience is a practical substrate for building agent harnesses that are more adaptive than a single static configuration, while leaving broader claims about statistical robustness and component attribution to future work.

</details>


### [93] [When a Verified World Model Still Loses: Play-Adequacy vs Prediction-Accuracy in LLM-Synthesized Code World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14169)
*Javier Aguilar Martín*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了用高过渡准确率（如100%）评估LLM合成的代码世界模型（CWM）是不充分的，因为模型可能遗漏关键动力学规则（<1%的错误）却通过采样校验，导致在规划中系统性失败。作者通过实验证明这种"验证-正确差距"遵循定量定律，且无法通过更多数据修复，并扩展到不完全信息CWM的信念推理函数，提出应在搜索分布上或通过直接游戏表现来评估世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常以LLM合成代码世界模型在采样轨迹上的高过渡准确率作为接受标准，但作者认为这种评估方式对规划任务而言是错误的，因为模型可能遗漏对规划至关重要的稀有但关键的动力学规则。

Method: 通过四个方面的实验和分析：（1）展示CWM通过采样校验（100%过渡准确率，≥98%状态准确率）但系统性输掉游戏；（2）推导危险定量定律danger=play_cost×(1−rarity)^N；（3）测试不同模型（GPT-5.x）和数据策略（包括DAgger）证明失败不可通过更多数据修复；（4）扩展到不完全信息CWM的信念推理函数，证明覆盖界并构建Beacon反例。

Result: CWM能在采样校验中达到100%过渡准确率但在实际游戏中系统性失败（play cost=0.091）；推导出定量定律；LLM合成表现为规则翻译而非规则推理，无法通过更多数据推断遗漏规则；不完全信息CWM中存在类似的验证但错误现象。

Conclusion: 面向规划的世界模型应该在搜索分布上评估或通过直接游戏表现衡量，而非基于采样轨迹的预测准确率。

Abstract: Large language models can synthesize a game's rules as executable code - a Code World Model (CWM) - which a classical planner then searches over. Such models are typically accepted when they reach high transition accuracy on sampled trajectories. We argue this is the wrong notion of adequacy for planning.
  We show four things. (1) An LLM-synthesized CWM can pass a sampling gate at 100% transition accuracy and be $\geq 98\%$ state-accurate on the planner's own search distribution, yet lose systematically at play, because the $<1\%$ it gets wrong is exactly the pivotal dynamics; the play cost of the omitted rule is $0.091$ (seed-clustered 95% CI $[0.065,0.117]$, $n=4800$). We call this the verified-vs-correct gap, and confirm it end-to-end through the synthesis pipeline. (2) The harm follows a quantitative law, $\mathrm{danger}=\mathrm{play\_cost}\times(1-\mathrm{rarity})^N$, whose $(1-\mathrm{rarity})^N$ gate-miss factor is proven exact and whose play cost is empirically bounded. (3) The failure is not repaired by more data: LLM synthesis behaves as rule translation, not rule inference, and did not infer the omitted rule across models (GPT-5.x) and data regimes (including DAgger and targeted examples). (4) The same mechanism recurs on the belief-inference function of imperfect-information CWMs: we prove a coverage bound (a size-$N$ gate is identifying when $N\gtrsim b^{d_{\max}}$), explaining why shallow games such as Kuhn poker show no gap, and hand-construct Beacon, a verified-but-wrong inference function that passes the gate yet loses every game.
  These results suggest adequacy for planning-oriented world models should be measured on the search distribution or by play directly, not by prediction accuracy on sampled transitions.

</details>


### [94] [ReasFlow: Assisting Reasoning-Centric Scientific Discovery in Applied Mathematics via a Knowledge-Based Multi-Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14178)
*Yutong He,Daibo Li,Guohong Li,Jiahe Geng,Zhengyang Huang,Can Ren,Zekun Zhang,Yifan Liu,Shuchen Zhu,Hengrui Zhang,Boao Kong,Ming Sun,Shu Li,Chenyi Li,Jiang Hu,Kun Yuan,Zaiwen Wen,Pingwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReasFlow是一个端到端自主智能体系统，专为以推理为中心的科学发现设计，通过内部验证循环和自动知识检索机制，能自主生成包含严格理论和实验内容的完整研究论文，在LLM评审中超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动研究系统主要关注经验驱动领域，缺乏对理论驱动发现（特别是需要严格证明和领域知识综合的数学基础学科）的支持，面临理论推理验证困难、自主前沿探索能力不足、文献中程序性启发缺乏等挑战。

Method: ReasFlow采用"首席研究员-研究生"协作范式，包含：(i)内部验证循环——在人工审查前审计逻辑一致性并纠正基础错误；(ii)自动知识检索与自我改进机制——主动获取陈述性事实和被忽视的程序性启发，减少专家干预。系统统一了文献综合、算法设计、定理证明、实验和稿件撰写。

Result: 部署后从最小提示自主生成五篇完整研究论文，包含严谨理论和实证内容，在LLM评审标准下持续获得最高评估分数，超越现有开源基线。

Conclusion: ReasFlow证明了面向推理中心的自主科研智能体的可行性，通过ReasLab平台公开提供，为AI辅助理论研究提供了协作工具。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models have fueled autonomous AI agents capable of tackling complex scientific tasks, yet existing automated research systems remain predominantly focused on empirically driven domains with quantitative benchmarks, leaving theory-driven discovery, particularly in mathematically grounded disciplines requiring rigorous proofs and synthesis of domain knowledge, largely underexplored. Key challenges include the difficulty of verifying theoretical reasoning at scale, insufficient reasoning ability for autonomous frontier exploration, and a scarcity of procedural heuristics in the literature. We introduce ReasFlow, an end-to-end autonomous agent system for reasoning-centric scientific discovery that operationalizes a collaborative paradigm where the human expert acts as Principal Investigator while the agent executes rigorous derivations as a capable graduate student. ReasFlow incorporates (i) a robust internal verification loop that audits logical coherence and corrects fundamental errors prior to human inspection, and (ii) an automated knowledge retrieval and self-improvement mechanism that proactively surfaces both declarative facts and overlooked procedural heuristics, substantially reducing expert intervention. The system unifies literature synthesis, algorithm design, theorem proving, experimentation, and manuscript preparation in a single system. Deployed to autonomously generate five complete research papers with rigorous theoretical and empirical content from minimal prompts, ReasFlow consistently achieves the highest evaluation scores among state-of-the-art open-access baselines under a curated LLM-based review rubric. ReasFlow is publicly accessible via the ReasLab platform, providing a collaborative workspace for AI-assisted theoretical research. Github repo: https://github.com/ReasLab/ReasFlow.git.

</details>


### [95] [RxBrain: Embodied Cognition Foundation Model with Joint Language-Visual Reasoning and Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14187)
*Haotian Liang,Mingkang Chen,Yufei Huang,Yuchun Guo,Xiaomeng Zhu,Xiangli Shi,Kaixuan Wang,Yunxuan Mao,Weijie Zhou,Ling Chen,Shirong Zeng,Yueyu Long,Yuchen Si,Yajuan Zhu,Xingyu Zhou,Minghui Wang,Wanjia He,Xin Yang,Lingzhu Xiang,Zhiqing Liu,Bohan Ma,Xiran Huang,Tianshuo Yang,Zhiheng Liu,Xuantang Xiong,Zisheng Lu,Ping Luo,Yao Mu,Han Hu,Zhengyou Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Hy-Embodied-RxBrain是一个具身认知基础模型，采用统一的Transformer架构实现语言与视觉想象的联合规划，将抽象语言规划与物理世界状态预测相结合，并提出了自动标注流程和评估基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身认知需要智能体将高层任务推理与需要达成的物理状态连接起来，而现有视觉-语言模型侧重于场景理解和文本决策，生成式世界模型主要预测未来视觉状态，缺乏将语言规划与视觉想象有机结合的能力。

Method: RxBrain采用统一的多模态Mixture-of-Transformers架构，支持语言、图像和视频的理解与生成；构建自动流水线将具身视频分解为规划步骤并与视觉状态转换对齐，生成联合文本-视觉规划监督；提出RxBrain-Bench评估基准。

Result: 实验表明RxBrain保持了具身理解与生成能力，能产生耦合文本推理、世界状态预测和联合子目标规划的规划方案；在连续机器人动作生成任务上展现出良好的实机表现，无需大规模动作数据预训练。

Conclusion: RxBrain为构建具身认知基础模型提供了初步探索，通过语言与视觉想象的互补协同实现了更完整的具身规划表征。

Abstract: Embodied cognition requires agents to connect high-level task reasoning with the physical states to be achieved. We introduce Hy-Embodied-RxBrain, an embodied cognition foundation model with joint language-visual reasoning and imagination. Unlike vision-language models that emphasize scene understanding and textual decision making, or generative world models that mainly predict future visual states, RxBrain represents embodied plans in a single planning sequence where language and visual imagination play complementary roles. Language provides the abstract structure of a plan, including task decomposition, planning primitives, constraints, temporal order, and decision logic, while visual imagination grounds this structure through world state prediction and joint subgoal planning, associating each planning step with intermediate and final physical states. RxBrain adopts a unified multimodal Mixture-of-Transformers architecture that supports language, image, and video understanding and generation within one model. To train this capability, we build an automatic pipeline that converts embodied videos into joint text-visual planning supervision by decomposing videos into planning steps and aligning them with visual state transitions. We further introduce RxBrain-Bench to evaluate whether models can represent embodied plans through joint textual and visual components rather than separate understanding or generation. Experiments show that RxBrain maintains embodied understanding and generation abilities, and produces plans with coupled textual reasoning, world state prediction, and joint subgoal planning. We also extend RxBrain to continuous robot action generation, where it shows promising real-robot performance without large-scale action-data pretraining. These results provide an initial step toward foundation models for embodied cognition.

</details>


### [96] [How Artificial Intelligence LLM Engines Shape the Global Conflict Information Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14197)
*Jason Miklian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究测试了五大AI问答引擎在28个冲突议题上的5,460条回答，发现冲突相关可检索记录越稀薄，引擎越容易产生幻觉、错误归因和错误计数。这种信息稀薄不仅助长幻觉，还使引擎易受生成式引擎优化（GEO）操控。通过对1,048个被AI引用网站的归因分析，发现GEO源优化已在发生，国家偏袒的数字捕获虽处初期但增长迅速。论文讨论了GEO信息战对学术研究的影响，并主张回归AI无法替代的深度本地监测和翻译研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI问答引擎在回答和平与冲突问题时是否存在可辨别的错误模式，以及这些模式如何反映全球冲突信息环境的变化，特别是信息稀薄程度与幻觉风险、生成式引擎优化（GEO）操控之间的关系。

Method: 向五大领先AI问答引擎提出关于28个冲突的系列问题，对5,460条回答进行基于文献证据的评分。同时，对AI大语言模型引用的1,048个网站进行归因分析，以检测GEO源优化的存在程度及国家偏袒数字捕获的发展趋势。

Result: 冲突相关的可检索记录越稀薄，AI引擎越频繁地产生幻觉、错误归因和错误计数。信息稀薄不仅助长幻觉，还创造了结构性暴露于错误信息和虚假信息的风险。GEO源优化已在发生，国家偏袒的数字捕获虽处初期但增长迅速。

Conclusion: 随着GEO信息战的兴起，AI引擎在冲突信息领域存在显著的结构性脆弱性。建议回归AI无法替代的深度本地监测和基于翻译的研究方法，以应对这一快速变化领域中的学术和政策挑战。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) answer engines now field a growing share of the questions that analysts, scholars, and the public ask about issues of peace and conflict. Large Language Models (LLMs) are known to hallucinate under certain conditions, but do these errors have discernible patterns when they are asked about conflicts, and if so what can that teach us about the changing global conflict information environment? To answer, we first asked a battery of questions about 28 conflicts to five leading answer engines and scored their 5,460 answers against documented evidence. We found that the thinner the retrievable record around a given conflict, the more the engines invent, misattribute, and miscount. Thin records don't just encourage hallucination, but create structural exposure to mis- and disinformation, because they are the easiest records to warp through Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) to bias engine responses. Through an analysis of 1,048 websites that the AI LLMs pulled conflict facts from, we found that GEO source optimization is already happening, and while state-partisan digital capture remains incipient it is rapidly growing. We explain what these findings mean for scholarship with the rise of GEO information warfare, and for policy argue for a return to the deep local monitoring and translation-based research that AI tools cannot replicate, closing with a discussion of future research opportunities and challenges in this fast-moving space.

</details>


### [97] [Align AI to Dynamic Human-AI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14240)
*Valerie Chen,Cleotilde Gonzalez,Anita Williams Woolley,Michael Lee,Tongshuang Wu,Vincent Conitzer,Aarti Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了当前AI对齐方法（基于静态偏好模拟）的局限性，提出应从静态模拟转向交互式互补对齐，通过跨学科视角（社会科学、决策科学）构建人机协同演化的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法依赖对人类偏好的静态表征，无法捕捉真实人机交互中偏好动态涌现、情境依赖的本质，难以实现真正的互补性对齐。

Method: 1) 形式化对比现有对齐与轨迹级视角（人类与模型行为随时间共演化）；2) 借鉴跨学科研讨会及社会科学中人类协作理论；3) 分析人机系统特有的新不对称性与协调挑战。

Result: 揭示了人机交互中偏好动态性、不确定性推理困难及协调问题，论证了现有ML公式无法捕捉交互动力学的根本缺陷。

Conclusion: 提出需融合机器学习与社会科学、决策科学，构建面向交互式对齐的研究议程，使AI系统在交互中与人类实现动态互补对齐。

Abstract: Current alignment approaches typically focus on emulating human behavior using static representations of human preferences, failing to capture the dynamic, context-dependent nature of real-world human-AI interactions. In this paper, we argue for a shift from static and emulative to interactive and complementary alignment, where preferences emerge through interaction and alignment is defined not by satisfying preferences alone. We first formalize this gap by contrasting existing alignment with a trajectory-level view in which human and model behavior co-evolve over time. Because these interaction dynamics have not been adequately captured within existing ML formulations, we ground this perspective in insights from an interdisciplinary workshop. We draw on lessons from social-science accounts of human-human collaboration and then argue that human-AI systems amplify these dynamics, introducing new asymmetries that make reasoning about uncertainty harder and introduce new coordination challenges. Based on these lessons and new challenges, we conclude by outlining a research agenda for developing AI systems that align with humans in interaction, requiring an interdisciplinary synthesis of machine learning and the social and decision sciences.

</details>


### [98] [The Steering Budget: Examples beat Knobs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14246)
*Raj Kumar Rajendran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 生成模型的属性调控存在一个“预算”上限（由训练数据决定），其中一部分可通过提示/引导等“旋钮”调节，而更大的一部分只能通过展示具体示例（而非调整旋钮）来达到。本文提供了一种通过审计训练数据来测量该预算的方法，并构建示例集以实现全覆盖，在图像和晶体结构生成两个领域验证了该方法的可达性和表现力优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示生成模型中属性调控的范围存在一个由训练数据预设的预算上限，并指出传统旋钮式调节（如提示、引导尺度）只能触及预算的一部分，而另一部分（通常更大）无法通过旋钮到达，从而提出需要新的调控范式。

Method: 通过审计训练数据来测量属性调控的预算（即训练数据中属性分布的边界），然后构建由模型已学内容组合而成的示例集（而非新增训练数据），用这些示例替代旋钮式调节来引导模型，从而覆盖整个预算范围。

Result: 在图像生成和晶体结构生成两个无关领域验证了：（1）旋钮方式只能触及预算的一部分，而示例方式可覆盖整个预算（可达性）；（2）示例方式能朝向难以用语言描述的复杂目标进行调控（表现力）。

Conclusion: 生成模型的属性调控存在由训练数据预设的预算上限，旋钮式调节只能触及其中一部分，而示例式调控可以突破这一限制，实现更全面的属性控制和更丰富的表达力。这一发现表明模型的能力瓶颈并非模型本身所致，而是源于训练数据的固有约束。

Abstract: Generative models are steered with knobs -- prompts, guidance scales, property tags. Turn one as hard as you like and, past a point, it stops moving the property you care about. We find that ceiling is not a shortcoming of the model but a budget, set by the training data before the model is trained: a property's movable range splits in two -- the part a knob can reach, and a second, significant part that only examples -- concrete instances of what you want more of -- can reach. That second part is usually much larger, but not always, and the same budget says so in advance.
  Reaching that second part takes a different move: instead of turning a knob, you show the model examples, composed from what it already learned rather than added to its training. A cheap audit of the training data measures the budget; we give a recipe for building the example set that reaches all of it.
  This buys two things a knob can't. Reach: it moves a property across the whole budget, not just the part a knob reaches. Expressiveness: it steers toward targets you can only specify by example -- including ones you can't put into words. We turn these into a handful of falsifiable claims and verify them in two unrelated domains, image and crystal-structure generation -- marking where a knob is enough, and where only examples will do.

</details>


### [99] [Automatic Hard Example Synthesis with Multi-Level Agentic Data Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14256)
*Genglin Liu,Muye Zhang,Krishnamurthy Viswanathan,Nichole J. Hansen,Blaž Bratanič,Nathan L Clement,Shalini Ghosh,Ariel Fuxman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个自动化、基于智能体系统的红队框架，通过多智能体架构（高推理能力的设计师Agent、高级图像生成器、多层级LLM评审委员会）迭代合成对抗性样本，无需人工干预即可发现政策边界案例和违规内容，并利用测试时检索将这些样本作为上下文示例，将目标模型的假阴性率从41.2%降至24.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在内容安全审核中仍易受对抗攻击和分布外边缘案例影响，传统主动学习和人工标注无法应对新型多模态威胁的复杂性和规模。

Method: 提出自动化红队框架：采用多智能体架构（高推理能力的Architect agent提出新假设和变异策略、高级图像生成器合成样本、多层级LLM评审委员会验证），迭代合成困难对抗样本，并通过测试时检索将这些样本作为上下文示例注入目标模型。

Result: 在公开图像安全基准上，将目标模型的假阴性率（FNR）从41.2%降至24.5%，且无需任何人工标注。

Conclusion: 该自动化框架能有效发现边界违规和模糊政策边缘案例，显著提升多模态模型对对抗攻击的鲁棒性，克服了传统方法在规模和复杂度上的局限。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed for nuanced content safety and moderation tasks, yet they remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks and out-of-distribution edge cases. Traditional active learning and manual annotation fail to scale against the complexity and volume of novel multimodal threats. In this paper, we propose an automated, agentic red-teaming framework that systematically synthesizes difficult examples using an iterative strategy that proposes novel hypotheses as well as mutating on past attempts. Leveraging a multi-agent architecture that consists of a high-reasoning Architect agent, an advanced image generator, and a multi-level verification committee of LLM raters, our system autonomously uncovers boundary-pushing violations and ambiguous policy edge cases without any human intervention. By employing these carefully synthesized adversarial examples as in-context demonstrations via test-time Retrieval, we substantially improve the target model's robustness, reducing the False Negative Rate (FNR) from 41.2% to 24.5% in a public image safety benchmark without relying on any human labeling.

</details>


### [100] [AI Agents Do Not Fail Alone:The Context Fails First](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14275)
*Fouad Bousetouane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了上下文工程质量是AI智能体可靠性的独立领先指标，通过ProofAgent-Harness开源框架对上下文进行七维度评分，实验证明上下文质量与行为表现（如幻觉抵抗、指令遵循等）存在一致因果关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文工程已成为构建可靠AI智能体的核心，但其质量缺乏系统度量。智能体失败并非孤立发生，而是由其上下文中积累的指令、工具、记忆、知识、护栏和不可信输入共同塑造。当上下文薄弱时，智能体会出现漂移、幻觉、工具误用、约束忽略、注入攻击和token浪费等问题。

Method: 实现ProofAgent-Harness开源评测基础架构，采用多陪审员、基于共识的评分机制，从角色清晰度、护栏覆盖、指令一致性、工具模式质量、接地充分性、注入加固和token效率七个维度评估上下文质量。通过控制实验，固定前沿LLM智能体不变，仅改变其操作上下文，验证上下文质量标准与相应行为结果间的因果关系。

Result: 上下文质量标准一致预测其对应的行为结果：接地充分性预测幻觉抵抗，护栏覆盖预测操控抵抗，指令一致性预测指令遵循，工具模式质量预测工具使用。上下文评分与行为指标和发布决策相隔离，实现了非循环验证。

Conclusion: 上下文测量可作为智能体可靠性的有效预检信号，将上下文工程定位为AI智能体评估与治理的可审计层。

Abstract: Context engineering has become central to building reliable AI agents, yet it remains largely unmeasured. Agents do not fail in isolation: their behavior is shaped by the instructions, tools, memory, retrieved knowledge, guardrails, and untrusted inputs accumulated in their context. When this context is weak, agents drift, hallucinate, misuse tools, ignore constraints, become vulnerable to injection, and waste tokens. This paper validates context-engineering quality as an independent leading indicator of agent reliability. We implement the measurement in ProofAgent-Harness, an open-source infrastructure for AI agent evaluation that uses multi-juror, consensus-based scoring. The harness assesses context across seven criteria: role clarity, guardrail coverage, instruction consistency, tool schema quality, grounding sufficiency, injection hardening, and token efficiency. Crucially, the context score is isolated from behavioral metrics and release decisions, enabling a non-circular validation. Through a controlled context-quality study across regulated agent domains, holding frontier LLM agents fixed and varying only their operating context, we show that context-quality criteria consistently predict their corresponding behavioral outcomes. Grounding sufficiency predicts hallucination resistance, guardrail coverage predicts manipulation resistance, instruction consistency predicts instruction following, and tool-schema quality predicts tool use. These findings establish context measurement as a validated preflight signal for agent reliability and position context engineering as an auditable layer of agent evaluation and governance.

</details>


### [101] [Measuring How Students Rely on Generative AI in Academic Writing: Development and Multi-Source Validation of the Generative AI Reliance Types Scale (GenAI-RTS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14301)
*Shahin Hossain,Tukhbita Afroz Nawmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发并验证了生成式AI依赖类型量表（GenAI-RTS），这是一个包含20个条目的工具，测量四种理论驱动的GenAI依赖类型：策略性、工具性、依赖性和对话性。通过对382名美国少数族裔服务机构的本科生调查和14名有目的抽样学生的访谈，确认了五因子结构（策略性依赖分为刻意使用和批判性评估两个子维度），量表具有良好的信度和跨性别、第一代大学生身份、STEM/非STEM专业的标量测量不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI在本科生学术写作中的日益普及，学生如何使用这些工具（而非简单的是否使用）已成为学习、学术诚信和教育公平的核心问题。现有依赖测量工具存在局限性：归纳式开发、局限于离散问题解决任务、样本同质化严重。

Method: 研究遵循《教育与心理测试标准》的多源框架，对382名美国少数族裔服务机构本科生进行问卷调查，并对14名有目的抽样学生进行访谈。采用验证性因子分析比较六种竞争模型，使用DWLS估计和Rasch分析评估量表结构、信度和响应格式，并检验跨群体的测量不变性。

Result: 五因子结构得到支持（CFI=.92, RMSEA=.08; DWLS CFI=.98, RMSEA=.07），策略性依赖包含刻意使用和批判性评估两个维度。子量表信度良好（omega=.75-.88）。标量测量不变性在性别、第一代大学生身份和STEM/非STEM专业间成立。五点评分格式优于原有格式。策略性依赖与AI素养正相关，不同依赖类型在写作过程和结果变量上存在区分。

Conclusion: GenAI-RTS为研究者和教育者提供了一个理论驱动、心理测量学验证的工具，可用于识别本科生的GenAI依赖特征，支持相关研究、评估和AI素养干预。

Abstract: As generative AI (GenAI) becomes increasingly embedded in undergraduate academic writing, how students rely on these tools, rather than simply whether they use them, has become a central question for learning, academic integrity, and educational equity. Existing measures of reliance were developed inductively, focused on discrete problem-solving tasks, and validated mainly with homogeneous samples. This study developed and validated the GenAI Reliance Types Scale (GenAI-RTS), a 20-item instrument measuring four theoretically derived types of GenAI reliance: Strategic, Instrumental, Dependent, and Dialogic. Validation followed the multisource framework of the Standards for Educational and Psychological Testing, drawing on a survey of 382 undergraduates at a U.S. Minority-Serving Institution and interviews with 14 purposively sampled students. Confirmatory factor analyses of six competing models supported a five-factor structure in which Strategic Reliance comprises two facets, Deliberate Use and Critical Evaluation, alongside Instrumental, Dependent, and Dialogic factors (CFI = .92, RMSEA = .08; DWLS CFI = .98, RMSEA = .07). Subscale reliability was acceptable to good (omega = .75-.88), and scalar measurement invariance held across gender, first-generation status, and STEM/non-STEM majors, to our knowledge the first such evidence for a GenAI reliance instrument. Rasch analysis indicated that a five-point response format would improve category functioning. Strategic reliance was positively associated with AI literacy, and the reliance types differentiated students across multiple writing process and outcome variables. The GenAI-RTS offers researchers and educators a theoretically grounded, psychometrically validated instrument for identifying undergraduate reliance profiles and supporting research, assessment, and AI literacy intervention.

</details>


### [102] [Tracing LLM Behavior to the Training Data with Empirical Next-Token Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14306)
*Zachary Izzo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM的输出分布与训练数据中经验下一个词分布（ENTD）之间的一致性，发现多数情况下二者高度吻合，且一致性随模型规模与训练计算量增加而提升，但存在长尾差异，并探讨了差异来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM的下一个词分布与其训练数据中的经验下一个词分布（ENTD）之间的关联程度，从而理解模型行为如何源于训练数据，推动"数据中心的机制可解释性"研究。

Method: 将LLM的下一个词分布与训练数据中给定上下文下的经验下一个词分布（ENTD）进行对比分析，评估二者一致性；考察模型规模、训练计算量对一致性的影响；分析差异来源（如Transformer架构、训练过程、有限样本噪声等）。

Result: 大部分输入下LLM的分布与ENTD几乎完全一致，且平均一致性随模型规模和训练计算量增大而提高；但存在长尾输入序列，二者存在显著差异。

Conclusion: LLM的预测分布与训练数据经验分布在多数情况下高度一致，但仍有长尾差异现象；呼吁更多关注"数据中心的机制可解释性"研究方向。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the connection between an LLM's output distribution and the data used to train it. Specifically, we study the degree to which an LLM's next-token distribution agrees with the empirical next-token distribution (ENTD) given the context in the training data. The ENTD is an appealing target because it is the unrestricted global minimizer of the next-token cross entropy loss used for pretraining, as well as an easily interpretable function of the pretraining corpus. We find that for a significant fraction of inputs, the LLM's distribution agrees with the ENTD almost perfectly, and the average agreement increases with model scale and training compute. Nevertheless, there is a long tail of input sequences where the LLM and ENTD differ significantly, and we examine several possible sources of this discrepancy across the transformer architecture, training procedure, and finite-sample noise in the ENTD estimate itself. More broadly, we hope our findings will encourage more work on ``data-centric mechanistic interpretability,'' a complement to standard mechanistic interpretability that opens the black box of how model behaviors arise from the data, rather than how they are encoded in the learned weights.

</details>


### [103] [Traccia: An OpenTelemetry-Based Governance Platform for AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14309)
*Nutan Kumar Naik,Aditya Kumar Saroj,Vijay Prasad Poudel,Saurav Samantray,Abhishek Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有LLM评估和AI代理监控平台存在安全漏洞，并提出基于OpenTelemetry构建的多层AI治理栈Traccia，通过遥测数据、语义护栏评估和执行溯源哈希账本，自动生成符合欧盟AI法案的可审计合规证据包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国际AI监管框架（如欧盟AI法案）对透明度和可问责性提出了严格要求，但现有的LLM评估、机器学习工作流和应用性能监控平台存在根本性缺陷，无法应对对齐漂移、SaaS安全风险和影子AI系统等威胁，理论与实践之间存在巨大鸿沟。

Method: 提出Traccia——基于OpenTelemetry基础设施构建的多层AI治理栈。通过集成遥测数据、被动语义护栏评估和执行溯源信息到哈希溯源账本中，自动生成合规证据包，并添加防篡改指纹和SHA-256内容哈希以映射到具体法规条款。

Result: Traccia在不侵犯数据隐私的前提下，能够自动创建符合欧盟AI法案（第12、14、19、26(6)和50条）要求的机器可读合规证据包，为自主AI系统的企业级管理提供了坚实基础。

Conclusion: 通过系统化评估，本文为自主AI系统的企业级治理建立了可靠的机器可读基础，填补了AI对齐的"最后一公里"问题。

Abstract: The rapid development of Large Language Models (LLMs) and Artificial Intelligent (AI) powered autonomous agents has fundamentally changed the existing forms of software governance. In spite of the rigorous standards of transparency and account ability required according to the international frameworks such as the European Union's AI Act, there is a considerable gap between theory and reality. The present study discusses the inherent drawbacks of currently utilized platforms for LLM evaluation, machine learning workflow, and application performance monitoring in general. It has been shown that current disjointed solutions fail to protect unbound state space agentic architecture from serious threats such as alignment drift, SaaS security concerns, and unauthorized deployment of shadow AI systems. Moreover, a solution is proposed for overcoming the discussed challenges in form of a coherent multi-level AI governance stack Traccia built on the top of OpenTelemetry infrastructure platform. Traccia resolves the last mile for AI Alignment by adding the telemetry data, passive semantic guardrail assessment, and execution lineage into a hashed trace ledger.
