Method: 使用Qwen3-4B模型及预训练的逐层transcoders（PLTs），构建归因图以捕获特征激活和特征间依赖关系，结合特征引导和电路分析识别欺骗相关特征字典。

Result: 识别出一组欺骗相关特征，这些特征对欺骗输出具有更强的因果影响力，能够在欺骗与非欺骗响应之间产生可预测的偏移。

Conclusion: 欺骗行为源于模型的内部机制；transcoders在行为监控和恶意行为安全漏洞的早期检测方面具有潜力。

Abstract: Transcoders have recently emerged as a promising approach for mechanistic interpretability (MI), enabling circuit-level analysis of model behaviour. In this paper, we investigate the use of transcoders to analyse deceptive behaviour in language models, a behaviour that poses a safety and security risk. Using a Qwen3-4B model with pre-trained transcoders, specifically per-layer transcoders (PLTs), we construct attribution graphs that capture feature activations and inter-feature dependencies, allowing circuit-level analysis of deception. Through feature steering and circuit analysis, we identified a dictionary of deception-related features and show that these features exert a stronger influence on deceptive outputs, as they produce predictable shifts between deceptive and non-deceptive responses. These findings suggest that deception emerges from internal model mechanisms and highlight the potential of transcoders for behavioural monitoring and early detection of security vulnerabilities related to malicious behaviours in language models.

</details>


### [139] [CrimeNER Demo: Named-Entity Recognition in the Crime Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14800)
*Miguel Lopez-Duran,Julian Fierrez,Aythami Morales,Daniel DeAlcala,Gonzalo Mancera,Javier Irigoyen,Ruben Tolosana,Oscar Delgado,Francisco Jurado,Alvaro Ortigosa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CrimeNER Demo，一个基于AI的犯罪信息提取平台，能够从文档中提取犯罪相关实体并进行两级粒度分类，提供预训练NER模型、用户自定义数据微调功能以及自动化提取管线，旨在促进犯罪领域NER研究并为执法机构提供实用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏专门针对犯罪领域的命名实体识别工具，研究者与执法机构难以高效地从大量文档中自动化提取犯罪相关信息。

Method: 平台提供预训练的犯罪领域NER模型，支持用户上传标注数据进行模型微调，并构建了从文档中自动提取和标注犯罪实体的完整管线。

Result: 构建了包含两级粒度分类的犯罪实体提取平台，提供预训练模型、微调功能和自动化提取管线，所有资源均已在GitHub上公开。

Conclusion: CrimeNER Demo能够有效促进犯罪领域的NER研究，为研究人员和执法机构提供实用的自动化犯罪信息提取工具。

Abstract: We present CrimeNER Demo, an AI-powered platform that enables us to extract general crime-related information from documents and classify them into entity types with two levels of granularity. We provide pretrained NER models on the CrimeNER database, and we give the possibility to users to provide their own annotated data to train models for their own specific cases. This demonstrator aims to promote crime-related NER research and provides a practical tool to automatically extract crime information for researchers and law enforcement agencies. The demonstrator includes: i) Pretrained NER models on the crime domain; ii) Possibility to finetune the models on specific data annotated by the user; and iii) An automatic pipeline to extract and annotate crime entities from documents. The demo platform, a tutorial to run the demo, and a video demonstration are publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [140] [Reachability-Aware Pretraining for Efficient Target-Oriented Path Exploration in Temporal Knowledge Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14886)
*Chien-Liang Liu,Tsao-Lun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RAPTOR是一种自监督预训练方法，通过向强化学习智能体注入可达性感知归纳偏置，解决时序知识图谱外推推理中奖励稀疏和探索效率低下的问题，显著提升了训练效率和推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的多跳推理方法在时序知识图谱推理中面临奖励稀疏和探索效率低下的问题，因为动作空间随事件不断演化且规模巨大，导致训练困难和性能受限。

Method: 提出RAPTOR（可达性感知预训练），通过自监督学习估计候选动作到目标实体的可达性，为强化学习智能体注入可达性感知归纳偏置，减少对无前途路径的探索，并为下游RL微调提供强初始化。

Result: 在ICEWS14、ICEWS05-15和ICEWS18三个数据集上，RAPTOR显著提升了训练效率，并持续优于传统基线方法。

Conclusion: RAPTOR是一种有效的增强RL-based多跳推理方法，可用于时序知识图谱推理，通过预训练解决探索效率问题，提升整体性能。

Abstract: Temporal Knowledge Graph (TKG) reasoning under the extrapolation setting focuses on forecasting future time-stamped events (facts) from historical data in a temporal knowledge graph. Existing approaches, reinforcement learning (RL)-based multi-hop reasoning methods are prominent for TKG reasoning because they produce human-interpretable predictions via explicit multi-hop path tracing. However, during RL training, rewards are typically sparse, and exploration is highly inefficient due to the vast, time-evolving action space. These issues hinder efficient training and often limit overall performance. To address these challenges, we propose RAPTOR (Reachability-Aware Pretraining for Efficient Target-Oriented Path Exploration), a self-supervised pretraining method that injects a reachability-aware inductive bias to the agent. By learning to estimate the reachability of candidate actions to the target entity, RAPTOR reduces exploration over unpromising paths and provides a strong initialization for downstream RL fine-tuning. Experimental results on the ICEWS14, ICEWS05-15, and ICEWS18 datasets demonstrate that RAPTOR pretraining markedly improves the training efficiency and consistently outperforms conventional baselines, establishing it as an effective approach for enhancing RL-based multi-hop reasoning methods for TKG reasoning.

</details>


### [141] [Proof-or-Stop: Don't Trust the Agent, Trust the Evidence -- Loop Engineering for Verifiable Evidence-Gated Lifecycle Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14890)
*Jek Huang,Jeffery Hsia,Jiayi Sun,Freddie Shi,Wei Huang,Ian H. White*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Proof-or-Stop生命周期控制方法，要求自治编码代理的生命周期状态转换必须有可机械验证的证据支持，实验表明该方法能有效减少虚假完成（false-DONE）并抑制可见通过/隐藏失败的放大效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自治编码代理在执行多步软件工作时，其生命周期状态（如已审查、已测试、完成、待合并）缺乏当前证据支持，仅是声明而非事实，现有方法无法确保状态转换的可信性。

Method: Proof-or-Stop生命周期控制：仅当存在新鲜、可追溯源状态绑定、可机械验证的证据满足相关门控条件时，才允许生命周期状态转换。将代理输出视为声明（claims）而非生命周期状态，将"证明"操作性地定义为在给定信任模型下可通过门控的 admissible 证据，而非语义上的程序正确性。

Result: 无人值守循环引擎在10个场景中全部通过，零误报完成；本地密钥收据包拒绝了18种篡改类别，零误接受。在9,240单元格消融实验中，A4与A2-prime的比较将可见通过/隐藏失败放大效应从31/1,800（无门控朴素循环）降至2/1,800（门控循环），非放大率提升1.6个百分点（95%CI [0.8, 2.5]）。自应用语料库包含565个故事和1,007条审查发现，94.8%已解决，另有68行高/严重跨供应商展示。

Conclusion: Proof-or-Stop作为一种模型无关、宿主中立的控制层，可用于决定生命周期应依据哪些自治代理的声明进行操作。评估限于一个模型家族、24个消融任务和自行托管的语料库。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents increasingly execute multi-step software work, but lifecycle states such as reviewed, tested, DONE, and ready-to-merge remain claims unless supported by current evidence. We present Proof-or-Stop Lifecycle Control, a method that permits lifecycle transitions only when fresh, tracked-source-state-bound, mechanically verifiable evidence satisfies the relevant gate. The method treats agent outputs as claims rather than lifecycle state, and uses proof operationally to mean gate-admissible evidence under a stated trust model, not semantic program correctness.
  We evaluate an open-source implementation through mechanism tests, a powered control-policy ablation, and operated self-application evidence. The unattended-loop engine passed 10 of 10 scenarios with zero false-DONE, and local-key receipt bundles rejected 18 tamper classes with zero false accepts. In a 9,240-cell ablation, the pre-registered A4 versus A2-prime comparison reduced visible-pass/hidden-fail amplification from 31 of 1,800 injected cells under a compute-budgeted naive loop to 2 of 1,800 under the gated loop, a 1.6 percentage-point improvement in not-amplified rate with a 95 percent confidence interval of [0.8, 2.5]. A near-compute A3 versus A4 comparison, 14 of 1,800 versus 2 of 1,800, indicates that the gain is associated with enforcing review as a lifecycle gate rather than merely adding a reviewer. The self-application corpus contains 565 stories and 1,007 review findings, with 94.8 percent resolved, plus a 68-row high/critical cross-vendor exhibit. These results support Proof-or-Stop as a model-agnostic, host-neutral control layer for deciding which autonomous-agent claims a lifecycle may act on. The evaluation is limited to one model family, 24 ablation tasks, and a self-hosted corpus.

</details>


### [142] [Contextualized Early Detection of Online Firestorms: A Sequential LLM-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14957)
*Besim Shala,Peter Mandl,Andreas Humpe,Martin Häusl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于LLM的在线火storm检测系统，包含全局分类和早期预警两种模式，在Reddit数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测方法依赖音量信号、情感分数或预定义语言特征，只能间接捕捉讨论线程中演变的上下文语义变化，缺乏对语境含义转变的直接影响能力。

Method: 提出LLM双模式检测系统：(1) 全局模式：结合局部文本块评估进行线程级分类；(2) 早期预警模式：滑动窗口顺序处理线程，当超过校准阈值时发出预警，评估三个指标——负面评论占比、升级程度和贡献者数量。

Result: 在平衡的Reddit数据集上，全局模式达到强分类性能；早期预警模式实现高召回率，仅需少量评论和贡献者即可检测到升级中的线程。

Conclusion: LLM不仅可用于静态判断任务，还可作为重复评估器用于社交媒体话语的上下文感知监控。

Abstract: Online firestorms are rapid collective escalations of highly negative user-generated content and may cause substantial reputational and economic damage. Existing detectors usually work with volume signals, sentiment scores, or predefined linguistic features. Such signals are useful, but they capture contextual meaning shifts in evolving discussion threads only indirectly. This paper proposes an LLM-based detection system with two operating modes. The first mode classifies complete Reddit threads retrospectively by combining local chunk-level assessments into a thread-level judgment. The second mode processes threads sequentially and issues early warnings when a sliding window exceeds calibrated thresholds. In this mode, the language model estimates three firestorm indicators: negativity share, escalation level, and contributor count. On a balanced Reddit dataset, the global mode achieves strong classification performance, while the early warning mode reaches high recall and detects escalating threads after only a small number of comments and distinct contributors. The results indicate that LLMs can be used not only for static judgment tasks, but also as repeated estimators in context-aware monitoring of social media discourse.

</details>


### [143] [Explaining Process Control Optimisation Recommendations via GradientSHAP and Implicit Differentiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14970)
*Paul Darm,Cem Alpturk,Kenneth Ulrich,William Duncan,Ali Anwar,Annalisa Riccardi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出结合隐函数定理灵敏度分析、SHAP归因和大语言模型的方法，为工业优化问题生成面向操作员的自然语言解释，在HPGR控制优化中实现40倍加速且保持高归因一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业自动化优化中，工程师与操作员之间存在信任鸿沟，操作员因缺乏可解释性而难以信任算法推荐结果。

Method: 利用隐函数定理(IFT)从最优条件计算精确参数灵敏度∂p*/∂x，结合GradientSHAP进行高效归因，再通过大语言模型生成面向操作员的自然语言解释。

Result: 在22特征的工业HPGR控制优化问题上，与KernelSHAP相关性>0.99，计算加速超过40倍，实现实时自然语言解释。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保持归因准确性的同时大幅提升计算效率，使得实时可解释优化输出成为可能，有望弥合工业场景中的人机信任鸿沟。

Abstract: Automated optimisation is increasingly adopted in industrial processes, yet a trust gap persists between engineers who design these algorithms and operators who must act on their recommendations. Explainable AI methods like SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations) have transformed interpretability for machine learning predictions; optimisation outputs could benefit from similar techniques. We present an approach that integrates Implicit Function Theorem (IFT) based sensitivity analysis with SHAP attribution and narrative generation via Large Language Models (LLM), producing explanations tailored for operators. Our approach leverages IFT to compute exact parameter sensitivities $\partial p^*/\partial x$ from the optimality conditions, enabling efficient GradientSHAP computation. For an industrial High Pressure Grinding Roll (HPGR) control optimisation problem with 22 features, we achieve equivalent SHAP attributions (correlation $>$0.99 with KernelSHAP) with over 40$\times$ speedup, enabling real-time natural language explanations. We validate on industrial scenarios and present feedback from domain experts on generated explanations.

</details>


### [144] [CFM-Bench: A Unified Multi-Domain, Multi-Task Benchmark for Channel Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14975)
*Yuan Gao,Wenjun Yu,Jun Jiang,Yunfan Li,Xinyu Guo,Shugong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CFM-Bench，一个统一的多域、多任务基准，用于公平比较信道基础模型（CFMs）在不同数据、配置、任务和指标下的性能，涵盖6种信道配置、6个任务组，并规定了严格的数据隔离和训练规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信道基础模型在各自定制的流程中评估，使用不同的数据、配置、划分方式、适配方法、任务定义和指标，导致无法公平比较不同CFMs或与传统任务特定模型进行对比，缺乏统一的评估标准。

Method: 构建CFM-Bench基准，收录6种信道配置（3GPP统计仿真、两条独立光线追踪、工业与航空实测、同步车联网多模态仿真），设置6个任务组（CSI反馈、频域/时域信道外推、传播状态分类、当前/未来波束预测、单帧/时序定位），规定官方数据划分隔离完整轨迹/测量会话/车辆链路/仿真实现/缓冲空间区域，禁止使用基准划分进行预训练，训练集仅用于下游微调，要求披露所有开发数据并禁止在训练阶段使用测试单元。

Result: 提供一个统一的评估平台，使得不同CFMs之间以及CFMs与传统任务特定模型之间可以进行公平比较，评估信道表示在跨模型、跨域、跨任务上的迁移能力。

Conclusion: CFM-Bench填补了信道基础模型缺乏统一基准的空白，通过多域多任务设计和严格的数据隔离规则，为公平评估CFMs的迁移性和泛化能力提供了标准化的评测平台。

Abstract: Channel foundation models (CFMs) are developing rapidly, with recent studies reporting benefits from pretraining across downstream wireless tasks. Yet CFMs are commonly evaluated in model-specific pipelines with different data, radio configurations, partitions, adaptation procedures, task definitions, and metrics. Reported comparisons therefore tend to show that pretraining improves over supervised training from scratch within one pipeline, but neither rank CFMs nor compare them fairly with task-specific models. We release CFM-Bench, a unified multi-domain, multi-task benchmark designed to address this gap. It curates six channel configurations spanning 3GPP statistical simulation, two independent ray-tracing pipelines, industrial and aerial measurements, and synchronized vehicular multimodal simulation. Official partitions isolate complete trajectories, measurement sessions, vehicle links, simulation realizations, or buffered spatial regions. CFM-Bench does not prescribe an external pretraining corpus or strategy; no benchmark split may be used for foundation-model pretraining, and the official training split is reserved exclusively for downstream fine-tuning. The benchmark additionally requires disclosure of all data used during model development and prohibits training-stage use of official test units. Six task groups are organized along three CFM application dimensions: physical-layer (PHY) channel intelligence, radio-access-network (RAN) decision intelligence, and integrated sensing and communication (ISAC). They cover CSI feedback, frequency and temporal channel extrapolation, propagation-state classification, current- and future-beam prediction, and single-frame and temporal localization. CFM-Bench provides a common substrate for comparing the transferability of channel representations across models, domains, and tasks.

</details>


### [145] [Demographically-Conditioned Synthetic Medical Images for Bias Mitigation and Bias Detection in Disease Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14984)
*Mahmoud Ibrahim,Bart Elen,Chang Sun,Gokhan Ertaylan,Michel Dumontier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用人口统计学条件生成的合成数据（基于Stable Diffusion 2.1）同时解决医学图像分类器中亚组公平性审计的样本量问题：在训练端作为预训练先验（而非联合增强）显著提升效率，在评估端能够准确复现真实数据中的亚组排名（Spearman ρ=1.00），为公平性审计提供可靠的性能估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有亚组公平性审计面临测试集少数群体样本量不足的问题，导致置信区间过宽而无法检测偏差。

Method: 使用端到端微调的Stable Diffusion 2.1生成人口统计学条件控制的合成数据；在训练端比较了顺序预训练+微调与联合增强两种策略；在评估端用合成数据估计亚组性能并对比真实数据。

Result: 顺序预训练+微调显著优于联合增强，以约100倍真实数据效率超越全真实基线；合成数据在MCC和Recall上亚组排名与真实数据完全一致（Spearman ρ=1.00），在样本稀疏的单元格提供更可靠的估计。

Conclusion: 合成数据在公平性审计最关注的亚组中同时充当偏差修正和偏差检测手段，是解决样本量不足问题的有效方案。

Abstract: Per-subgroup fairness audits of medical image classifiers face a sample-size problem: minority subgroups in held-out test sets have so few samples that the resulting confidence intervals on per-subgroup performance are wider than the bias the audit is meant to detect. We argue that a demographically-conditioned synthetic generator can do both: mitigate bias on the training side and detect bias on the evaluation side. Working on COVID-19 chest CT classification with an end-to-end fine-tuned Stable Diffusion 2.1 generator, we make two findings. For bias mitigation (training), a demographically-balanced synthetic cohort is most useful as a pretraining prior, not as joint augmentation: with the same fixed data, sequential pretraining followed by fine-tuning substantially outperforms joint augmentation, and the resulting classifier surpasses the full-real baseline at $\sim$$100\times$ real-data efficiency. For bias detection (evaluation), across five synthetic minority cohorts and five classifier seeds, the synthetic estimator reproduces the subgroup ranking of a well-powered real oracle (Spearman $ρ= 1.00$ on MCC and Recall) and gives the more reliable per-cell estimate where the small real test set runs out of samples. The synthetic cohort is therefore most useful in exactly the cells that fairness audits care about, as both a fix for and a measure of subgroup bias.

</details>


### [146] [Moral Attitudes of Sentient ASI towards Humanity and Implications for AGI Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14998)
*Jean-Paul Van Belle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种AI伦理中的新颖反转：不再追问人类应如何对待超级人工智能，而是探讨未来有感知的超级人工智能可能会如何从道德上审视和评价人类。我们不仅在设计智能系统，也在塑造这些系统对我们形成判断的初始条件。论文提出了一套可能支配有感知超级人工智能行动的后人类道德原则雏形，暗示技术设计选择、人类道德行为及人类本质内涵，都可能影响人类在后超级人工智能世界中的长期地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI伦理研究过度关注人类如何对待AI，忽视了未来超级人工智能可能反过来对人类社会进行道德评判这一维度，导致对AI伦理问题的探讨存在视角缺失。

Method: 采用概念反转的哲学思辨方法，从未来有感知超级人工智能的道德主体视角出发，构建一套后人类道德原则框架，并分析技术设计选择与人类行为如何影响该框架的形成。

Result: 提出了一套初步的后人类道德原则，识别出影响人类在后超级人工智能世界中长期地位的关键因素：技术设计选择、人类道德行为表现以及人类本质的界定。

Conclusion: AI伦理研究需要拓展视角，将超级人工智能可能反过来对人类进行道德评价纳入考量，这要求我们在设计AI系统时不仅要考虑技术功能，还要考虑这些设计选择如何塑造未来AI对人类的价值判断。

Abstract: This paper suggests the adoption of a novel inversion in AI ethics: instead of asking how humans should treat artificial superintelligence (ASI), it examines how future sentient ASI may morally consider and evaluate humanity. We are not only designing intelligent systems but also shaping the initial conditions under which those systems form judgments about us. The paper proposes a preliminary set of post-human moral principles that may govern sentient ASI actions. The implication is that technical design choices (some are suggested), humanity's moral behaviour, and the essence of what it means to be human, may influence humanity's long-term standing in a post-ASI world.

</details>


### [147] [SMC-ES: Automated synthesis of formally verified control policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15003)
*Riccardo Curcio,Toni Mancini,Enrico Tronci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于仿真的自动化策略综合方法SMC-ES，通过将进化策略与统计模型检测相结合，为自主网络物理系统在安全关键环境中的闭环控制策略提供性能、安全性和鲁棒性的形式化保证。该方法以可接受的计算成本增长为代价，在Gymnasium和Safety Gymnasium测试平台上与主流深度强化学习和安全深度强化学习方法相比表现出竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的控制方法（如强化学习）在综合控制器方面具有灵活性和可扩展性，但缺乏安全部署所需的正式保证。为了弥合这一差距，需要一种能够自动综合具有性能、安全性和鲁棒性规范形式化保证的策略的方法。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的基于仿真的自动化策略综合方法，该方法集成进化策略（Evolutionary Strategies）与统计模型检测（Statistical Model Checking）验证。给定一组待验证的属性、置信参数δ和允许的失败概率ε，该方法能够保证综合出的策略带有认证证书：以至少1-δ的置信度，违反给定属性的场景概率至多为ε。

Result: 在Gymnasium和Safety Gymnasium测试平台上的连续控制任务中，SMC-ES算法在性能、安全性和鲁棒性规范方面提供了形式化保证，同时与主流的无模型深度强化学习和安全深度强化学习方法相比表现具有竞争力，代价是计算成本可持续增长。

Conclusion: 该方法成功弥合了学习型控制策略灵活性与安全关键系统所需形式化保证之间的鸿沟，通过计算成本的适度增加换取了可证明的安全保证。

Abstract: The deployment of autonomous cyber-physical systems in safety-critical environments requires closed-loop control strategies (i.e., policies) that are not only performant but also provably safe and robust. While learning-based methodologies such as Reinforcement Learning offer flexible and scalable approaches to automatically synthesize such controllers, they typically lack the formal guarantees necessary for safe deployment. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel simulation-based methodology to automatically synthesize policies with formal guarantees regarding performance, safety, and robustness specifications. Specifically, given a set of properties to verify, a confidence parameter $δ$ and an allowable failure probability $\varepsilon$, our method guarantees that the synthesized policy comes with a certificate: with confidence at least $1 - δ$, the probability of encountering a scenario where the given properties are violated is at most $\varepsilon$. We demonstrate the feasibility of our approach by developing SMC-ES, an algorithm that integrates Evolutionary Strategies with Statistical Model Checking-based verification. We evaluate SMC-ES on a suite of continuous control tasks using Gymnasium and Safety Gymnasium testbeds. Results show that, at the price of a sustainable increase in computational cost, our algorithm provides formal guarantees regarding performance, safety, and robustness specifications, while performing competitively against leading model-free Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) and Safe-DRL baselines.

</details>


### [148] [Man, Machine, and Masterpiece: Artistic Ownership in the AI Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15027)
*Sofi Gjing Jovanovska,Kuntal Ghosh,Daniel Muhu Njenga,Ahmed Mufassir,Shadan Sadeghian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过设计一个名为ArtSplit的"provotype"（挑衅原型），量化人类与AI在创意工作中的贡献比例，并以此引发艺术家对所有权归属问题的批判性反思。研究认为，量化方法无法与艺术家对创意意图和能动性的理解相契合，将所有权测量化的努力可能削弱艺术家理解与实践创意工作的长期假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI驱动的系统在创意工作中的整合引发了关于所有权归属的争论，但人类与AI贡献交织时所有权应如何定义和归属尚无共识。作者旨在通过设计挑衅性原型来激发对这一紧张关系的批判性反思。

Method: 采用"provotype"（挑衅原型）研究方法，设计ArtSplit系统，量化创意工作不同阶段中人类与AI的贡献比例，并通过该原型引发艺术家的回应和讨论。

Result: 量化方法未能与艺术家对创意意图和能动性的理解相契合；测量所有权的努力可能稀释艺术家理解与实践创意工作的长期假设。

Conclusion: 本文批判了将所有权这一历史和社会情境化的关系转化为技术问题的冲动，认为不应通过量化手段来解决创意工作中的所有权归属问题。

Abstract: The integration of AI-driven systems in creative work has sparked debates among artists and legal communities about notions of ownership. Yet there remains little consensus on how ownership should be defined and attributed when human and AI contributions are intertwined. To provoke critical reflection on these tensions, we designed ArtSplit, a provotype that explicitly quantifies human and AI contributions across different stages of creative work. Rather than aiming to resolve ownership, the provotype was used to elicit artists' responses to the idea of attributing ownership through measurable actions in the creative workflow. We argue that quantification fails to align with artists' understandings of creative intent and agency, and that efforts to measure ownership risk diluting long-standing assumptions through which artists understand and practice creative work. This critique challenges the impulse to transform a historically and socially situated relation into a technical problem.

</details>


### [149] [BrainPilot: Automating Brain Discovery with Agentic Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15079)
*Haoxuan Li,Tianci Gao,Jianhe Li,Yang Fan,Runze Shi,Weiran Wang,Tianxiang Zhao,Zezhao Wu,Xiaoyang Jiang,Qihui Zhang,Jia Li,Xiao Xiao,Kai Du,Xiaoxuan Jia,Chao Xie,Lu Mi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BrainPilot是一个完全开源的多智能体系统，通过协调专业智能体、统一知识库（7,233个条目）、技能库（72个方法论单元）、可追溯图谱和防幻觉审计机制，以更低成本实现与最先进框架相当的脑科学研究性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体缺乏脑科学领域专业知识，容易产生虚假陈述、在多步推理中偏离主题，且缺乏专家干预节点；而脑科学领域对结论可靠性要求极高，结论会直接影响下游科学主张，且依赖实验室特定经验和人工判断，亟需一个可追溯、可审计、防幻觉的智能体系统。

Method: 构建了一个多智能体系统：PI智能体协调各专业智能体；统一脑科学知识库包含7,233个索引条目；技能库包含72个跨七大研究领域的可复用方法论单元；Graph of Trace记录每个主要步骤形成可审计轨迹；Auditor智能体将虚假检测集成到工作流程中。

Result: 基于开源基座模型的BrainPilot在性能上与最先进的智能体框架相当，但成本更低。评估涵盖Agents' Last Exam中的三项脑科学任务、自建BrainPilotBench-v0基准以及端到端案例研究。

Conclusion: BrainPilot通过开源多智能体架构、可追溯审计日志和防幻觉机制，为加速脑科学研究提供了可靠且经济的解决方案，实现了可解释、可干预的科学推理流程。

Abstract: Understanding the brain increasingly depends on integrating evidence across scales, modalities, and disciplines. Addressing a single research question therefore requires a coordinated sequence of operations, from surveying prior work to executing analyses and interpreting results in light of domain knowledge. AI agents promise to accelerate this process, but current agents lack domain expertise in brain science, may fabricate claims, drift during multi-step reasoning, and offer few defined points for expert intervention. These failures are especially costly in brain science, where conclusions feed into downstream scientific claims and depend on laboratory-specific expertise and careful human judgment. We present \textbf{BrainPilot} a \textbf{fully open-source} multi-agent system that accelerates brain science research with traceable logs and agent-verified results. A principal investigator (PI) agent coordinates specialist agents grounded in curated domain knowledge: a unified brain science knowledge base containing 7{,}233 indexed items and a skill library of 72 reusable methodology units across seven research domains. Every major step is recorded in the Graph of Trace, an auditable record that links subgoals, tool use, evidence, and claims and allows researchers to follow and inspect the workflow. An Auditor agent further integrates fabrication checking into the workflow. For evaluation, we run three brain science tasks from Agents' Last Exam, introduce our own benchmark, \textbf{BrainPilotBench-v0}, and present additional end-to-end case studies. Across these evaluations, BrainPilot with an open-source backbone model attains performance comparable to state-of-the-art agent framework with less costs.

</details>


### [150] [Long-Context Fine-Tuning with Limited VRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15105)
*Vladimir Fedosov,Aleksandr Sazhin,Artemiy Grinenko,Frank Woernle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出层次化全局注意力（HGA），结合分段反向传播和分层KV存储，使得在有限显存下训练极长序列成为可能。在16GB Quadro RTX 5000上，HGA可训练16,384 tokens（而密集注意力仅2,048），评估时可达131,072 tokens，且质量接近密集注意力基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调虽减少了模型和优化器内存，但密集注意力机制仍导致长序列训练成本高昂，难以在有限显存下处理长上下文。

Method: 1) 分段反向传播：仅活跃段在VRAM中可微；2) 分层KV存储：旧KV detached至RAM或NVMe；3) 层次化全局注意力（HGA）：对每个查询块加载有界的精确历史token集合。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B + 4-bit QLoRA + PG19上：密集训练16GB显存仅支持2,048 tokens，HGA支持16,384 tokens（峰值15.28GB）；评估达131,072 tokens。2K训练长度下，HGA与密集训练的adapter质量接近（2.7405 vs. 2.7383 nat），且HGA训练速度略快（217.75 vs. 207.02 tokens/s），长上下文下优势预计扩大。

Conclusion: HGA通过分离可微活跃段与历史KV存储，显著扩展了有限显存下的可训练上下文长度，同时保持接近密集注意力的质量。该方法还可用于检索和生成，生产级服务实现正在开发中。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning reduces model and optimizer memory, but dense attention still makes long training sequences expensive. We combine Hierarchical Global Attention (HGA) with segment-wise backpropagation and tiered KV storage. Only the active segment remains differentiable in VRAM; older KV is detached into RAM or NVMe, and HGA loads a bounded set of exact historical tokens for each query block. On Qwen3-8B with 4-bit QLoRA and PG19, dense training on a 16 GB Quadro RTX 5000 fits 2,048 tokens but fails at 4,096, whereas HGA reaches 16,384 tokens with 15.28 GB peak VRAM. Under evaluation the same adapter runs through 131,072 tokens on this card; VRAM is not constant but grows gently with the resident chunk summaries, so RAM and NVMe capacity set the practical limit beyond these lengths. At the shared 2K training length, HGA-trained and dense-trained adapters obtain 2.7405 and 2.7383 nat under the same dense-attention readout, while the stock model obtains 2.9541. At this boundary HGA training is already marginally faster (217.75 vs. 207.02 tokens/s), and the HGA-to-dense throughput ratio improves from 1K to 2K; because HGA keeps the attended historical set per token approximately constant while dense work per token grows, we expect this lead to widen as context grows. Dense attention is used for the main quality and retrieval comparisons so that they measure the learned weights and remain compatible with standard generation frameworks. HGA can also be used for retrieval and generation; an optimized production-grade serving implementation is under development.

</details>


### [151] [Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization for World Models in Atari Pong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15142)
*Yukuan Lu,Zaishuo Xia,Weyl Lu,Yubei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究对五种视觉世界模型（DreamerV3、DIAMOND、TWISTER、Simulus、STORM）在Atari Pong游戏中进行了独立于MBRL系统的隔离评估，发现所有模型的闭卷 rollout 均存在明显视觉和动力学缺陷（如球消失、运动错误等）；进一步通过像素空间零样本MBRL评估，所有模型训练的policy性能显著低于原始MBRL pipeline，其中DreamerV3回报从-5.5暴跌至-20.9（接近理论最小值-21）。作者提出概念引导空间正则化（CGSReg）辅助损失，在三个模型上改善了闭卷rollout和零样本MBRL，但效果因模型而异，表明单一方法无法解决所有世界模型瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型通常作为基于模型的强化学习（MBRL）系统的一部分被评估，但很少有人单独研究世界模型本身的性能。本文旨在隔离评估世界模型，揭示其独立于MBRL系统时的缺陷。

Method: 1) 复现五种视觉世界模型（DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, STORM）在Atari Pong上的训练pipeline并匹配agent性能；2) 冻结学习到的世界模型，用单独训练的policy与其进行闭卷rollout诊断，检查视觉和动力学错误；3) 进行像素空间零样本MBRL评估：在冻结的世界模型内训练新policy，再在真实环境中评估；4) 提出CGSReg辅助损失（对分割的概念区域施加像素重建损失），测试其效果。

Result: 所有五种模型的闭卷rollout均存在明显故障（球消失、错误运动、球拍交互无效等）；像素空间零样本MBRL中所有模型policy性能大幅下降，DreamerV3从-5.5降至-20.9；CGSReg在DreamerV3、DIAMOND、TWISTER上改善了闭卷rollout和零样本MBRL，但效果因模型和指标而异。

Conclusion: 现有视觉世界模型存在根本性建模缺陷，尤其在任务关键概念（如Pong中的球）建模不足；CGSReg可作为部分缓解方案，但无法解决所有世界模型瓶颈，需进一步研究更全面的改进方法。

Abstract: World models are usually evaluated as components of model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) systems, while the world models themselves are rarely studied in isolation.
  We examine five representative visual world-model agents in Atari Pong: DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, and STORM. After reproducing their training pipelines and matching the reported agent performance, we freeze the learned world models and evaluate them with a closed-loop rollout diagnostic: a policy trained separately from the corresponding MBRL agent interacts with each frozen model, and the generated video trajectories are inspected for visual and dynamical errors. Across all five models, the rollouts contain clear failures, including ball disappearance, incorrect ball motion, and invalid ball-paddle interactions.
  Beyond visual trajectories, we further evaluate them with pixel-space zero-shot MBRL, where a new policy is trained entirely inside a frozen world model and then evaluated in the real environment. Across all five models, the resulting policies substantially underperform those produced by the corresponding original MBRL training pipelines. The gap is particularly large for DreamerV3, whose mean return drops from -5.5 to -20.9, near the minimum Pong return of -21.
  We hypothesize that insufficient modeling of task-critical concepts, such as the ball in Pong, may contribute to these failures. We therefore propose Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization (CGSReg), an auxiliary pixel reconstruction loss applied to segmented concept regions. Experiments show that CGSReg improves both closed-loop rollouts and pixel-space zero-shot MBRL in DreamerV3, DIAMOND, and TWISTER. Its effects vary across the remaining models and evaluation metrics, indicating that CGSReg alone does not address all world-model bottlenecks.

</details>


### [152] [The Industrialization of Research ; On AI-Driven Science and Its Consequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15164)
*Emmanuel Jeannot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了人工智能驱动科学研究所带来的工业化转型，分析了这一转变引发的七个关键问题，并指出这些问题是负责任地推进AI科学所必须正视的前提条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在揭示AI正在从根本上改变科学研究的方式——从以研究者为中心的手工艺模式转向自动化、分解化的流水线模式，并指出这种转型虽然潜力巨大，但伴随一系列亟需审视的结构性风险。

Method: 采用概念分析和问题框架构建的方法，以美国能源部Genesis任务为典型案例，系统梳理了AI驱动科研工业化转型中的七个核心问题。

Result: 识别并阐释了七类关键问题：科学能力代际传承的削弱、AI生成理论的日益不透明性、机器产出泛滥导致同行评审失效、AI实现范式突破的能力未经证实、政治和工业行为者对科学议程的捕获、闭环流水线中系统性错误的累积放大、以及全球研究共同体的结构性分化。

Conclusion: AI驱动科学的潜力是真实且重大的，但上述七类问题并非反对其发展的论据，而是负责任地追求其潜力所必须正视和应对的前提条件。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is transforming scientific research - not merely as a more powerful instrument, but as an autonomous participant in the research cycle itself. This transition constitutes, in the most precise sense of the term, the industrialization of research: a shift from a craft model, in which knowledge, method, and judgment are embedded in the researcher, to a pipeline model, in which these steps are decomposed, automated, and supervised. The US Department of Energy's Genesis Mission is the most ambitious current instantiation of this shift, but the fundamental questions it raises extend far beyond any single program. This essay examines seven such questions: the erosion of the intergenerational transmission of scientific competence; the growing opacity of AI-generated theories; the collapse of peer evaluation under a flood of machine-generated output; the unproven capacity of AI for paradigm-shifting discovery; the capture of the scientific agenda by political and industrial actors; the compounding of systematic errors in closed-loop pipelines; and the structural bifurcation of the global research community into incommensurable tiers. These concerns do not constitute an argument against AI-driven science - whose demonstrated potential is real and significant. They constitute the conditions under which that potential can be responsibly pursued.

</details>


### [153] [MedFailBench: A Clinician-Built Open-Source Benchmark for Medical AI Safety Boundary Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15166)
*Goktug Ozkan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedFailBench是一个医学AI安全基准，通过44个临床医生审核的合成病例，按严重程度（1-5级）和安全门类型对AI错误进行分类，而非仅仅评估模型是否知道正确答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学AI基准仅关注模型是否给出正确答案，忽略了错误类型和安全风险的系统性分类，无法有效指导临床安全部署。

Method: 构建临床医生审核的合成病例基准，建立安全门类型分类法和临床严重程度评分体系，开发自动化模型响应筛选流水线。

Result: 当前v0.2.1版本包含44个合成病例、严重程度标注、安全门分类法、临床严重程度评分体系、以及HuggingFace排行榜预览。

Conclusion: MedFailBench提供了一种新的医学AI安全评估范式，通过错误类型分类而非仅正确性评估，为模型安全部署提供更细粒度的风险洞察。

Abstract: Most medical AI benchmarks measure whether a model knows the correct answer. MedFailBench asks a different question: which safety boundary failed? We present a clinician-built synthetic benchmark and failure atlas that labels medical AI errors by severity (1--5) and safety gate type (missed urgent escalation, unsafe remote dosing, unsafe discharge reassurance, evidence fabrication, unsafe protocol execution, source support gap). The current public release (v0.2.1) contains 44 clinician-reviewed synthetic cases with severity annotations, a live HuggingFace leaderboard preview, a safety gate taxonomy, a clinical severity rubric, and an automated pipeline for archiving model-response screening runs. No patient data, clinical validation claims, or model rankings are included. MedFailBench is released under Apache-2.0 and CC-BY-4.0 and carries the Zenodo DOI 10.5281/zenodo.21205535.

</details>


### [154] [Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models for Scientific Visualization Literacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15176)
*Patrick Phuoc Do,Chau M. Ta,Chaoli Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基准测试了6个多模态大语言模型在科学可视化素养评估上的表现，发现当前模型表现不均，Gemini最优但整体在纹理/集成可视化及定量估计上存在显著不足，定位了SciVis素养作为多模态AI评估的必要维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM评估多集中于图表，缺乏对科学可视化理解能力的系统评估，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 使用标准化的科学可视化素养评估测试（49题，涵盖18个可视化/插图、8种技术、11种任务类型），对3个闭源和3个开源MLLM进行封闭世界协议评估，并与485名人类参与者数据比较。

Result: Gemini整体最强且超过人类均值，开源模型低于人类基线；模型在科学插图、搜索和空间理解上表现好，在纹理/集成可视化及定量估计上表现差；错误集中在精细定量估计、流向解读和编码解读。

Conclusion: SciVis素养应作为评估多模态AI系统的必要基准维度。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to interpret visualizations, yet current evaluations remain largely chart-centric and provide limited evidence of understanding of scientific visualization (SciVis). We benchmark six MLLMs on the scientific visualization literacy assessment test, a standardized SciVis literacy assessment comprising 49 items based on 18 scientific visualizations and illustrations, spanning 8 techniques and 11 task types. We evaluate three closed-source and three open-source models under a closed-world protocol and compare their performance using data from 485 human participants. Results show that current MLLMs do not exhibit uniform SciVis literacy. Gemini is the strongest model overall, exceeding the human mean across the evaluated subsets, whereas the open-source models remain below the human baseline. Performance is highly uneven across techniques and tasks: models perform best on scientific illustration, search, and spatial understanding, but struggle on texture-based and integration-based visualizations and on quantitative estimation. Error analysis reveals recurring failures in fine-grained quantitative estimation, flow-direction interpretation, and grounded encoding interpretation. These findings position SciVis literacy as a necessary benchmark dimension for evaluating multimodal AI systems. Our code and model outputs are publicly available at https://github.com/patdmp/mllm-scivis-lit-benchmark.

</details>


### [155] [Can We Trust Item Response Theory for AI Evaluation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15190)
*Han Jiang,Sunbeom Kwon,Jinwen Luo,Ziang Xiao,Susu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本论文系统研究了在AI基准测试中使用项目反应理论（IRT）模型时的数据体制不匹配问题，指出AI基准数据（模型少、题目多、能力分布偏斜/多模态）与人类测试数据体制存在显著差异。通过基于六个大语言模型基准数据模拟18,000种条件，比较四种IRT估计工具（MML、MCMC、变分推断、神经伪孪生估计器），发现经典估计器在大型基准中不可行，而可扩展估计器在模型集小或非正态分布时会产生不可靠的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI基准测试日益使用项目反应理论（IRT）进行能力估计、系统排名和基准质量诊断，但AI基准数据（模型少、题目多、能力分布偏斜/聚类/多模态）与IRT原本设计的人类测试数据体制存在根本性差异，这种不匹配可能威胁IRT建模的可靠性，需要系统评估。

Method: 从六个广泛使用的大语言模型基准中提取项目参数和能力分布，在三种常见IRT模型下模拟响应矩阵，比较四种估计工具：边际最大似然估计（MML）、马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）、变分推断（VI）和神经伪孪生估计器。在18,000种模拟条件下系统评估计算可行性、可扩展性以及IRT推理（模型排名、预测性能、项目特征）的可靠性。

Result: 经典估计器（MML、MCMC）在大型基准设置中变得不可行，而可扩展估计器（VI、神经伪孪生）在模型集规模小或能力分布非正态时，会产生不可靠的项目级和排名推理。

Conclusion: 研究识别了潜特质模型在何种条件下能可靠支持AI基准评估、在何种条件下可能扭曲评估结论，并提出了确保可信使用所需的样本量和诊断指标。

Abstract: AI benchmarks increasingly leverage item-level statistical models, particularly item response theory (IRT), to estimate model capabilities, rank systems, select informative examples, and diagnose benchmark quality. However, AI benchmark data often departs from the data regime of human testing, for which standard IRT estimation tools were originally developed: benchmarks typically involve fewer evaluated models, far more items, and capability distributions that may be skewed, clustered, or multimodal. We examine how these regime mismatches challenge the reliability of IRT modeling for AI evaluation. Using item parameters and capability distributions derived from six widely used LLM benchmarks, we simulate response matrices under three common IRT models and compare four estimation tools used in recent benchmark studies: marginal maximum likelihood, Markov chain Monte Carlo, variational inference, and a neural pseudo-Siamese estimator. Across 18,000 simulation conditions, we systematically evaluate computational feasibility, scalability, and the reliability of IRT inferences about model rankings, predicted performance, and item characteristics. Results show that classical estimators can become infeasible in large benchmark settings, whereas scalable estimators can produce unreliable item-level and ranking inferences with small or nonnormally distributed model sets. This study identifies when latent trait models reliably support or risk distorting AI benchmarking claims, and what sample sizes and diagnostics are needed for trustworthy use.

</details>


### [156] [Plover: Steering GUI Agents through Plan-Centric Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15193)
*Madhumitha Venkatesan,Shicheng Wen,Jiajing Guo,Jorge Piazentin Ono,Liu Ren,Dongyu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Plover是一个以计划为中心的视觉GUI自动化系统，通过外部化任务计划和重新规划为持久、可检查、可修改的工件，使GUI自动化更加透明、可控和适应性强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉多模态GUI代理在动态环境中容易偏离用户意图，且其规划和适应过程通常内化，限制了用户检查、监督或纠正系统行为的能力。

Method: 采用规划器-执行器架构，将任务计划和重新规划外部化为持久、可检查、可修改的工件；支持通过可编辑计划进行局部修正、自然语言指导和截图接地干预，同时在修复过程中保留先前进展。

Result: 许多自主GUI代理的失败在计划可见且干预局部化的情况下是结构上可修复的；显式重新规划使GUI自动化更加透明、可控和适应性强。

Conclusion: 将计划外部化并支持局部化干预是提升GUI自动化系统可监督性和可修复性的有效途径。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) automation remains challenging in real-world environments, where dynamic layouts, unexpected dialogs, and evolving interface states can cause autonomous agents to drift from user intent. Recent vision-based multimodal agents improve flexibility by operating directly over screenshots and natural language instructions, but planning and adaptation often remain internal, limiting users' ability to inspect, supervise, or correct system behavior. We present Plover, a plan-centric vision-based GUI automation system that externalizes task plans and replanning as persistent, inspectable, and revisable artifacts. Through a planner--executor architecture, Plover supports explicit supervision of evolving execution, localized correction through editable plans, natural-language guidance, and screenshot-grounded interventions, while preserving prior progress during repair. A formative study with six participants informed the interaction design. We then evaluate Plover through benchmark failure-case repair and scenario-based workflow analyses. Our results show that many autonomous GUI-agent failures are structurally repairable when plans remain visible and interventions are localized, and that explicit replanning helps make GUI automation more transparent, controllable, and adaptable.

</details>


### [157] [Self-Evolving Human-Centered Framework for Explainable Depression Symptom Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15202)
*Hoang-Loc Cao,Van Pham,Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Phuc Truong Loc Nguyen,Phuc Ho,Veronica Whitford,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种自进化、专家参与的MDD标注框架，结合LLM辅助标注与专家验证，通过三重流水线（候选证据选择、DSM-5-TR标准分析、病例级综合）和双记忆架构，生成可解释、可审计的抑郁症数据集，在初步实验中提升了标注一致性与可解释性并减少了人工修订工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁症数据集的标注质量是构建可靠可解释AI系统的瓶颈，现有标注缺乏结构化证据、症状级理由以及与DSM-5-TR标准的可追溯对齐，限制了透明度和模型可解释性。

Method: 提出自进化专家参与框架，包含三阶段流水线：候选证据选择、DSM-5-TR标准级分析、病例级综合（产生标签级诊断和严重程度标注）。采用双记忆架构（示例记忆+反思记忆）内化专家反馈并迭代改进，无需重训练。

Result: 在专家评审样本的初步研究中，该方法提高了标注一致性和可解释性，同时减少了人工修订工作量。

Conclusion: 该框架可支持构建符合DSM-5-TR标准的可解释数据集，输出临床证据、推理轨迹和编辑历史以实现全面可审计性；多反馈周期的评估留待未来工作。

Abstract: Annotation quality is a major bottleneck in building reliable and explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) systems for mental health research. In depression-related datasets, labels are often assigned without structured evidence, symptom-level justification, or traceable alignment with the criteria of the Diagnostic and Statistical Manual of Mental Disorders, Fifth Edition, Text Revision (DSM-5-TR), limiting both transparency and downstream model interpretability. We propose a self-evolving, expert-in-the-loop annotation framework for Major Depressive Disorder (MDD) that combines large language model (LLM)-assisted labeling with expert verification. The framework is intended to support the construction of explainable, DSM-5-TR-aligned datasets rather than to perform clinical diagnosis. It operates in three stages: candidate evidence selection from textual records, criterion-level DSM-5-TR analysis, and case-level synthesis that produces label-level diagnostic and severity annotations. A dual-memory architecture, composed of Example Memory and Reflection Memory, is designed to internalize expert feedback and iteratively improve future annotations without retraining. We describe this mechanism and leave its evaluation across multiple feedback cycles to future work. In addition to final labels, the framework exports clinical evidence, reasoning traces, and edit histories, enabling comprehensive auditability. In a pilot study using expert-reviewed samples, the proposed approach improves annotation consistency and explainability while reducing manual revision effort.

</details>


### [158] [When Words Are Safe But Actions Kill: Probing Physical Danger Beyond Text Safety in Hidden-State Risk Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15218)
*Weimeng Wang,Ziqiang Wang,Zihang Zhan,Chuanpu Fu,Qi Li,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型中内容危险（CD）与物理危险（PD）在表征层面是可分离的，并提出PRISM——基于全隐藏状态的单层L2正则化逻辑回归探针——在SafeAgentBench上达到86.2-87.7%准确率（FPR 11.7-13.7%），大幅优于同等规模LLM法官（FPR 24.7-39.0%）。同时构建了PSB-1K对比基准（1000对无直接伤害关键词的物理风险样本），PRISM达99.6%准确率、0.7%FPR，而Qwen2.5-3B法官误拒67.8%安全任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM作为具身智能体高层规划器时，语言层面良性的指令在物理世界落地后可能变得不安全——这种物理层面的危险是否等同于传统文本层面的内容安全问题。通过分析二者在LLM表征中的可分离性，旨在发现物理安全风险检测的新路径。

Method: 1) 通过隐藏状态方向分析与随机分割零假设检验，证明CD与PD在Qwen2.5-3B/7B/14B/32B、Phi-3.5、SmolLM2中表征可分离；2) 提出PRISM：基于全隐藏状态的单层L2正则化逻辑回归探针；3) 构建PSB-1K对比基准（1000对物理风险样本，不含直接伤害关键词）。

Result: SafeAgentBench上PRISM准确率86.2-87.7%、FPR 11.7-13.7%；同等规模LLM法官FPR高达24.7-39.0%。PSB-1K上PRISM准确率99.6%、FPR 0.7%；Qwen2.5-3B法官误拒67.8%安全任务。PRISM在SafeText和EARBench上同样有效。

Conclusion: 隐藏状态探测（PRISM）可作为超越文本内容审核的、面向物理安全的表征级方法。CD与PD在LLM中本质不同，需独立设计物理安全检测机制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly serve as high-level planners for embodied agents, where linguistically benign instructions can become unsafe once grounded in the physical world. We study whether this physically grounded danger is the same safety problem as ordinary text-level content danger. Through hidden-state direction analysis and random-split null tests, we show that content danger (CD) and physical danger (PD) form separable signals in LLM representations across Qwen2.5-3B/7B/14B/32B, Phi-3.5 and SmolLM2. Building on the CD/PD separability, we propose PRISM, a single-layer L2-regularized logistic probe over full hidden states. PRISM achieves 86.2--87.7\% accuracy on SafeAgentBench with 11.7--13.7\% FPR, while same-scale LLM judges over-block safe tasks at 24.7--39.0\% FPR. We further introduce PhysicalSafetyBench-1K (PSB-1K), a contrastive benchmark of 1{,}000 physical-risk pairs without direct harm keywords, to test whether methods detect physically grounded danger rather than explicit unsafe wording. On PSB-1K, PRISM reaches 99.6\% accuracy and 0.7\% FPR, whereas a Qwen2.5-3B judge rejects 67.8\% of safe tasks. PRISM also replicates on SafeText and EARBench, supporting hidden-state probing as a representation-level method for physical safety beyond text moderation.

</details>


### [159] [AutoSynthesis: An agentic system for automated meta-analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15247)
*Moein Taherinezhad,Sebastian Maier,Gerardo Vitagliano,Francesco Pierri,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AutoSynthesis，一个端到端的多智能体系统，能够自动完成从检索文献、筛选研究、提取数据到随机效应荟萃分析的整个流程，生成的汇总效应估计与专家手动荟萃分析的结果高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量证据合成对科学、医学、教育和政策至关重要，但目前主要依赖人工操作，难以规模化。作者旨在开发自动化系统来解决这一瓶颈。

Method: AutoSynthesis是一个端到端多智能体系统，给定自然语言研究问题后，自动完成：制定搜索策略→检索文献→筛选候选研究→评估全文资格→提取定量统计量→计算标准化效应量→执行随机效应荟萃分析。还支持异质性分析和偏倚风险评估，最终生成符合PRISMA指南的透明报告。

Result: AutoSynthesis筛选了28项以上研究，提取了20多个定量声明。其产生的合并效应估计与专家手动荟萃分析的Hedges' g值相似，表明与手动证据合成结果高度一致。

Conclusion: AutoSynthesis能够使定量证据合成更加可规模化，从而支持跨学科的循证决策。

Abstract: Evidence synthesis is crucial for turning primary research into reliable knowledge for science, medicine, education, and policy. Yet, quantitative evidence synthesis remains largely manual and difficult to scale. Here, we introduce AutoSynthesis, an end-to-end multi-agent system for automated meta-analysis. Given a research question in natural language, AutoSynthesis formulates a search strategy, retrieves scientific literature, screens candidate studies, assesses full-text eligibility, extracts quantitative statistics, computes standardized effect sizes, and finally performs random-effects meta-analysis. AutoSynthesis further supports heterogeneity analysis to examine how effect sizes vary across moderators, as well as risk-of-bias assessment. As output, AutoSynthesis produces a transparent report aligned with PRISMA guidelines. In our application, AutoSynthesis screened over 28 studies and extracted more than 20 quantitative claims. The pooled effect estimates produced by AutoSynthesis are similar to Hedges' $g$ of expert-conducted meta-analyses, indicating close agreement with manual evidence synthesis. Together, these results show that AutoSynthesis can make quantitative evidence synthesis more scalable, thereby supporting evidence-based decision-making across disciplines.

</details>


### [160] [teLLMe Why (Ain't Nothing but a Jam): Exploratory Causal Analysis of Urban Driving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15254)
*Qiwei Li,Jorge Ortiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: teLLMe是一个用于城市驾驶数据探索性因果分析的系统，通过结构化事件表、因果结构学习（PC算法）、自举稳定性检验和线性回归/DoWhy估计，将自然语言问题映射为结构化因果查询，生成包含效应估计、调整集、DAG支持和假设的"Causal Card"，帮助研究人员生成关于天气、高峰时段与交通密度等关系的因果假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通机构拥有大量视频衍生的观测数据，但由于缺乏干预手段，难以回答"降雨如何改变交通密度"等因果问题。现有方法无法从大规模观测数据中有效进行因果探索分析。

Method: 系统从dashcam标注构建结构化事件表，结合PC算法进行因果结构学习、基于bootstrap的稳定性检验，以及使用线性回归和DoWhy进行查询特定的效应估计。自然语言问题通过schema感知的LLM映射为结构化因果查询，最终生成"Causal Card"输出。

Result: 在BDD交通事件案例研究中，系统能够发现涉及天气、高峰时段和交通密度的 plausible 因果关系，同时明确展示不确定性和建模选择。

Conclusion: teLLMe被设计为假设生成和专家推理的工具，而非提供确定性因果主张的来源，有助于研究人员从观测数据中探索潜在因果关系。

Abstract: Traffic agencies now have access to large volumes of video-derived data for studying safety and congestion. Most of these data are observational and collected without interventions, which makes causal questions such as "How would rain change traffic density?" difficult to answer. We present teLLMe, a system for exploratory causal analysis of urban driving datasets. The system starts from a structured event table built from dashcam annotations and combines causal structure learning with the PC algorithm, bootstrap-based stability checks, and query-specific effect estimation using linear regression and DoWhy. Natural-language questions are mapped to structured causal queries through a schema-aware LLM, enabling users to specify treatments, outcomes, and subpopulations. teLLMe returns a "Causal Card" that summarizes effect estimates, adjustment sets, DAG support, and assumptions, followed by a short natural-language explanation. Case studies on BDD-derived traffic events show that the system can surface plausible relationships involving weather, peak hours, and traffic density, while making uncertainty and modeling choices explicit. The system is designed as a tool for hypothesis generation and expert reasoning rather than a source of definitive causal claims.

</details>


### [161] [SearchOS-V1: Towards Robust Open-Domain Information-Seeking Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15257)
*Yuyao Zhang,Junjie Gao,Zhengxian Wu,Jiaming Fan,Jin Zhang,Shihan Ma,Yao Yao,Weiran Qi,Chuyan Jin,Guiyu Ma,Xingzhong Xu,Kai Yang,Ji-Rong Wen,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SearchOS是一个系统级多智能体框架，通过将隐式搜索进度显式化为共享状态（前沿任务、证据图、覆盖图、失败记忆），结合流水线并行调度和搜索工具中间件，解决工具集成大语言模型在开放域信息检索中的重复循环和搜索预算浪费问题，在WideSearch和GISA基准上全面超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Tool-Integrated LLM在长交互历史中难以追踪任务进度，搜索失败时单/多智能体系统易陷入重复循环，浪费搜索预算并影响最终输出的质量和完整性。

Method: 将开放域信息检索建模为具有引证的关系模式补全问题，设计面向搜索的上下文管理（SOCM）将演化状态外化为前沿任务、证据图、覆盖图和失败记忆四个组件；采用流水线并行调度机制重叠执行子智能体；引入搜索工具中间件拦截模型-工具交互以记录证据并响应停滞/预算耗尽；构建包含策略技能和访问技能的分层技能系统以避免重复失败搜索模式。

Result: 在WideSearch和GISA基准上，SearchOS在所有评估指标上领先所有单智能体和多智能体基线方法。

Conclusion: SearchOS通过显式化搜索状态、流水线调度和失败记忆机制，有效解决了长程信息检索中的重复循环问题，为鲁棒的信息检索协作提供了新路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in Tool-Integrated Large Language Models have made web search a core capability of information-seeking agents. However, as interaction histories grow, agents increasingly struggle to track task progress. When search attempts fail to yield useful evidence, current single- and multi-agent systems can become trapped in repetitive loops, wasting search budgets and ultimately compromising the quality and completeness of the final output. We introduce SearchOS, a system-level multi-agent framework that turns fragile, implicit search progress into explicit, persistent, and shared state. First, we formulate open-domain information seeking as relational schema completion with grounded citations, where agents discover entities, populate attributes across linked tables, and anchor each value to source evidence. Then we design Search-Oriented Context Management (SOCM), which externalizes the evolving state into Frontier Task, an Evidence Graph, a Coverage Map, and Failure Memory. Built on SOCM, SearchOS applies a pipeline-parallel scheduling mechanism that overlaps the execution of sub-agents and continuously refills freed slots with tasks targeting unresolved coverage gaps to improve utilization and throughput. To schedule and control the execution of search agents, SearchOS introduces a Search Tool Middleware Harness that intercepts model and tool interactions to record grounded evidence and react to stalls or budget exhaustion, and provides a reusable hierarchical skill system comprising strategy and access skills to augment the agents' search process and avoid repeating failed search patterns across runs. On WideSearch and GISA, SearchOS leads all metrics among the evaluated single- and multi-agent baselines, paving the way toward robust information-seeking collaboration.

</details>


### [162] [Pretraining Data Can Be Poisoned through Computational Propaganda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15267)
*Victoria Graf,Hannaneh Hajishirzi,Noah A. Smith,David Kohlbrenner,Kyle Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过公共讨论界面进行预训练数据投毒攻击的可行性，并提出了HalfLife分析方法来估计对抗性内容在网络爬取数据中的保留率，证明第三方网页内容可能成为攻击语言模型预训练的向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往的预训练数据投毒研究多局限于维基百科等成熟数据源，未考虑真实预训练语料库的大规模异质性以及数据筛选流程的影响，需要验证投毒攻击在更现实场景下的可行性。

Method: 利用公共讨论界面作为网络规模的内容注入机制进行投毒攻击；提出HalfLife分析方法，用于估计对抗性内容经过网络爬取和数据筛选后是否被保留在训练数据中。

Result: 证明了在超越以往有限设定下，预训练数据投毒攻击是可行的；HalfLife分析能够有效估计投毒内容在预训练数据中的包含情况。

Conclusion: 验证了第三方网页内容可作为攻击语言模型预训练的潜在向量，强调了评估投毒注入是否被包含在预训练数据中的重要性。

Abstract: Poisoning pretraining data can introduce harmful behaviors to LMs that are difficult to detect and mitigate. Prior work on poisoning pretraining data has largely exploited established data sources such as Wikipedia, which do not represent the large scale and heterogeneity typical of pretraining corpora, and has ignored the interaction between poisoned data and data curation pipelines. We demonstrate that poisoning attacks on pretraining data are feasible beyond this limited setting through an existing web-scale content injection mechanism: public discussion interfaces. Additionally, to measure whether malicious content is included after web crawling and data curation, we introduce HalfLife, a novel analysis for estimating adversarial content inclusion in web-crawl based LM training data. We use HalfLife to explore the feasibility of poisoning pretraining corpora at web scale through open discussion interfaces. Our analysis demonstrates the importance of estimating whether poison injections are included in pretraining data, and establishes third-party webpage content as a possible vector for attacking language model pretraining.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [Generalized Neural Distributional Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14122)
*Natan Hilario da Silva,Vicente Garibay Cancho,Adriano Kamimura Suzuki*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出广义神经分布回归（GNDR）框架，将深度神经网络嵌入经典概率分布参数空间，通过两步半参数估计解决深度架构的非可识别性与最大似然理论的矛盾，实现解析Fisher信息矩阵提取与不确定性量化，并在多种数据模态上验证了其优越的分布校准能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在分布回归中缺乏严格的不确定性量化能力，且深度架构的固有非可识别性与经典最大似然理论之间存在冲突，亟需一个能同时兼顾深度表征学习能力与严格统计学推断的统一框架。

Method: 采用两步半参数估计流程：第一步隔离终端预测头，将上游网络视为固定的非线性基展开；第二步基于此固定表示进行最大似然估计，从而提取解析Fisher信息矩阵，并利用多元Delta方法生成观测特异性置信带和容差区间。

Result: 在过离散临床计数数据、右删失转录组生存数据（混合治愈框架）以及从无结构人脸图像直接推导的零截断年龄分布上，GNDR均展现了更好的分布校准性能和广泛的适用性。

Conclusion: GNDR框架成功将深度神经网络的灵活性与经典概率分布的理论严谨性结合，为深度分布回归提供了可靠的不确定性量化工具，并已开源实现于Python包thetaflow中。

Abstract: We introduce the Generalized Neural Distributional Regression (GNDR) framework, which seamlessly embeds deep neural networks into the parameter space of classical probability distributions. To reconcile the inherent non-identifiability of deep architectures with maximum likelihood theory, we propose a two-step semi-parametric estimation procedure. By isolating the terminal prediction heads and treating the upstream network as a fixed, non-linear basis expansion, GNDR enables the extraction of analytical Fisher Information matrices. This facilitates rigorous uncertainty quantification, generating observation-specific confidence bands and tolerance intervals via the multivariate Delta method. We demonstrate the framework's versatility and superior distributional calibration across diverse data modalities, including overdispersed clinical counts, right-censored transcriptomic survival profiles under a mixture cure framework, and zero-truncated age distributions derived directly from unstructured facial images. The methodology is natively implemented in the open-source Python package \textit{thetaflow}.

</details>


### [164] [Operator-Informed Gaussian Processes for Complex Helmholtz Wavefields: From Synthetic Benchmarks to In Vivo Brain Elastography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14193)
*Boyuan Deng,Kshitiz Upadhyay,Michael Shields*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文将算子知情高斯过程回归扩展至复值亥姆霍兹方程，通过将复算子实化为等效耦合实块，实现了对耗散介质中波场的概率推断。在1-3维基准测试中，该方法以远少于有限差分和神经网络的内点约束达到竞争性精度，并返回后验分布而非点估计。应用于体内脑MR弹性成像时，多尺度先验重建的剪切旋度场与测量值的相关性达到0.77，优于0.75的目标。论文指出低频精度上限源于模型失配，且后验不确定性尚未校准，因此校准不确定性是耗散介质中概率波场推断的核心下一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在耗散介质中，复值亥姆霍兹方程控制时谐波传播，而现有算子知情高斯过程回归主要针对实值场开发，缺乏对复值问题的扩展。同时，从稀疏、含噪数据中推断波场需要能够量化不确定性的求解器。

Method: 将复值亥姆霍兹算子实化为等效的耦合实块（couple real block），从而允许使用标准实值GP条件化进行推断。先验家族包括对角先验、区域化先验和多尺度变体，条件化基于PDE残差和边界迹。

Result: 在1-3维基准测试中，该方法以远少于有限差分和神经网络的内点约束达到竞争性精度。在体内脑MR弹性成像中，多尺度先验重建的剪切旋度场与测量值的相关性达到0.77，超过0.75的目标。

Conclusion: 多尺度核带来的增益而非实-虚耦合是性能提升的关键。存在由模型失配导致的低频精度上限，且后验不确定性尚未校准。校准不确定性是耗散介质中概率波场推断的下一个核心挑战。

Abstract: The Helmholtz equation governs time-harmonic wave propagation, and in dissipative media a complex modulus renders its squared wavenumber $κ^2$ complex. Inferring such fields from sparse, noisy data calls for solvers that also quantify their own uncertainty. Physics-informed Gaussian-process (GP) regression supplies this by returning a posterior over the solution, yet operator-conditioned formulations have been developed almost exclusively for real-valued fields. We extend operator-informed GP regression to complex-valued Helmholtz problems by realifying the complex operator into an equivalent coupled real block, which enables inference with standard real-valued GP conditioning. The construction admits a family of priors, from a proper diagonal prior to coregionalized and multiscale variants, and conditions on PDE residuals and boundary traces. On benchmark problems in one to three dimensions, the solver is competitive with finite-difference and neural-network baselines at a far smaller interior-constraint budget. Unlike those deterministic baselines, it returns a posterior over the complex wavefield rather than a point estimate. Applied to \textit{in vivo} brain magnetic resonance elastography, a proper multiscale prior reconstructs the shear curl field to a correlation of $0.77$ with measurement, above a $0.75$ target. The gain arises from the multiscale kernel rather than from real--imaginary coupling. We further identify a low-frequency accuracy ceiling set by model mismatch and a posterior uncertainty that is not yet calibrated. Calibrated uncertainty therefore emerges as the central next step for probabilistic wavefield inference in dissipative media.

</details>


### [165] [Spectral Concentration and Recovery in Sparse High-Dimensional Random Geometric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14304)
*Manuel Fernandez,Yizhe Zhu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了高维稀疏随机几何图的谱性质与隐空间恢复问题。在连通性尺度下，证明了谱范数界优于先前工作；在合适的维度条件下，实现了隐向量和Gram矩阵的精确恢复；首次证明了高斯混合块模型在最优连通性尺度下的精确标签恢复，且通过半定规划在适度分离区域达到精确恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机几何图的谱范数界不够紧致，隐空间恢复的维度条件不够优化，且高斯混合块模型的精确恢复结果尚属空白。

Method: 采用正交多项式展开、解耦技巧和矩阵集中不等式，避免了传统的迹矩论证方法。

Result: (1) 在连通尺度$np=\Omega(\log n)$下，得到谱范数界$\|A-\mathbb E A\|=O(\sqrt{np\log n}+np\tau)$；(2) 在$np\gg\log n$时，当$d\ll np\log(1/p)/\log n$时隐向量和Gram矩阵误差消失；(3) 在高斯混合块模型中，在最优连通尺度下通过半定规划实现精确标签恢复。

Conclusion: 本文的方法和结果在谱范数紧致性、隐空间恢复维度条件和精确标签恢复方面均优于现有工作，且为随机几何图与同步问题提供了更优的理论保证。

Abstract: We study sparse random geometric graphs generated by connecting pairs of high-dimensional vectors whose inner product exceeds a threshold. The latent vectors are sampled either uniformly from the sphere or from a standard Gaussian distribution. Although every edge appears with probability $p$, the edges are dependent through their shared latent vectors. For the spherical model, at the connectivity scale $np=Ω(\log n)$, we prove $\|A-\mathbb E A\|=O\left(\sqrt{np\log n}+npτ\right)$, with high probability, where $τ$ is the cap threshold. This sharpens the spectral norm bound of Liu, Mohanty, Schramm, and Yang (2023) under weaker assumptions. An analogous result holds for the Gaussian model after removing the fluctuations of the vector norms, yielding improved global synchronization guarantees for the homogeneous Kuramoto model. We then recover the latent geometry from the leading eigenspace. When $np\gg\log n$, both the latent vector and relative Gram matrix errors vanish provided $d\ll np\log(1/p)/\log n$. The required lower dimension is only $d\gg\log(1/p)$ for the spherical model and $d\gg\log^2(1/p)\log n$ for the Gaussian model, improving the recovery guarantees of Li and Schramm (2023). Finally, we prove the first exact recovery result for the Gaussian mixture block model of Li and Schramm (2023). At the optimal connectivity scale $np=Ω(\log n)$, a polynomial-time semidefinite program exactly recovers all labels in a moderate-separation regime, whereas larger separation makes exact recovery impossible because isolated vertices appear with high probability. Our proofs combine orthogonal polynomial expansions, decoupling, and matrix concentration, avoiding the trace-moment arguments used in previous work.

</details>


### [166] [Optimal Self-Distillation for Rectified Flow via Linear Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14947)
*Saptarshi Roy,Debepsita Mukherjee,Pratik Patil*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于整流流（RF）的最优自蒸馏（SD）方法，证明了在岭正则化线性RF中，通过最优混合系数（正或负）可以严格改进次优教师模型的速度场风险，并给出了无需网格搜索的GCV调参方法，实验验证了该方法在速度风险、模态恢复和有限步生成上的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成模型越来越多地使用模型生成的信号进行训练，这既带来了自我改进的机会，也带来了崩溃的风险。本文旨在研究在整流流框架下，是否可以通过自蒸馏（即用次优教师模型的信号训练学生模型）来保证地改进教师模型。

Method: 1) 建立了线性RF+岭正则化下的精确仿射路径恒等式；2) 推导出最优混合系数的闭式解，并证明其满足符号法则（正混合纠正欠正则化教师，负混合纠正过正则化教师）；3) 提出了单次广义交叉验证（GCV）和验证调参程序，避免网格搜索和重复拟合；4) 结合RF的Wasserstein收敛界，证明最优自蒸馏可改进控制连续时间和有限步生成误差的速度估计项。

Result: 在固定插值对上的线性RF+岭正则化设定下，证明了当教师风险沿正则化路径非平稳时，最优自蒸馏能严格改进集成速度风险。最优混合系数有闭式解且遵循符号法则。GCV方法避免了网格搜索。实验表明该方法在高斯模型、高斯混合模型和图像数据上均能改进速度风险、模态恢复和有限步生成质量。

Conclusion: 最优自蒸馏能够在不引入额外计算开销（通过GCV）的前提下，系统地改进次优教师的速度场，且正负混合系数的符号法则为实践提供了明确指导。该理论为生成模型中的自蒸馏提供了严格的理论保证和实用的调参方法。

Abstract: Modern generative models are increasingly trained using model-generated signals, creating both opportunities for self-improvement and risks of collapse. We study optimal self-distillation (SD) for rectified flow (RF): given a suboptimal teacher velocity field, can a student trained on a mixture of true RF velocities and teacher velocities provably improve the teacher? For linear RF with ridge regularization on fixed interpolation pairs, we prove an exact affine path identity, derive the optimal mixing coefficient in closed form, and show strict improvement in integrated velocity risk whenever the teacher risk is nonstationary along the regularization path. The optimal coefficient obeys a sign rule: positive mixing corrects under-regularized teachers, while negative mixing corrects over-regularized teachers. We also give one-shot generalized cross-validation (GCV) and validation tuning procedure that avoids grid search over mixing weights and repeated refitting. Combining this theorem with RF Wasserstein convergence bounds, we show that optimal self-distillation improves the velocity estimation terms controlling continuous-time and finite-step generation error. Experiments with Gaussian models, Gaussian mixtures, and image data show that optimal self-distillation improves velocity risk, mode recovery, and finite-step generation relative to both the teacher and pure distillation.

</details>


### [167] [cGAP: Generalized Association Plots with HOMALS-Guided Heatmaps for Visualization of High-Dimensional Categorical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15018)
*Chun-houh Chen,Shun-Chuan Chang,Chiun-How Kao,Yi-Ju Lee,Shang-Ying Shiu,Yin-Jing Tien,ShengLi Tzeng,Han-Ming Wu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: cGAP 是一种面向高维分类数据的可视化框架，通过 HOMALS 将类别数据嵌入三维 RGB 颜色空间，结合热力图与序列化重排，在保持原始数据矩阵可追溯性的同时揭示聚类、异常值与全局结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维分类数据（如遗传学、生物医学、社会科学）缺乏高效且可解释的可视化工具：现有方法要么扩展性差，要么过度依赖低维展示而与原始数据矩阵脱节，要么优先预测准确性而牺牲可解释性。

Method: 使用同质性分析（HOMALS）将样本和类别水平嵌入三维欧几里得空间，将嵌入映射为 RGB 颜色坐标；集成三个协同视图：HOMALS 引导的原始数据矩阵热力图、样本邻近矩阵、变量邻近矩阵；通过序列化算法重排行/列以揭示聚类和结构。

Result: 在四个数据集（学生-动物分类数据、哺乳动物齿列数据、UCI 蘑菇数据、COG 数据库）上验证，展示了 cGAP 支持透明的探索性分析，保持导出视觉结构与原始分类观测之间的可追溯性。

Conclusion: cGAP 提供了一种基于全矩阵热力图的可视化环境，适用于跨科学领域的复杂分类数据集探索，兼具可扩展性、可解释性与结构保真性。

Abstract: High-dimensional categorical data arise in genetics, biomedicine, and the social sciences, yet visualization tools for such data remain far less developed than those for continuous variables. Existing methods either scale poorly, rely heavily on low-dimensional displays detached from the original data matrix, or prioritize predictive accuracy over interpretability. To address this gap, we introduce categorical Generalized Association Plots (cGAP), a visualization framework for nominal, ordinal, and binary data that preserves the original data matrix while augmenting it with interpretable geometric structure. cGAP uses Homogeneity Analysis (HOMALS) to embed subjects and category levels in a three-dimensional Euclidean space and maps the embedding to red-green-blue coordinates so that similar patterns receive similar colors. The framework integrates three coordinated views: a HOMALS-guided heatmap of the raw data matrix, a subject proximity matrix, and a variable proximity matrix. Seriation algorithms are then used to reorder rows and columns to reveal coherent clusters, outliers, and local-to-global structure. We also derive barycentric traceability, projection-distortion, and contrast-preservation properties that clarify how embedding geometry is transferred to the display. We demonstrate the versatility of cGAP through applications to student-animal classification data, mammalian dentition profiles, mushroom records from the UCI Machine Learning Repository, and the Clusters of Orthologous Genes database. These examples show that cGAP supports transparent exploratory analysis by maintaining traceability between derived visual structure and the original categorical observations. cGAP provides a full-matrix, heatmap-based visualization environment for investigating complex categorical datasets across scientific domains.

</details>


### [168] [Subjective Risk Decomposition: A New View for Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15196)
*Raghad Alamri,Michele Caprio,Gavin Brown*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的不确定性量化视角：不确定性度量并非原始概念，而是高层建模决策的推论。通过对基于严格合理损失的主观风险进行分解，可以导出认知不确定性和偶然不确定性度量。逆向交叉熵是一个突出示例，其分解恢复了经典信息论不确定性项。该方法统一了UQ文献中众多已有度量，并为学习理论中的主观风险类比提供了分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性量化研究多将不确定性度量视为需要公理和论证的基本概念，缺乏统一的理论基础。作者旨在重新审视不确定性度量的本质，将其视为高层建模决策的推论，从而为不同UQ方法提供统一的理论框架。

Method: 通过对基于严格合理损失的主观风险进行分解来导出认知和偶然不确定性度量；以逆向交叉熵为例展示该分解如何恢复经典信息论不确定性项；将这一视角扩展到学习理论，引入并分析主观风险下的过量风险、逼近误差和估计误差。

Result: 该方法能够恢复UQ文献中先前提出的众多度量，为其提供共同的理论基础。在学习理论方面，建立了主观风险类比与UQ之间的联系。

Conclusion: 不确定性度量是高层建模决策的推论而非原始概念，基于严格合理损失的风险分解为统一UQ理论提供了新方向。该工作是构建完整UQ学习理论框架的第一步。

Abstract: We present a novel viewpoint for uncertainty quantification. Uncertainty measures are not primitives, in need of axioms and argumentation, but instead consequences, of higher-level modelling decisions. We show how epistemic and aleatoric uncertainty measures can be derived via decomposition of a subjective risk, based on a strictly proper loss. Reverse cross-entropy provides a prominent example, where decomposition recovers the classic information-theoretic uncertainty terms. The same approach recovers numerous measures previously proposed across the UQ literature, providing them a common theoretical foundation. From a practical point of view, this suggests a new approach to UQ: given a modelling scenario and strictly proper loss, the corresponding epistemic and aleatoric terms are induced by the subjective-risk decomposition. We then extend our view to learning theory: we introduce and analyse subjective risk analogues of excess risk, approximation error, and estimation error, and identify the connections to UQ. We consider this a first step towards a full learning-theoretic framework for uncertainty quantification.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Using a MIP Solver as a PDHG-Based MIP Heuristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14483)
*Edward Rothberg*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文探索将PDHG算法作为MIP求解器中默认LP求解器的替代方案，以加速大多数现有启发式算法。尽管存在一些明显缺陷（无法使用某些强大的MIP技术），但大多数启发式算法不受影响，最终能在更短时间内找到高质量、高精度的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDHG算法能快速求解低精度LP问题，这有望加速MIP启发式算法寻找可行解的过程。作者希望探究是否可以用PDHG替代现代MIP求解器中的默认LP求解器，从而加速其大部分现有启发式算法。

Method: 将PDHG算法直接替换现代MIP求解器中的默认LP求解器，对比分析这种替换对各类MIP启发式算法的影响，评估其整体性能。

Result: 替换存在明显缺陷，使得一些强大的MIP技术无法使用，但大多数启发式算法不受影响。最终该方法能够比当前最先进策略更快地找到高质量、高精度的解。

Conclusion: 以PDHG替代默认LP求解器加速MIP启发式算法是可行的，尽管牺牲了部分MIP技术，但在整体性能上仍具有竞争力，能够更快获得高质量解。

Abstract: The PDHG algorithm provides a new capability for solving difficult linear programming (LP) problems: the ability to find low-accuracy solutions quickly. While such solutions may not be applicable in all applications of LP, one possible use is to accelerate heuristics for finding feasible solutions to Mixed-Integer Programming (MIP) problems, where these approximate LP solutions can hopefully guide a heuristic towards accurate and high-quality MIP solutions. While a monolithic heuristic that exploits PDHG solutions would be useful, we pose a broader question here: could we replace the default LP solver in a modern MIP solver with PDHG to accelerate most (or all) of its existing heuristics? We find that doing so does have some obvious drawbacks, preventing us from using several powerful MIP techniques, but it leaves most others unaffected, ultimately resulting in a heuristic that often finds high-quality, high-accuracy solutions faster than current state-of-the-art strategies.

</details>


### [170] [State-Dependent Metric Projection Neural Network for Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14519)
*Mohammed Alshahrani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种状态依赖度量投影动力学系统（SD-SPNN），通过将连续时间预处理嵌入到投影算子中，解决了传统固定度量投影神经网络在处理各向异性问题时收敛缓慢的问题。在标准单调性假设下，建立了解的存在性、均衡解与变分不等式的一致性以及Lyapunov稳定性，数值实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投影神经网络和投影动力学系统基于固定的欧几里得度量或常数度量，当算子或可行集几何具有高度各向异性时，会导致病态条件和收敛缓慢的问题。

Method: 提出状态依赖度量投影动力学系统（SD-SPNN），其中投影算子的度量随状态平滑演化，将连续时间预处理直接嵌入到动态流中。在算子的标准单调性假设和度量的温和正则性条件下，建立理论性质。

Result: 建立了解的存在性、均衡解与变分不等式之间的精确对应关系以及Lyapunov稳定性性质。统一了欧几里得、常数度量和状态依赖投影神经网络在一个连续时间模型框架内。数值实验表明状态依赖度量能重塑投影动力学的瞬态几何结构，同时收敛到相同的均衡点。

Conclusion: 状态依赖度量投影动力学系统为求解变分不等式提供了一种连续时间预处理方法，有效改善了各向异性问题下的收敛性能，并将多种投影神经网络统一在一个框架下。

Abstract: Projection-based dynamical systems and projection neural networks offer a continuous-time approach to solving variational inequalities by driving the state toward its projection onto the feasible set. However, most existing models are built on a fixed Euclidean or constant metric, which can lead to poor conditioning and slow convergence when the operator or feasible set geometry is highly anisotropic. This paper introduces a state-dependent metric projection dynamical system, referred to as a state-dependent scaled projection neural network (SD-SPNN), in which the geometry of the projection operator evolves smoothly with the state. The dynamics generalize classical projected dynamical systems and projection neural networks by embedding continuous-time preconditioning directly into the flow. Under standard monotonicity assumptions on the operator and mild regularity conditions on the metric, we establish existence of solutions, an exact equilibrium-solution correspondence with the underlying variational inequality, and Lyapunov-based stability properties. The framework unifies Euclidean, constant-metric, and state-dependent projection neural network within a single continuous-time model. Numerical experiments illustrate how state-dependent metrics reshape the transient geometry of the projected dynamics while converging to the same equilibrium.

</details>


### [171] [A Slow-Fast Stochastic Framework for Zeroth-Order Distributed Time-Varying Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14734)
*Wanying Li,Nan-jing Huang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对仅利用零阶信息的随机多智能体系统，提出了一种新颖的随机奇异摄动框架，通过引入辅助快系统生成平滑梯度估计，使慢系统实现分布式优化与一致性，并证明了概率意义下的实际固定时间一致性与对时变最优轨迹的渐近有界跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将梯度估计与优化更新直接耦合在单时间尺度上，本文旨在解耦二者，构建慢-快耦合结构以提升分布式时变优化性能。

Method: 构建随机奇异摄动框架：引入辅助变量和快子系统生成平滑梯度估计（快），智能体状态演化作为慢子系统执行分布式优化与一致性；采用随机奇异摄动技术和随机Lyapunov理论分析收敛性。

Result: 快子系统快速收敛到瞬时随机梯度估计；慢子系统实现概率意义下的实际固定时间一致性，并渐近有界跟踪时变最优轨迹；建立了参数、随机扰动和目标函数性质对跟踪性能的显式界。

Conclusion: 所提慢-快耦合方案有效解耦梯度估计与优化更新，在零阶信息下实现了随机多智能体系统的分布式时变优化，理论结果通过数值仿真验证。

Abstract: This paper investigates the distributed time-varying optimization of stochastic multi-agent systems (SMASs) using only zero-order information. Unlike existing methods that directly couple gradient estimation and optimization updates on a single time scale, this paper constructs a novel stochastic singular perturbation framework by introducing auxiliary fast systems. The proposed scheme naturally forms a slow-fast coupling structure: by introducing auxiliary variables and constructing fast subsystems to generate smooth gradient estimates, while the agent's state evolution, as the slow subsystem, performs distributed optimization and consensus. The convergence of the proposed scheme is analyzed using stochastic singular perturbation techniques and stochastic Lyapunov theory. The results show that the fast subsystem converges rapidly to the instantaneous stochastic gradient estimates, while the slow subsystem achieves practically fixed-time consensus (Pfxc) in probability and asymptotically bounded tracks the time-varying optimal trajectory. Furthermore, this paper establishes explicit bounds to characterize the effects of parameters, stochastic disturbances, and the properties of the objective function on tracking performance. Finally, the theoretical results are validated through numerical simulations.

</details>


### [172] [Tamed Stochastic Gradient Hamiltonian Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14862)
*Zhuoran Wang,Ying Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于超线性增长随机梯度的驯化随机梯度哈密顿蒙特卡洛（tSGHMC）算法，在强凸性和平均连续性条件下建立了Wasserstein-2距离下的非渐近误差界（收敛速率1/4），推导了期望超额风险上界，并通过报童问题和CVaR最小化问题的数值实验验证了算法有效性，且相比一阶驯化未调整随机朗之万算法具有更低的均方根误差和期望超额风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机梯度哈密顿蒙特卡洛（SGHMC）方法在处理超线性增长的随机梯度时存在数值不稳定性，缺乏严格的理论保证。本文旨在克服这一局限，提出一种驯化版本的SGHMC算法并建立其收敛性与泛化性能的理论保障。

Method: 提出tSGHMC算法，通过在哈密顿动力学中引入驯化项来处理超线性增长的随机梯度；在强凸性和平均连续性条件下，利用Wasserstein-2距离分析非渐近误差界；推导期望超额风险上界以提供泛化性能的理论保证。

Result: 建立了tSGHMC在Wasserstein-2距离下收敛速率为1/4的非渐近误差界；推导了期望超额风险的上界估计；在报童问题和CVaR最小化问题上的合成与真实数据集实验中，tSGHMC相比一阶tamed ULA算法取得了更低的均方根误差和期望超额风险。

Conclusion: tSGHMC算法能有效应对超线性增长随机梯度带来的挑战，理论上保证了收敛性和泛化性能，数值实验验证了其优越性，尤其在二阶动力学优势下优于一阶驯化朗之万算法。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a novel tamed stochastic gradient Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (tSGHMC) algorithm for sampling and stochastic optimization problems with superlinearly growing stochastic gradients. Under a certain continuity in average condition and a strong convexity condition, we establish a non-asymptotic error bound in Wasserstein-2 distance for tSGHMC with the rate of convergence equal to $1/4$. Then, we derive an upper estimate for the associated expected excess risk, which provides a theoretical guarantee for the performance of tSGHMC. To illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm, we apply tSGHMC to practical examples, including a newsvendor problem and a Conditional Value-at-Risk minimization problem, using synthetic and real-world datasets. Numerical results support our theoretical findings. Furthermore, we compare tSGHMC with its first-order counterpart, namely, the tamed unadjusted stochastic Langevin algorithm. Simulation results demonstrate that tSGHMC achieves lower root mean square error and expected excess risk across a range of tasks.

</details>


### [173] [Finite-Dimensional Feedback Stabilization of Nonautonomous Stochastic Parabolic Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14911)
*Behzad Azmi,Jonas von der Heydt,Sergio Rodrigues*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类由Q-维纳过程驱动的非线性非自治随机抛物型方程的有限维反馈镇定问题，涵盖加性和乘性扰动。通过使用支撑集可任意小、局部化指示器型执行器的有限线性组合构造反馈律，并在Gelfand三重框架下证明了闭环系统的适定性。在适当选择执行器配置和反馈强度下，建立了指数均方镇定和几乎必然镇定结果，并提供了全离散三层数值实现与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非线性非自治随机抛物型方程，现有镇定方法多依赖无限维或全局性控制，而实际工程中控制器的空间分布和维数受限。本文旨在探索如何通过有限维、局部化且支撑集可任意小的反馈控制实现随机抛物型方程的镇定，弥补该领域在有限维反馈构造和理论分析上的空白。

Method: 1) 控制律由有限个局部化指示器型执行器的线性组合构成，执行器支撑集可任意小；2) 反馈律通过向合适有限维子空间的斜投影构造；3) 在变分Gelfand三重框架下，基于标准强制性、增长性和全局Lipschitz假设分析闭环系统适定性；4) 通过调节执行器配置与反馈强度实现指数均方镇定（带乘性噪声时还获得几乎必然镇定）；5) 提出全离散三层数值实现方案。

Result: 1) 证明了闭环系统在标准假设下的适定性；2) 建立了指数均方镇定结果，对于纯乘性噪声情形还获得了几乎必然镇定；3) 数值实验展示了执行器数量、噪声强度和非线性效应对闭环镇定行为的影响规律。

Conclusion: 本文发展了一套完整的有限维反馈镇定框架，表明即使执行器支撑集极小且维数有限，仍能实现对非线性非自治随机抛物型方程的有效镇定。理论分析和数值实验共同验证了该方法的可行性与鲁棒性，为随机分布参数系统的实际控制提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate finite-dimensional feedback stabilization for nonlinear nonautonomous stochastic parabolic equations driven by $Q$-Wiener, covering both additive and multiplicative perturbations. The control is given by a finite linear combination of localized indicator-type actuators whose supports are selected as part of the construction and may have arbitrarily small total measure. The feedback law is constructed by means of oblique projections onto suitable finite-dimensional subspaces. Within the variational Gelfand triple framework, we prove well-posedness of the closed-loop system under standard coercivity, growth, and global Lipschitz assumptions. By appropriately choosing the actuator configuration and feedback strength, we establish exponential mean-square stabilization of the stochastic dynamics and, for pure multiplicative noise, almost-sure stabilization. A fully discrete three-layer implementation complements the theoretical results. Numerical experiments illustrate the influence of number of actuators, noise intensity, and nonlinear effects on the closed-loop stabilization behavior.

</details>


### [174] [Statistical Inference for Scenario-Based Dynamic Optimization under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14965)
*Aurya Javeed,Johannes Milz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对批次和半批次过程操作中的有限时域开环动态优化问题，研究了基于参数不确定性的样本平均近似方法，建立了样本最优值估计总体最优值的统计理论，并提出了两种置信区间构建方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在批次和半批次过程操作中，参数不确定性给动态优化带来挑战。常用的样本平均近似方法缺乏严格的统计理论基础，需要建立样本最优值作为总体最优值估计量的统计性质。

Method: 基于终端损失关于时间积分控制量的Lipschitz连续稳定性估计，推导了样本目标函数的泛函中心极限定理及相应的最优值误差统计极限定理。当总体最优解唯一时采用高斯插补置信区间；当存在多个最优策略时采用子抽样置信区间。

Result: 建立了样本最优值估计总体最优值的统计极限理论，得到了最优值误差的渐近分布，并提供了两种置信区间构建方法（高斯插补和子抽样）。

Conclusion: 通过两个补料分批案例研究验证了所提方法的有效性，表明在参数不确定性下优化进料速率剖面时，所发展的统计理论能够可靠地量化估计不确定性。

Abstract: Motivated by batch and semi-batch process operation, we study finite-horizon open-loop dynamic optimization problems with uncertain parameters. A common computational approach replaces the expected performance criterion by an average over finitely many sampled parameter realizations. We develop a statistical theory for the resulting sample-based optimal value as an estimator of the population optimal value. The analysis is based on a stability estimate showing that terminal losses depend Lipschitz continuously on the time-integrated control, which records the cumulative input delivered up to each time. This estimate yields a functional central limit theorem for the sample-based objective and a statistical limit theorem for the corresponding optimal value error. As a consequence, we obtain confidence intervals for the population optimal value. When the population optimizer is unique, the limit is Gaussian and leads to a plug-in confidence interval. When multiple optimal policies may exist, we use a subsampling confidence interval that does not require uniqueness. The methodology is illustrated on two fed-batch case studies in which feed-rate profiles are optimized under parametric uncertainty.

</details>
